### [1] [Worldsheet string theory on $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \times \mathbf{S}^3 \times \mathrm{K3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20696)
*Lorenz Eberhardt,Matthias R. Gaberdiel,Sameer Murthy,Mukund Rangamani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在纯NS-NS通量下AdS₃×S³×K3背景中RNS形式的弦论，指出标准RNS分区函数构造会破坏时空超对称性，并提出通过强制要求时空超对称性来唯一确定物理态投影的新方法；该方法导致非标准模性质的分区函数，并成功复现超引力谱、计算出含Mock模特征的椭圆亏格，并构造了一个无快子的非超对称理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RNS形式下构造AdS₃×S³×K3（纯NS-NS通量）的worldsheet分区函数会导致与时空超对称性矛盾的结果，需厘清原因并给出自洽方案。

Method: 通过强制要求时空超对称性来唯一确定物理态投影规则，放弃传统按自旋结构求和的模不变性构造；进而计算修正后的分区函数、BRST上同调谱、椭圆亏格，并分析其模性质与CFT对偶含义。

Result: 得到一个不分解为玻色/费米部分、且不含模协变性的新分区函数；成功复现Type IIA/IIB超引力谱；椭圆亏格包含全纯短弦（具对称 orbifold 结构）与非全纯长弦（类mock modular但有差异）两部分；并构造出一个无快子的非超对称理论。

Conclusion: 时空超对称性比worldsheet模不变性更基本，应作为分区函数构造的首要约束；该框架可推广至其他AdS₃背景，并为理解非全纯CFT及mock模对象提供新视角。

Abstract: The RNS formalism describing string theory on $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \times \mathbf{S}^3 \times \mathrm{K3}$ supported by pure NS-NS flux is in principle straightforward to write down. One can directly check that the worldsheet theory reproduces the correct chiral spectrum expected from supergravity by analyzing the BRST cohomology. However, when one attempts to construct the worldsheet partition function using the standard rules of the RNS formalism by appropriately summing over spin structures to ensure worldsheet modular invariance, one finds a result in tension with spacetime supersymmetry. We explain why this is the case and, more importantly, propose a resolution. Specifically, we fix the physical state projection for the partition function uniquely by requiring spacetime supersymmetry. Perhaps surprisingly, the projected worldsheet partition function does not factorize into a bosonic and a fermionic part and involves functions that are not modular covariant. As a consistency check, we show that our prescription for the worldsheet partition function reproduces the supergravity spectrum for both Type IIB and IIA theories. Furthermore, we compute the elliptic genus of the dual CFT from the worldsheet, which has both a holomorphic short string and a non-holomorphic long string contribution. The latter displays characteristics of a mock modular function, albeit with some key differences, while the former has the tantalizing structure of a symmetric orbifold. We also point out the worldsheet construction of a non-supersymmetric theory, whose spectrum is free of tachyons.

</details>


### [2] [An SYK wormhole under the microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20699)
*Valérie Bettaque,Martin Sasieta,Brian Swingle,Alejandro Vilar López*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了SYK模型中重算符热单点函数方差的大N虫洞鞍点，提出其对应双喇叭几何；在双标度极限和Schwarzian极限下，分别将其表示为弦连接边界或JT引力中的量子跃迁振幅，并通过与含物质JT引力的对比检验SYK中重算符随机性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验SYK模型中重算符随机性假设，并建立微观耦合与宏观虫洞几何之间的对应关系。

Method: 基于大N展开和双标度极限分析SYK模型中热单点函数方差的虫洞鞍点；引入弦描述并构造半虫洞的体波函数；类比JT引力构建开放与闭合宇宙的体希尔伯特空间。

Result: 发现虫洞鞍点对应双喇叭几何；弦结构在Schwarzian极限下涌现为JT引力中的量子跃迁振幅；半虫洞波函数在长度基下呈现非自平均的涨落关联；弦结构类似局域观测者的世界线。

Conclusion: SYK模型中的虫洞可统一描述为弦构成的几何结构，其参数由微观耦合决定；该框架支持重算符随机性假设，并为全息对偶中观测者角色提供了新视角。

Abstract: We derive a large-N wormhole saddle in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model for the variance of the thermal one-point function of a heavy operator across realizations of the microscopic couplings. We propose that this saddle describes a double trumpet geometry. In the double-scaling limit, we express the wormhole as a transition amplitude in an emergent bulk Hilbert space of chords connecting the two boundaries. In the Schwarzian limit, the chords proliferate and the wormhole defines a quantum transition amplitude in the open-universe Hilbert space of Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity. Comparing the SYK wormhole amplitude with its counterpart in JT gravity coupled to matter provides a direct test of the heavy-operator randomness hypothesis in SYK.
  The microscopic SYK couplings specify the α-parameters of the wormhole. For a fixed realization of the couplings, we compute the observable in the corresponding α-state. We provide the bulk wavefunction of the associated half-wormhole, which exhibits erratic correlations in the length basis that do not self-average. We extend the bulk description to the Hilbert space of a closed universe. We point out that the correlation chords from which the wormhole geometry emerges structurally resemble local observer worldlines.

</details>


### [3] [Region bounds, error margins and certified positivity in the numerical conformal bootstrap; Or the first mathematically rigorous bounds on the 3d Ising CFT critical exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20701)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格、可验证的数值共形自举方法，填补了现有方法在参数空间覆盖、共形块截断、自旋范围和浮点精度四方面的理论缺口，并首次对三维Ising模型临界指数给出了严格双向边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值共形自举界限虽被称为‘严格’，实则依赖采样点、截断近似、有限自旋列表及浮点运算，缺乏数学严谨性与可验证性。

Method: 通过区域正性泛函构造、大自旋向量的区域约束、共形块递推关系的解析界、基于Bernstein基与ℚ(√2)精确算术的正性认证、无穷自旋尾部紧致化处理，以及截断误差显式上界，将无限多正性条件归约为有限个机器可检符号条件。

Result: 首次在(Δ_σ, Δ_ε)平面上严格排除一大片区域，仅保留包含3d Ising岛的小矩形；给出临界指数η和ν的严格双向界限；证明了混合σ-ε系统的解析输入，可消除自旋-2能隙假设；并提升实际计算鲁棒性与效率。

Conclusion: 该工作将共形自举从数值启发式提升为可验证的数学工具，为高精度临界现象研究提供了首个完全严格且开源可复现的框架。

Abstract: Numerical conformal bootstrap bounds are commonly called rigorous, yet as published they are not theorems: they hold at sampled points rather than on regions of parameter space, for truncated rational approximations of the conformal blocks, for a finite list of spins, and up to floating-point rounding. We close all four gaps. We show how to search efficiently for functionals positive on regions, including external-dimension space; how positivity on the large-spin crossing vectors can be imposed as such a region constraint; how new analytic bounds on the conformal-block recursion relations give functionals positive on the true, untruncated crossing vectors; and how to certify the positivity of these functionals in exact arithmetic. The certification rests on Bernstein-basis positivity certificates in up to three variables, exact arithmetic in $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$, a compactified treatment of the infinite spin tail, and an explicit bound on the difference between the true blocks and their truncations, so that infinitely many positivity conditions reduce to finitely many machine-checked sign conditions. As a flagship application we certify an exclusion region in the $(Δ_σ,Δ_ε)$ plane: for $Δ_σ\in[0.505,0.5405]$ all of $Δ_ε\in(1/2,3]$ is excluded except a small rectangle containing the 3d Ising island, giving rigorous two-sided bounds on the critical exponents $η$ and $ν$ under explicitly stated spectrum assumptions. The analytic inputs for the mixed $σ$-$ε$ system, which would remove the spin-2 gap assumption, are also proved. The same methods prevent common practical failure modes and can make bootstrap searches faster: region bounds turn the mapping of an island into a handful of one-dimensional bisections. The essential code to reproduce the search and the certification, and all machine-checkable certificates, are released.

</details>


### [4] [Finite-cutoff geometry from double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20703)
*Sergio E. Aguilar-Gutierrez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了双标度SYK（DSSYK）模型的有限截断全息描述，通过微观变形实现类T T¯ 及其推广变形，对应有限截断正弦稀释引力与AdS/dS几何，并探讨了ETW膜、CMC切片及复杂度等全息对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在全息对偶中建立有限截断边界下DSSYK模型的微观描述，连接量子多体系统与低维引力理论，特别是含截断的AdS/dS几何、虫洞态及复杂度等概念。

Method: 构造Kourkoulou-Maldacena和热场双重SYK态的微观变形，取双标度极限得到类T²和T²+Λ₁变形；分析有限截断正弦稀释引力中的虫洞态与AdS/dS几何；求解能量谱流方程（含微扰与非微扰解）；引入端界（ETW）膜并研究其对应的边界变形；推广常曲率（CMC）切片的有限截断长度计算并诠释为边界复杂度。

Result: 建立了DSSYK的有限截断全息框架：重现类T T¯ 变形、描述有限截断下的AdS（低能）与dS（高能）几何、识别仅微扰分支支持物理量子态、揭示ETW膜导致黑洞或可穿越虫洞取决于是否存在可归一化束缚态、将CMC切片截断长度解释为边界复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为DSSYK模型提供了首个微观自洽的有限截断全息字典，统一处理截断边界、CMC切片、ETW膜及可能的物质耦合，深化了对全息复杂度、虫洞物理与低维量子引力的理解。

Abstract: We develop a finite-cutoff holographic description of the double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model. We construct microscopic deformations of Kourkoulou-Maldacena and thermofield-double SYK states whose double-scaling limits reproduce a lower-dimensional analogue of $\text{T}\overline{\text{T}}$ and $\text{T}\overline{\text{T}}+Λ_2$ deformations, which we denote $T^2$ and $T^2+Λ_1$, respectively. The ensemble-averaged theory describes wormhole states in finite-cutoff sine dilaton gravity, and finite-cutoff anti-de Sitter and de Sitter geometries in the low- and high-energy regimes, respectively. The analysis includes both perturbative and non-perturbative solutions to the energy spectrum flow equation, and the regions behind and beyond the respective horizons. Only the perturbative branch admits physical quantum states. We study how to incorporate end-of-the-world (ETW) branes in the finite cutoff holographic dictionary. The ETW brane leads to an additional boundary theory deformation, which leads to black hole or eternal traversable wormhole geometries depending on the existence of normalizable bound states. We extend the finite cutoff length evaluations for constant mean curvature (CMC) slices, and interpret them as complexity in the boundary theory. We discuss possible extensions with matter. Our results provide a microscopic framework for studying the finite-cutoff boundaries, CMC slices and branes in the holographic dictionary of DSSYK.

</details>


### [5] [Gluon Scattering in Expanding de Sitter Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20707)
*Audrey Lindsay,Tomasz R. Taylor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维德西特空间膨胀庞加莱块中的观测者中心S矩阵，其渐近态沿共动（永恒）观测者的轨迹定义，并计算了库仑规范下纯杨-米尔斯理论的三胶子和四胶子树图振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在德西特时空中缺乏全局时间平移对称性，难以定义标准S矩阵；需为宇宙学背景下的散射过程建立一个与观测者相关的、物理上自洽的S矩阵框架。

Method: 基于共动（永恒）观测者的固有时定义自由哈密顿量，在其世界线上构造渐近态，建立观测者中心的S矩阵；在库仑规范下对纯杨-米尔斯理论进行具体计算。

Result: 所得S矩阵与所选永恒观测者无关；三胶子和四胶子树图振幅具有类似宇宙学电流关联函数的结构，并受德西特等距变换的Ward恒等式约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为德西特时空中的散射理论提供了可观测、协变且对称性保持的构造方案，弥合了高能散射与宇宙学关联函数之间的概念鸿沟。

Abstract: We construct an observer-centric S-matrix in the expanding Poincaré patch of four-dimensional de Sitter space. The asymptotic states are defined along the worldline of a comoving, or eternal, observer, the only kind of geodesic observer that remains in the patch for all proper time, and the free Hamiltonian is the conserved charge generating translations in her proper time. The resulting S-matrix is independent of which eternal observer is chosen. We apply the construction to pure Yang--Mills theory in Coulomb gauge and compute the three- and four-gluon amplitudes at tree level. The amplitudes have a structure similar to the cosmological correlators of gauge currents and are constrained by the Ward identities of de Sitter isometries.

</details>

*Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了6维N=(1,0)超共形场论（SCFT）中E弦理论的扩展，通过在椭圆纤维化基曲线上的终端奇点嵌套额外物质扇区，并通过圆周紧化关联到5维E_n理论的类似扩展，最终解释了一个新近构造的5维理论的6维起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对6维N=(1,0) SCFT的分类虽成功，但存在可拓展空间；作者旨在探索E弦理论中引入额外物质扇区的可能性，并建立其与5维理论的联系。

Method: 利用F理论中椭圆纤维化三重体的分类框架，在基流形曲线上引入终端奇点以嵌套额外物质扇区；再通过圆周紧化（即6维到5维的维度约化）分析对应5维理论及其RG流。

Result: 发现了E弦6维SCFT可被额外物质扇区‘装饰’的新结构；相应地得到一类被装饰的5维E_n理论；其RG流终点复现并解释了一个近期提出的5维理论的6维起源。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了现有6维SCFT分类中未被充分探索的结构自由度，为构造潜在新SCFT提供了路径，但暂未断言这些装饰理论是否构成真正全新的SCFT。

Abstract: The classification of 6d $\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$ SCFTs has been carried out with great success, enumerating consistent elliptically fibered threefolds in F-theory. In this note, we observe that the E-string 6d SCFT can be decorated with extra matter sectors. This decoration exploits an opening allowed by the current classification, implementing terminal singularities nested on the curve of the base of the elliptic fibration that supplies the tensor branch. Additionally, via dimensional reduction on a circle, we identify versions of the 5d $E_n$ theories decorated by extra matter sectors. As a bonus, the end of the resulting RG flow cascade reproduces a 5d theory recently constructed in the literature, explaining its 6d origin. We remain agnostic as to whether the decorated 6d and 5d theories constitute novel SCFTs.

</details>

*Zohreh Ashkipoor Gofreh,Reza Moazzemi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在CPT偶极以太类洛伦兹破缺背景下，自由大质量实标量场在矩形紧致化时空上的重整化Casimir能量，重点分析了背景矢量方向与紧致格点取向之间的几何响应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解洛伦兹破缺背景下真空能量如何依赖于紧致化格点与破缺背景的相对取向，拓展Casimir效应在新物理模型中的应用。

Method: 通过积分非紧致动量、利用Schur补和Poisson重求和，导出指数收敛的Bessel函数格点和表达式；分离出取向无关的动能归一化因子与取向相关的像格点度规变形。

Result: 得到重整化Casimir能量的闭合解析表达式，揭示其对背景矢量取向的依赖性；获得精确无质量极限、紧致方向压强，并针对二维紧致子空间推导出广义Casimir力矩。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破缺背景与各向异性紧致格点的相对取向显著影响重整化真空能量及其方向响应，该结果为探测洛伦兹破缺提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We derive the renormalized Casimir energy of a free massive real scalar field on the rectangular compactification \(\mathbb{R}^{1,r}\times T^p\) in the presence of a constant CPT-even aether-like Lorentz-violating background. Allowing the spatial part of the background vector to have an arbitrary orientation relative to the compactification lattice, we characterize the resulting orientation-dependent geometric response of the vacuum. Integrating out the noncompact momenta reduces the effective spatial kinetic matrix to its Schur complement, and Poisson resummation yields a closed, exponentially convergent Bessel-function lattice sum within the stability domain. The resulting expression separates an orientation-independent kinetic normalization, \([1+λu_μu^μ]^{-1/2}\), from an orientation-dependent deformation of the image-lattice metric. Coordinate-aligned configurations are recovered as special cases, and the exact massless limit and the compact-direction pressures are obtained. For a two-dimensional compact subspace, we explicitly analyze the angular dependence of the vacuum energy and derive the associated generalized Casimir torque. These results establish how the relative orientation between a Lorentz-violating background and an anisotropic compactification lattice enters the renormalized vacuum energy and its directional response.

</details>


### [8] [The Magic Barrier: A Universal Feature of Quantum Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20768)
*Jakob J. Minar,Andreas Schäfer,Leonhard Schmotzer,Clemens Seidl*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子遍历理论中热化过程中的“魔法壁垒”现象，即在快速纠缠增长期间非稳定性的瞬态最大值，并发现这一现象与随机矩阵谱特性密切相关。作者通过分析高斯系综随机矩阵哈密顿量下的幺正演化，解析地确定了纠缠熵、反平坦度、纠缠容量及非局域迹距离魔法的时间演化，发现这些量的瞬态极大值发生在纠缠增长速率最大的同一特征时间尺度上，表明“魔法壁垒”是随机矩阵热化的普适特征，暗示其在量子混沌系统中的普适性；最后指出对遍历量子理论热化的定量模拟本质上需要量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示量子遍历理论中热化过程中‘魔法壁垒’现象的普适性及其与随机矩阵理论（RMT）谱特性的内在联系，为理解量子混沌与热化机制提供新视角。

Method: 基于所有三个高斯系综（GOE、GUE、GSE）抽取的随机矩阵哈密顿量，对纯积态初态下的幺正演化进行解析分析，考察纠缠熵、反平坦度、纠缠容量和非局域迹距离魔法的时间演化行为。

Result: 发现反平坦度、纠缠容量和非局域魔法的瞬态极大值与纠缠增长速率的最大值出现在同一特征时间尺度上，证实‘魔法壁垒’是随机矩阵热化的普适特征。

Conclusion: ‘魔法壁垒’是量子遍历理论中热化的普适现象，其存在意味着对热化过程的定量模拟本质上依赖于量子计算能力。

Abstract: We show that the emergence of a ``magic barrier'', characterized by a transient maximum of nonstabilizerness during rapid entanglement growth, is a universal feature of thermalization within quantum ergodic theories, which is intimately connected to random matrix spectral properties. Starting from a pure product state, we analytically determine the time evolution of entanglement entropy, antiflatness, capacity of entanglement, and nonlocal trace distance magic under unitary dynamics generated by random matrix Hamiltonians drawn from all three Gaussian ensembles. We find that the transient maxima of antiflatness, capacity of entanglement, and nonlocal magic occur on the same characteristic timescale as the maximum rate of entanglement growth. These results establish that the magic barrier is a universal feature of RMT thermalization, suggesting universality across quantum chaotic systems. We conclude that quantitative simulations of thermalization in ergodic quantum theories inherently require quantum computation.

</details>


### [9] [All Order Gravitational Wave Tails for Subleading Post-Newtonian Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20770)
*Debanjan Karan,Babli Khatun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典散射过程中低频引力波形中的尾项结构，特别是在牛顿极限和首阶后牛顿近似下，证明了这些尾项系数具有普适性，仅由散射物体的渐近轨迹决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The CMB constrains the gravitational constant $G$ at recombination to a few percent of its laboratory value, but the effect of $G$ on recombination itself is usually absorbed into a global fit rather than made explicit. We return to the semi-analytic description of the acoustic spectrum to ask how the acoustic scale $\ell_A$, the equality scale $\ell_{\rm eq}$ and the damping scale $\ell_{\rm damp}$ respond to a variation of $G$ during recombination, and where $G$ enters the residual ionization of the dark ages. We use a modified version of CLASS in which $G$ is varied through $λ_G \equiv \sqrt{G/G_{\rm N}}$, scanning $λ_G \in [0.25,2]$ at fixed physical densities and CMB temperature, with helium recomputed for each model. Analytic rate arguments are confronted with the numerical ionization histories, the visibility function, the Hu & White damping integral and the TT spectra. While $\ell_{\rm eq}$ and $\ell_A$ are nearly independent of $G$, non-equilibrium recombination gives $\ell_{\rm damp} \propto λ_G^{-0.304}$. The dependence exists only above a critical value of $G$, set by fundamental and cosmological parameters, a factor of 49 below $G_{\rm N}$. The dark-ages optical depth varies by only 16% for $G$ multiplied by 64, a negligible channel. Because $G$ enters only through the expansion rate, the imprint comes entirely from out-of-equilibrium recombination, and the competition between the atomic rates and the expansion is governed by one dimensionless combination, $α_G \equiv G m_e μ/\hbar c$, with $μ$ the mean mass of matter per photon. Each scale is the distance to last scattering divided by a length imprinted before it, so a late-time change of gravity rescales all three alike. The ratio $\ell_{\rm damp}/\ell_A$ therefore isolates $G$ at recombination and, up to lensing and screening, compares it directly with the laboratory value.

</details>

Abstract: The discovery of Little Red Dots (LRDs) at high redshifts with JWST has revealed a previously unknown phase of black-hole growth. However, their black-hole (BH) masses, BH-to-stellar mass ratios, cold-gas reservoirs, and depletion times remain uncertain. The recent discovery of low-redshift LRDs at $z\simeq0.1 - 0.9$ creates a decisive opportunity to access their low-$J$ CO lines for measuring molecular gas reservoirs, and the nearest systems can in principle be spatially resolved. Assuming a molecular gas mass of $10^9\,M_\odot$, the CO(2-1) detectability strongly depends on the assumed CO-to-H$_2$ conversion ($α_{\rm CO}$). With a large $α_{\rm CO} \simeq 315 - 725\,M_\odot\,({\rm K\,km\,s}^{-1}\,{\rm pc}^2)^{-1}$ at a low gas-phase metallicity, the CO line would remain very challenging for the current ALMA to detect, while ALMA2040 will likely deliver a robust CO detection and thus a gas-reservoir constraint for The Egg. Furthermore, with the best angular resolution at its CO(2-1) frequency (14.6 mas, or 27 pc), ALMA2040 may spatially resolve the CO emission from The Egg. Assuming an optimistic, SMC-like $α_{\rm CO}$ with a 50-hour deep integration, we produce mock position-velocity diagrams that may distinguish a $10^7\,M_\odot$ black hole from the no-BH model through its steeper central velocity structure. We conclude that ALMA2040 has the potential to directly measure the gas masses, depletion times, and excitation of local LRDs, while the CO-bright subset may ultimately yield dynamical constraints on the mass of their central black holes.

Abstract: Astrophysics seeks to explain how galaxies form and evolve through the interplay of stars, gas, and dust. Giant molecular clouds (GMCs), the main reservoirs and sites of star formation, link the cold interstellar medium to the birth of stars and stellar structures. Their properties reflect galactic disk conditions, and their evolution is shaped by stellar feedback and host-galaxy dynamics. Studying GMCs across cosmic time is therefore key to understanding how gas turns into stars and drives galaxy evolution. Hubble Space Telescope and James Webb Space Telescope observations reveal compact star-forming clumps in galaxies from Cosmic Noon to the Epoch of Reionization ($z\approx6$), but their link to the molecular gas reservoir is still unclear. We do not yet know how GMC properties vary with redshift and environment, how molecular gas, dust, and young stars are arranged, or whether clumps form in unstable disks or disturbed systems. Addressing these questions requires spatially resolved observations of the cold interstellar medium at the scales probed by optical/near-infrared facilities. ALMA2040, a transformational next-generation sub-mm facility, will provide the sensitivity and angular resolution needed to detect individual GMCs in representative lensed and unlensed galaxies at $z>1$. CO and [CII], complemented by dust continuum and molecular-gas kinematics, will measure the molecular gas reservoir, its physical conditions, and its dynamical state. Together with JWST and the ELT, ALMA2040 will connect cold gas, dust, and stellar structures across cosmic time. ALMA2040 will revolutionize our understanding of (i) the properties and evolution of GMCs and their connection to star-forming clumps, (ii) the spatial interplay between molecular gas, dust, and young stars, and (iii) the dynamics, survival, and formation of GMCs and stellar clumps.

Upcoming wide-field mm surveys will detect a new population of short-lived transients but will be limited by coarse localization and limited spectral characterization. ALMA2040, with dramatically improved sensitivity, wide instantaneous spectral coverage, rapid-response and sub-arraying modes, polarimetry and extended baselines, is essential for converting these discoveries into robust physical constraints. Abstract: In this report, we discuss the performance of the Walsh phase-switching scheme for Band 4 (550-750 MHz) polarimetric observations with the upgraded GMRT (uGMRT). We have carried out test observations with the Walsh modulation enabled and disabled, using the polarized calibrator 3C138 as the target and unpolarized calibrator 3C147 for amplitude, phase and leakage calibration. The two Walsh datasets (ON & OFF) were processed using identical flagging, calibration, and imaging procedures. The Walsh ON data showed smoother leakage solutions and lower residual scatter in the Stokes I, Q, U data. The Walsh scheme also suppressed a fraction of anomalous data at the correlation stage, reducing the amount of subsequent flagging required during data processing. The final self-calibrated images obtained with Walsh ON showed improved image fidelity, lower r.m.s. noise, and reduced error in the fractional polarization of 3C138. However, the possibility that the Walsh scheme itself introduces spurious frequency-dependent structure into the Stokes Q and U signals remains and warrants closer investigation. Overall, these results demonstrate the potential of Walsh phase switching to mitigate instrumental cross-talk and improve the reliability of polarimetric measurements with the uGMRT.

Abstract: We present a multi-method machine-learning study for the binary photometric classification of point-like and extended sources in the Rubin Observatory Data Preview 1 (DP1) and Data Preview 2 (DP2). The workflow runs on the Rubin Science Platform using DP1 and DP2 ugrizy photometry, the DP1 ancillary photometric-redshift catalog, and the Monster astrometric and photometric reference catalog, without relying on external spectroscopy. Six methods (SVM, Random Forest, XGBoost, k-Nearest Neighbors, an Artificial Neural Network, and a soft Voting ensemble) are benchmarked in the deepest DP1 field, the Extended Chandra Deep Field South. These methods reach 10-fold cross-validation macro-F1 scores of 0.95-0.97 on the balanced training subset and test macro-F1 scores of 0.87-0.92 on the imbalanced held-out set, with RF and the SV ensemble tied at the top (0.918); the differences among the leading methods are within the statistical uncertainty. Permutation importance identifies FWHM as the dominant feature, and the six methods agree on 79.5% of the full catalog. The same pipeline is then applied without retuning the settings to the two other DP1 fields with full ugrizy coverage and photometric-redshift support, Euclid Deep Field South and the Low Galactic Latitude Field. Together, the three fields span a factor of approximately 7 in E(B-V) and approximately 10 in stellar density, yet headline macro-F1 remains above 0.87 in every field. We then apply the same pipeline to four DP2 fields (ECDFS, COSMOS, EDFS, and ELAIS-S1). Using colors and morphology, the best method reaches a test macro-F1 of 0.83 to 0.88 across these fields, and the six methods agree on 77 to 86% of each catalog. Adding the per-band photometric uncertainties as features raises the best macro-F1 to about 0.95 in ECDFS DP1 and 0.97 in ECDFS DP2 (and to approximately 0.97-0.98 in the other fields).

However, direct observational constraints on their spatial, temporal, and spectral signatures remain hard to obtain. At millimeter wavelengths, optically thick thermal emission provides a comparatively direct measure of gas temperature. While the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) already provides unique diagnostic capabilities, offering temperature diagnostics and high-cadence imaging, the current capabilities are still limiting the study of such dynamic, small-scale phenomena. Improved spatial resolution, enhanced uv-coverage, and simultaneous multi-band observations, as proposed for ALMA2040, would enable transformative progress in capturing dynamic processes across multiple heights and enable time-dependent three-dimensional thermal and magnetic tomography of the chromosphere. We present CHARIZARD, a pipeline that carries a raw measurement set through flagging, cross-calibration, self-calibration and imaging to full-Stokes images, and which is built around distributing that work. The observing band is repartitioned into sub-bands that stay independent through cross-calibration, the unit of work becomes the target field and sub-band pair at self-calibration, and stages touching disjoint data are run together. Each stage is an ordinary PBS or SLURM job, submitted and tracked by a scheduler-agnostic layer, and every computation runs inside a single rootless container, so a user holding no administrative rights gets the same behaviour on any cluster. Residual on-axis instrumental polarization is $0.13$ per cent in linear and $0.06$ per cent in circular; the deepest field reaches $4.11\,μ$Jy~beam$^{-1}$ over $787$~MHz with $σ_I/σ_V = 1.010$; positions agree with RACS-low to better than $0.4\arcsec$ and flux densities to a median ratio of $1.06$. A $1.5$~TB track completes $48.6$ core-hours of work in $18.4$~hours with self-calibration and polarization calibration. The next generation of the radio telescopes will not be short of data. CHARIZARD attempts to show how the distribution of the work provides a useful alternative for handling the staggering processing requirements.

In particular, both the outer Milky Way and the Magellanic clouds provide the closest low-metallicity environments with conditions similar to those of metal-poor galaxies, while remaining sufficiently close to soon allow spatial resolution of individual star-forming cores and disks. Recent observations with ALMA and other facilities have begun to unveil the physical and chemical properties of star-forming regions in low-metallicity environments. (3) How does dust evolution depend on metallicity, and what is the impact in the survival of protoplanetary disks? (4) Is the reduced amount of metals and dust shielding at low metallicity critical for the formation and survival of molecules? ALMA2040 will identify similarities and differences between solar- and sub-solar-metallicity star-forming regions, and answer the question: how do different environmental conditions influence the physical and chemical pathways leading to star formation?

By resolving CO and [C II] at kpc scales, ALMA revealed a surprising population of rotation-dominated disks, even at $z>2$, exhibiting kinematic signatures consistent with spiral arms, bars, and tidal tails. Achieving this goal demands a tenfold increase in line sensitivity and hence a next-generation sub-/millimetre facility: ALMA2040. This leap in sensitivity will enable routine resolved studies of hundreds of $M_\star>10^9$ M$_\odot$ galaxies, including those on and below the main sequence of star-forming galaxies, providing representative, statistical galaxy samples with sub-kpc constraints on the cold gas kinematics and morphology. By uniquely providing resolved CO, [C I], and [C II] observations across $z=1-6$, ALMA2040 will trace a much larger fraction of the cold-gas reservoir than is accessible today, extending resolved studies from rare massive and/or lensed systems to representative galaxy populations. Simultaneously, ALMA2040 will measure the fraction of star formation that is obscured by dust at cosmic dawn, and thereby the extent to which rest-UV and optical surveys miss early galaxy build-up. Abstract: The discovery of massive quiescent galaxies at high redshift by JWST has revealed that star formation can cease rapidly in the early Universe, yet the physical mechanisms responsible for quenching remain unknown. A key missing element is the systematic measurement in statistically representative quiescent samples of the cold gas and dust content, which are crucial for distinguishing quenching mechanisms and evolutionary pathways. Such observations are currently not feasible because state-of-the-art sub-millimeter facilities (e.g., ALMA) cannot routinely detect the faint molecular gas and dust reservoirs of quiescent galaxies, nor pinpoint outflowing molecular gas. A next-generation facility (ALMA2040) capable of providing transformative access to the cold interstellar medium of quiescent galaxies across redshift is therefore essential. In particular, it will allow the community to probe the molecular gas content of quiescent galaxies at cosmic noon and beyond by targeting the CO, [CI], and [CII] emission lines, as well as unveiling the physical conditions of the cold interstellar medium by constraining the CO spectral line energy distribution. It will also reveal the dust content of the passive population, a crucial ingredient to constrain the different quenching mechanisms. Finally, it will enable probing the molecular gas kinematics and identify molecular outflows driven by supermassive black holes. When combined with the rich legacy of JWST, ELT, and SKAO surveys, ALMA2040 will deliver a comprehensive, multi-phase view of feedback and quenching, allowing us to establish when, how, and why galaxies shut down their star formation across cosmic time.

Abstract: The 2026 update to the Declaration of Principles Concerning the Conduct of the Search for Extraterrestrial Intelligence provides a framework for post-detection practice. Like earlier versions, it addresses information content directly: a confirmation report should record any detected content, and the underlying data, methods, and code should be openly preserved and disseminated. The declaration is necessarily concise and is intended to be supported by supplementary material that examines specific issues in greater depth. This paper contributes to that process by considering the possible detection of an information-rich technosignature: a signal carrying data, imagery, or machine-readable content. Whereas the prevailing concept of a detection remains that of a beacon, a signal with recoverable and potentially exploitable content would shift much of the post-detection challenge from verification to information stewardship, while encouraging competitive efforts to decode and use the signal. Few studies have addressed this scenario directly, and the geopolitical literature has so far framed post-detection competition mainly in terms of state actors. Large corporations now possess the computing power, AI capabilities, and, in some cases, receiving infrastructure needed to independently pursue an extraterrestrial signal without any obligation to disclose their findings. This largely unexamined dimension materially changes the post-detection landscape. These considerations bear directly on the declaration's commitment to openness and transparency, and on how that commitment can be upheld when powerful actors outside the SETI community may not share it. I identify information-rich technosignatures as a topic for supplementary guidance and offer a preliminary set of questions for the community to consider.

</details>

Method: 基于天线散射理论，将互耦分解为可建模的天线模式和剩余的结构模式；利用HERA类似构型的全波仿真验证模式贡献；引入扩展的全息反投影方法在孔径平面直观表征耦合扰动。

Abstract: Mutual coupling between elements of low-frequency arrays is a leading systematic for 21 cm cosmology, leaking bright foregrounds into otherwise clean regions of the power spectrum. Many mitigation models describe coupling as a linear combination of uncoupled signals re-radiated from neighboring feeds, but data suggest additional mechanisms. Inspired by antenna scattering theory, we decompose coupling into an antenna mode, constructed from translated isolated responses and expected to be removable by linear combination models, and a structural mode, the remainder. Full-wave simulations of HERA-like feeds and dishes in a 37-element hexagonal array show that the antenna mode accounts for only about 1% of the beam perturbation power, suggesting limited scope for mitigation with linear models. Two effects nevertheless lead to substantially better suppression. First, correlating beams and weighting them by the sky changes the modes' relative importance: correcting the antenna mode can remove more than half of the coupling power in simulated visibilities. Second, relaxing the coefficient relationships imposed by re-radiation allows linear corrections to remove structural-mode coupling too. Beam-derived corrections can reduce coupling power by over an order of magnitude in our noiseless simulations of perfectly redundant arrays. Fitting directly to known isolated visibilities reduces residuals further on the fitted field, but this target is generally unavailable in observations. Practical correction therefore requires additional constraints to separate the unknown sky from coupling while preserving the cosmological signal. We introduce an extension of holographic back-projection to coupled beams, providing an intuitive picture of these perturbations in the aperture plane.

</details>

Abstract: Galaxies do not form in isolation, but rather as part of a wider interconnected web mediated at their interface by an extended gaseous atmosphere - the Circum-Galactic Medium (CGM). This dynamic and multiphase region is fed and shaped by gas inflows and mergers from the intergalactic medium and by feedback-driven outflows and radiation from the central galaxy. The CGM is imprinted by the past, present and future evolution of its central galaxy and environment. The cool atomic and molecular phases are of particular interest as they dominate the mass budget of outflows and provide a reservoir of direct star forming fuel for the galaxy to accrete. In the early universe where accretion rates, mergers, and feedback processes peak, the cycle of gas through the CGM is most vigorous. Sub-mm observations have detected cold gas in extended halos and gas flows around high redshift galaxies but are thus far limited to the most massive, extreme or gravitationally lensed sources, using just a handful of the strongest emission and absorption lines. A significant increase ($5-10\times$) in spectral line sensitivity compared with the current ALMA is needed to push physical scales, densities and properties to a new regime. We present three key scientific cases for an ALMA2040 telescope: (i) direct imaging of extended [CII]-traced CGM halos around Milky-Way-progenitor galaxies at $z=4$-8; (ii) resolved detection of cold, clumpy outflows on $\sim$100 pc scales tied to individual star-forming clumps; and (iii) multi-species/-transition emission and absorption diagnostics that jointly constrain the density, chemistry, and radiation fields permeating the cool CGM and its multiphase gas flows.

Abstract: Water megamasers in the circumnuclear disks of active galactic nuclei are among the most precise probes of supermassive black holes. VLBI imaging of 22 GHz disk masers has provided geometric distances to independently constrain $H_0$, accurate black hole mass measurements, and sub-parsec views of accretion disk geometry. ALMA has opened a new window into water maser transitions in the sub-mm/mm regime, which trace temperatures, densities and disk regions that are inaccessible at 22 GHz, and can be observed at higher spatial resolution and with less free-free absorption. However, current detections are limited to a few bright extragalactic masers in nearby galaxies. We present the case for a next-generation sub-mm/mm interferometer - ALMA2040 - that would open a new parameter space for maser studies. We show that a 10-fold increase in line sensitivity and three times longer baselines compared to ALMA would transform sub-mm maser studies from a few bright detections into a population-level probe, delivering black hole masses and geometric distances from sub-mm masers alone, independent of and complementary to 22 GHz measurements. Integrated into a VLBI array, ALMA2040 would resolve maser disks out to $>$160 Mpc, extend maser black hole masses into the largely untested intermediate-mass regime, reveal magnetic field structure in accretion disks through polarization studies, and test systematics in $H_0$ measurements.

Abstract: The interstellar medium (ISM) contains a rich molecular inventory that provides a unique window into the chemistry operating during star and planet formation and its connection to prebiotic chemistry. More than 350 interstellar molecules are currently known, including increasingly complex organic molecules and species containing elements beyond the second row of the periodic table. Yet the limits that interstellar chemical complexity can reach, its evolution from molecular clouds to planetary systems, and its contribution to the molecular feedstock of life remain poorly constrained. Addressing these questions requires sensitive, broadband observations capable of detecting multiple weak rotational transitions of molecular species, such as amino acids, nucleobases, sugars and lipids, towards star- and planet-forming environments. Current facilities, including ALMA, are limited by instantaneous spectral coverage, sensitivity (especially on extended spatial scales), and the trade-off between total bandwidth and integration time. The proposed ALMA2040 facility would overcome these limitations through at least a tenfold improvement in spectral-line sensitivity, high-fidelity imaging from sub-arcsecond to tens-of-arcsecond scales, and ultra-broad instantaneous frequency coverage from 35 to 950 GHz at high spectral resolution. These capabilities would allow systematic detections of species with molecular abundances with respect to H$_2$ below 10$^{-11}$, facilitating the identification of new complex and heavy-element-bearing species through multiple transitions, and providing complete molecular inventories across evolutionary stages. Abstract: The processes in the last stages of planet formation determine the final assembly and composition of planets. After the protoplanetary discs dissipate, a system of planets and planetesimals -- analogues of the Asteroid and Kuiper Belts -- remains. Ongoing collisions between these bodies produce second-generation dust and gas that form a debris disc. Resolved images of such belts allow us to trace the dynamical history of these systems, including giant impacts and planet-belt interactions, during the crucial era from 10 to a few 100 Myr of terrestrial planet formation. High-resolution mm and optical/near-IR imagers have revolutionised our view of the brightest planetesimal belts, providing first glimpses of diverse structures, many of which show plausible planet imprints, while ALMA has revealed gas in a growing number of systems. Yet these results are strongly biased toward the brightest, mostly young belts around early-type stars, and even for these, crucial details remain undetectable at the required resolution due to their typically low surface brightness. A future (sub-)mm facility with an order of magnitude leap in continuum sensitivity and 10x higher line sensitivity would transform our understanding of late-stage planet formation by enabling the first statistically meaningful surveys of faint outer planetesimal belts across all spectral types, imaging planetary collisions in the inner regions of planetary systems, and gas measurements that trace volatile delivery to terrestrial planets. For the first time, this would reach the faint majority of belts, rather than only the rare bright discs accessible today.

Abstract: Polarization reveals details of circumstellar disks that are not readily available from total intensity observations. Via multi-wavelength polarimetric observations of disks, we can trace how planets form, how dust grains evolve at the onset of planet formation, and how magnetic fields (B-fields) influence accretion. Since polarized emission from these disks is typically at levels from 0.5% to a few percent of the total emission, very high sensitivities are needed. Current ALMA capabilities make these key measurements of protoplanetary disks challenging due to (1) long integrations with separate observations to measure multi-wavelength polarization of substructures, (2) prohibitive time requests to observe large samples of disks, (3) limited sensitivity and frequency coverage for polarization in spectral lines; and (4) insufficient calibration of polarization in all the Stokes parameters. An ALMA2040 with large collecting area, increased bandwidth with multi-band capabilities, and accurate polarization calibration will provide a great leap in our understanding of the formation of protoplanets through polarization. Specifically, such an ALMA2040 can use polarized emission from the continuum and spectral lines to: (A) provide stringent tests of streaming instabilities; (B) accurately constrain grain shape, size, porosity, and alignment; (C) resolve polarization of substructures for a large sample of disks with resolutions less than 5 au; (D) quickly survey 100s of disks; and (E) measure the B-field strength and morphology of disks.

*Omer Imran*

TL;DR: 本文通过注入15,000个不同形态与强度的真实信号和干扰到Green Bank望远镜实测背景中，评估了标准机器学习射电技术信号探测器的鲁棒性。结果表明该探测器在训练分布外表现差：召回率不随信噪比单调上升，甚至随亮度增加而下降；对某些干扰的误报率高达0.627；Grad-CAM显示其关注区域与注入路径一致；且因仅使用ON数据（忽略关键的OFF对照数据），本质上无法区分天体信号与地面干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射电技术信号搜索依赖机器学习检测器，但其验证仅基于训练分布内留出数据，无法反映真实场景中信号形态未知、人为干扰种类无限的实际挑战。

Method: 在真实Green Bank观测背景中，向模型输入前冻结一个标准竞赛级检测器，系统注入15,000个涵盖10种信号形态、5种幅度的信号及干扰；结合Grad-CAM可视化注意力机制，并测试ON-OFF时间平均滤波效果。

Result: 检测器召回率未按预期随信噪比提升，反而在最亮‘针状’信号下从0.590骤降至0.090；一种干扰形态误报率达0.627，超过其对真实信号的基线召回率（0.5525）；Grad-CAM证实其关注注入路径；ON-OFF滤波等效剔除真假信号；检测器仅用ON面板，完全忽略可判别源位置的OFF信息。

Conclusion: 当前主流检测器本质是训练数据相似性匹配器，而非物理源判别器；其高误报与漏检源于架构设计（忽略OFF数据）与训练偏差；提升可靠性需引入物理约束（如ON-OFF对比）与分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Radio technosignature searches rely on machine learning detectors to shrink the candidate pool before a human looks at it. They are validated on held-out data from the training distribution, which excludes the cases where they are most likely to fail: a technosignature has unknown morphology, and terrestrial interference is unbounded in variety. I trained a standard competition detector once, froze it, and probed it with 15,000 injections across ten morphologies and five amplitudes, placed into real Green Bank backgrounds. Two directions were tested: genuine signals with morphologies other than a straight narrowband line, and interference resembling the training data. Recall did not follow the predicted ordering, and it fell as signals brightened, even for a needle of the kind the detector was trained on (from 0.590 to 0.090). One interference morphology reached a false positive rate of 0.627, comparable to the detector's own baseline recall of 0.5525 on genuine signals. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) showed attention along the injected path in one missed family by a pre-specified test, and visually in a second. A pre-specified, time-averaged ON-OFF filter removed false alarms and genuine detections at the same rate. The detector reads only the ON panels. Since the only evidence distinguishing a genuine candidate from terrestrial interference lies in the discarded OFF panels, it cannot tell if a signal originates from the target star; it can only tell if it resembles the training data. This explains the false alarm directly; the missed signals reflect what the detector learned to recognise, which reading the OFF panels would not by itself change.

</details>

Abstract: The initial chemical composition of planets, primitive bodies in our solar system, and exoplanets is ultimately set by their nascent protoplanetary disks. Yet observing key carbon-, oxygen-, and nitrogen-bearing molecules (as well as rarer but important sulphur- and phosphorus-bearing species) in the inner (exo)planet-forming zone ($\lesssim 10$~au) remains extremely challenging with current facilities. Therefore, to construct a complete picture of the chemistry of planet formation and understand how (exo)planetary composition is set by physical and chemical processes within the protoplanetary disk, a more powerful observing facility, i.e., ALMA2040, is needed. Compared to current ALMA (the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array) this new facility must provide >~ a factor of 10 in line sensitivity at a spatial resolution of <~ 5 au at 140 pc, i.e., a factor of 3 - 4 better resolution to low surface brightness emission than currently possible. This distance includes many star-forming regions such as Taurus and Lupus that are host to populations of Sun-like stars. ALMA2040 will enable mapping of disk chemical composition on terrestrial planet-forming scales (within the water snowline) and detect molecules that are the feedstock for potentially habitable worlds.

With an anticipated ~30 times improvement in continuum sensitivity (compared to the current ALMA), simultaneous multi-band receivers, and (sub-)kpc angular resolution, ALMA2040 will transform resolved dust thermometry into a systematic survey science. Here, we present the scientific rationale and technical requirements, highlighting a future statistically meaningful survey with > 100 representative main-sequence galaxies across cosmic epochs. Realistic visibility simulations demonstrate that ALMA2040 measures radial temperature gradients with ~0.5-4 K precision (L_IR~10^{12} L_sun in 2 h on-source at z~2). It also offers some natural advantages: above 35 GHz, the interstellar and interplanetary media are effectively transparent and non-dispersive, terrestrial interference is sparse, and interferometry suppresses what interference remains. Both antenna gain and usable fractional bandwidth increase with frequency, so a civilisation optimising directed, high-data-rate links has advantageous reasons to operate in this regime, as illustrated by the ongoing migration of our own telecommunications systems to much higher frequencies. Abstract: In analytically tractable models of Einstein-scalar critical collapse, it has been shown that the quantum self-energy of the collapsing matter in the dominant $s$-wave sector grows with Lyapunov exponent $D-2$, eventually competing with the classical instability that controls black hole formation. This competition shifts the naked singularity threshold and produces a finite mass gap within semiclassical control, yet that control may appear threatened as the spacetime dimension $D\to\infty$. We study this quantum growing mode in both continuously (CSS) and discretely (DSS) self-similar critical spacetimes at large $D$ and establish that the universal Lyapunov exponent survives both forms of self-similarity. In CSS, the classical approach to a naked singularity is interrupted by the appearance of trapped surfaces. At large $D$, the horizon tracing reduces to a Bessel problem, yielding a quantum wall whose radius obeys $r_h\propto(D-2)^{-2}$. This radius can remain above the Planck scale at finite $D$, although maintaining this hierarchy becomes increasingly demanding as $D$ grows. In DSS, the same growth is modulated by echoing and can retain memory of previous echoes. The simplest anomaly-induced action exhibits accumulating memory but is singular at the spherical center. We construct a covariant one-loop action whose stress tensor remains smooth throughout the DSS domain and organize it into an echo-periodic structure without memory. This suggests that the smooth vacuum polarization of the genuine four-dimensional DSS Choptuik spacetime may similarly be governed by its exact echoing symmetry.

</details>

Motivation: 理解超相对论粒子在黑洞光圈附近散射时的引力波辐射特性，特别是通量的发散行为及其物理起源，为自力计算和强场引力波建模提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin（WKB）近似求解Regge-Wheeler方程，结合对无穷多极矩求和的解析处理，推导通量发散的精确对数增强系数；辅以低阶多极的数值计算与高阶多极的渐近分析，估算次主导项；并尝试将结论外推至克尔度规下的赤道圆轨道。

Conclusion: WKB方法能有效刻画黑洞强场区自力可观测量的渐近行为；光圈附近的对数发散结构具有普适性，反映时空几何不稳定性与辐射过程的深层联系；该框架为未来高精度波形建模和极端质量比旋进（EMRI）研究提供了新工具与见解。

We then estimate the coefficients of the subleading pole contributions by combining numerical calculations at low multipoles with the exact asymptotics at large multipoles. We further conjecture an extension to equatorial circular orbits in Kerr. Interestingly, our results also inform the analytical structure of the energy and angular momentum losses of ultrarelativistic scattering orbits near the light ring. More broadly, our work illustrates how WKB methods can provide the asymptotic behavior of self-force observables in regimes where numerical mode sums become increasingly challenging.

Abstract: We study the bound states perturbations in the interior of the Schwarzschild black hole and examine whether or not their microstates spectrum can furnish a statistical description of the black hole entropy. These states contain a discrete set of modes characterized by the labels $(s, p,\ell,m)$, where $s$ represents the spin of the species, $p$ denotes the radial excitation number while $(\ell,m)$ are the usual angular momentum labels. Requiring that the shortest wavelength resolved by the highly excited bound states remains above the quantum gravity scale introduces an angular cutoff $\ell_{\rm max}\sim r_H/L_{\rm Pl}$. The resulting angular degeneracy therefore scales with the horizon area. We count the corresponding configurations in the microcanonical ensemble and find an entropy of the form $S_{\rm bs}=αA/\hbar G$. Demanding that the bound state sector to be in thermal equilibrium with the black hole at the Hawking temperature fixes the fraction of the bound state energy in total black hole energy. For the gravitational perturbations sector, the equilibrium bound state contribution in entropy and the energy fraction are typically small. We also examine the conditions under which the bound states could account for the full black hole entropy. Although such saturation is possible at the level of state counting, it requires an order unity fraction in the black hole energy and therefore the backreactions on the background geometry cannot be ignored.

Abstract: We investigate the fate of big-bang singularity and coherence between distinct clock states in a quantum cosmological setting within the Page-Wootters framework. By constructing its solution as a Gaussian wave packet in the expanding branch, we derive the corresponding conditional state and probability density of the universe. We find that the conditional probability density vanishes in the zero-volume limit, indicating resolution of the big-bang singularity within this probabilistic framework. By tracing out the remaining subsystem, we obtain the reduced density matrix and analyze the resulting coherence as a function of separation between different clock states. Thus near extremal Kerr exhibits parametrically distinct winding and polar-focusing scales, rather than a discontinuous jump between extremal and subextremal lensing hierarchies.

Abstract: Hydrodynamic modeling of viscous neutron stars requires a choice of theory of relativistic hydrodynamics. That distinct formulations contain the same physical content in the long-wavelength limit is referred to as ``hydrodynamic universality.'' It has been recently shown that hydrodynamic universality can break down in radial neutron star oscillations: causality and finite-size effects restrict access to the long-wavelength universal hydrodynamic regime in sufficiently viscous stars. We show that this breakdown occurs for a broad range of causal hydrodynamic frames in Bemfica--Disconzi--Noronha--Kovtun (BDNK) theory. Increasing viscosity collectively pushes this class of causal frames away from the BDNK regime of validity, eventually forcing all to lie outside it at bulk viscosities in the range $ζ\sim10^{30}$--$10^{31}\mathrm{g}/\mathrm{cm}/\mathrm{s}$. This breakdown of universality is accompanied by material changes in the oscillation mode structure: non-hydrodynamic modes become damped on timescales comparable to those of hydrodynamic modes, the first overtone can become longer lived than the fundamental mode, and unphysical instabilities with sub-millisecond timescales can emerge. Even in regimes where universality has broken down, we find that the inviscid threshold of gravitational collapse remains unchanged in BDNK theory. These results provide evidence that first-order theories of relativistic hydrodynamics may be insufficient for modeling sufficiently viscous neutron stars.
