Method: 基于多轴子混合通过QCD反常耦合的机制，构建包含QCD轴子与暗色禁闭规范扇区耦合的新模型；分析单轴子极限与耦合动力学，数值求解全耦合系统演化方程。

Result: 模型可同时再生观测重子不对称性与暗物质丰度；参数空间具可检验性：QCD轴子衰变常数接近当前天体物理下限，新重轴子处于未来天文与宇宙学观测灵敏度范围内；暗物质主导成分可为QCD轴子或重轴子。

Conclusion: 多轴子混合提供了一种技术自然且自洽的框架，将强CP问题、重子生成与暗物质起源统一于轴子物理；该框架预言可观测信号，有望被近期实验检验。

Abstract: The strong CP problem, dark matter, and the observed baryon abundance may all be connected dynamically through the QCD axion, though whether this connection is realized in Nature depends on additional degrees of freedom. We show that the required dynamics can arise from technically natural ingredients if multiple axions mix through the QCD anomaly. We review the connection between axion rotation and the baryon asymmetry, revisit the axiogenesis and kinetic-misalignment proposals, and derive new limits on their dynamics. Motivated by these considerations, naturalness and axion quality, we construct a realization in which mixing with additional axions both enhances the effective QCD axion field range through monodromy and dissipates excess rotational energy through mixing with a dark confining gauge sector, thereby avoiding overclosure. We study the resulting effective dynamics in several illustrative limits, uncovering phenomena that may arise more generally in multi-axion theories. We numerically evolve the full coupled system and show that it can simultaneously reproduce the observed baryon asymmetry and dark matter abundance within a predictive region of parameter space testable by near-future direct and indirect searches. Depending on the model parameters, either the QCD axion or the new heavy axion constitutes the dominant dark matter component. Successful baryogenesis favors a QCD axion decay constant near its current astrophysical lower bound, while the heavy axion lies within reach of forthcoming astrophysical and cosmological observations.

</details>


### [101] [Leptogenesis with a GeV-Scale Quasi-Dirac Neutrino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20729)
*Stephen F. King,Soumen Kumar Manna,Rishav Roshan,Arunansu Sil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GeV尺度准狄拉克中性轻子的轻子生成机制，通过自发破缺的整体轻子数与Z2对称性实现共振增强的希格斯衰变轻子生成，并预言其可在SHiP、MATHUSLA和FCC-ee等实验中探测，同时该对称性破缺还产生可探测的引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙中观测到的重子不对称性，同时使GeV尺度的类狄拉克中微子可被未来实验探测，并提供多信使（如引力波）验证途径。

Method: 基于类型I跷跷板机制，引入自发破缺的整体轻子数和离散Z2对称性，构造两个近简并的右手性中微子形成准狄拉克态；结合有限温效应，分析热希格斯衰变中的CP不对称性及共振增强机制；并研究对称性破缺导致的不稳定畴壁及其引力波信号。

Result: 成功复现观测到的重子不对称性（0.1–10 GeV质量范围），CP不对称性源于真空与热致质量劈裂的协同作用；准狄拉克中微子参数区与SHiP、MATHUSLA、FCC-ee探测灵敏度重叠；畴壁湮灭可产生可观测的随机引力波背景。

Conclusion: 该框架统一解释重子不对称性、中微子质量起源与新物理可探测性，兼具粒子物理与引力波天文检验前景。

Abstract: We discuss leptogenesis in a model with a GeV-scale quasi-Dirac neutral lepton, comprising mainly sterile states, which is accessible to future experiments. In particular, we show that Higgs-decay leptogenesis can naturally arise from the type-I seesaw mechanism with spontaneously broken global lepton number together with a discrete $Z_2$ symmetry, yielding two quasi-degenerate right-handed neutrinos (RHNs) which, to excellent approximation, form a single Dirac neutrino, with a tiny mass splitting as required for resonant enhancement generated by higher-dimensional lepton-number-violating operators. Incorporating finite-temperature effects, we show that the observed baryon asymmetry can be successfully generated through thermal Higgs decays for 0.1-10 GeV mass quasi-Dirac neutrinos, with the CP asymmetry arising from the interplay between the vacuum and thermal contributions to the mass splitting. In the parameter region of successful leptogenesis, the quasi-Dirac neutrino is accessible to future heavy neutral lepton searches at SHiP, MATHUSLA and FCC-ee. In addition, the same symmetry-breaking sector gives rise to unstable domain walls whose annihilation can generate a stochastic gravitational-wave background, providing a complementary probe of the framework.

</details>


### [102] [Auditing anapole dark matter: the limits of model independence in dark matter effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20751)
*Duncan K. Adams,Jared Barron,Megan H. McDuffie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在暗物质研究中，有效场论（EFT）方法的局限性，特别是针对通过维度六环极子算符与光子耦合的马约拉纳暗物质。作者指出不同紫外（UV）完备模型在高能对撞机、早期宇宙及直接探测实验中给出定性不同的预言，并强调通过搜寻UV模型中额外粒子的信号，可获得比直接探测暗物质本身更具竞争力甚至更优的限制。因此，所谓‘模型无关’的EFT约束并非普遍适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 底向上的EFT分析可能遗漏紫外完备模型中对暗物质产生和探测至关重要的过程，需考察具体UV实现以避免误导性结论。

Method: 选取Majorana暗物质通过anapole算符耦合光子的典型例子，构造并对比多个不同紫外完备模型，系统分析其在高能对撞机、热历史（早期宇宙）和直接探测中的现象学差异。

Result: 不同UV完成给出显著不同的预测：如对撞机末态、暗物质丰度机制、直接探测截面大小与能量依赖形式；且对UV中伴生粒子的搜索可提供比传统EFT暗物质探测更强的实验约束。

Conclusion: EFT框架下的‘模型无关’约束存在本质局限；暗物质研究必须结合具体紫外物理，否则可能导致错误或不完整的物理结论。

Abstract: Effective field theory is a powerful tool for studying the low-energy behavior of dark sector models without explicit dependence on the high-energy physics. However, bottom-up EFT analyses can miss important processes in UV completions that are relevant for dark matter production and detection. In this work we demonstrate explicitly that for Majorana dark matter coupled to the photon by the dimension-six anapole operator, different UV completions make qualitatively different predictions at high energy colliders, in the early universe, and in direct detection experiments. Additionally, we show that competitive, and in some cases, leading constraints on anapole dark matter can be derived by searching for signatures of additional particles in the UV completion rather than direct probes of the DM itself. We argue that the UV completions studied in this work have categorically different phenomenologies and that `model-independent' EFT constraints are not always applicable.

</details>


### [103] [Signatures of Leptophilic Baryogenesis at Muon Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20774)
*Hooman Davoudiasl,Ian M. Lewis,Miguel A. Soto Alcaraz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三希格斯场（Higgs Troika）的重子不对称产生机制，无需强一阶电弱相变，并在自发味破坏（SFV）框架下约束新希格斯与标准模型费米子的耦合；特别聚焦于轻子偏好型SFV，在未来多TeV量级缪子对撞机上探索其信号及宇宙学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙中重子不对称性，同时规避对强一阶电弱相变的依赖，并满足味物理实验限制。

Method: 构建含三个希格斯场的模型，采用轻子偏好型自发味破坏（leptophilic SFV）机制约束耦合，结合未来高能缪子对撞机（多TeV质心能量）模拟探测新标量粒子信号。

Result: 该模型可在缪子对撞机上实现可观测信号，其探测灵敏度足以覆盖满足宇宙学重子不对称要求的参数空间。

Conclusion: Higgs Troika模型在不依赖强一阶相变且兼容味物理约束的前提下，可通过未来缪子对撞机实验进行有效检验，为重子生成提供可行且可证伪的新路径。

Abstract: The baryon asymmetry of the Universe may be produced in a model with 3 Higgs fields, a Higgs Troika, without the need for a strong first order electroweak phase transition. The couplings of the new Higgs fields to Standard Model fermions are assumed in accord with the "Spontaneous Flavor Violation (SFV)" framework, in order to avoid large deviations from flavor physics constraints. We consider a leptophilic version of SFV and outline its potential signatures at future muon colliders, envisioned to be multi-TeV partonic center of momentum machines. Hence, they provide optimal venues to search for the leptophilic Higgs Troika baryogenesis mechanism. The projected reach for signals of the new scalars allows for probing the key components of the baryogenesis model at scales that accommodate cosmological requirements.

</details>


### [104] [Radiative transitions of hybrid mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22088)
*Shahriyar Jafarzade,Eric S. Swanson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用基于库仑规范QCD重夸克展开的组分胶子模型，计算了最低粲夸克-混合介子多重态的辐射跃迁，结果与部分格点QCD计算一致，但某些跃迁宽度存在明显差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射跃迁是探测混合介子中非微扰胶子动力学（特别是夸克自旋与胶子激发之间相互作用）的重要手段，对杰斐逊实验室寻找奇异强子具有直接意义。

Method: 采用基于库仑规范QCD重夸克展开的组分胶子模型，计算最低粲夸克-混合介子多重态的辐射跃迁过程。

Result: Y→η_cγ 和 Y→χ_{c0}γ 的跃迁宽度与淬火格点QCD计算结果吻合；而 η_{c1}→J/ψγ 的跃迁宽度则显著低于相应格点估计值。

Conclusion: 该模型在多数跃迁中表现良好，但在 η_{c1}→J/ψγ 过程中存在明显偏差，提示需进一步改进理论处理或考虑更完整的物理效应。

Abstract: Radiative transitions of hybrid mesons probe nonperturbative gluodynamics, particularly the interplay between quark spin and gluonic excitations, and are of direct relevance to the search for exotic hadrons at Jefferson Lab. We calculate these transitions across the lowest charmonium-hybrid multiplet in a constituent gluon model based on Coulomb-gauge QCD in the heavy quark expansion. The results for $Y\toη_cγ$ and $Y\to χ_{c0}γ$ overlap the width obtained in quenched lattice computations, whereas $η_{c1}\to J/ψγ$ is substantially below the corresponding lattice estimate.

</details>


### [105] [Collective Organization of String Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20793)
*Romuald A. Janik,Maciej A. Nowak,Marek M. Rams,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实时张量网络模拟，揭示了强耦合规范动力学中束缚弦碎裂的集体组织机制，发现中间质量区域的动力学可由单一集体模式主导，并为Lund碎裂模型中的面积律提供了微观支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相干束缚弦如何重组为强子物质是非平衡规范动力学中的核心未解问题。

Method: 采用实时张量网络方法模拟有质量Schwinger模型，分析电场概率分布的时间演化及集体模式行为。

Result: 发现中间质量下99%以上的电场分布演化由单一集体模式控制；该模式衰减对应母弦存活概率，其存活律呈现与Lund世界面面积律一致的高斯依赖；该模式还调控弦到介子的相变、非热有效流体状态方程及纠缠增长。

Conclusion: 集体碎裂是相干弦断裂的基本组织原理，为Lund强子化模型的核心机制提供了微观动力学起源解释。

Abstract: How coherent confining strings reorganize into hadronic matter is a central open problem in nonequilibrium gauge dynamics. Using real-time tensor-network simulations of the massive Schwinger model, we identify a collective organization underlying string fragmentation. For intermediate masses, the dynamics simplifies and more than $99\%$ of the temporal evolution of the electric-field probability distribution is governed by a single collective mode whose decay tracks the survival probability of the parent string. The extracted survival law exhibits the same Gaussian dependence implied by the Lund world-sheet area law, providing microscopic support for its characteristic area law dependence without assuming the Lund fragmentation mechanism. The same mode controls the transition from the coherent string to the produced mesons, explains the universal non-thermal effective fluid equation of state, and essentially controls the growth of bipartite entanglement through the causal propagation of massive pairs. These results identify collective fragmentation as an organizing principle of coherent string breaking and provide a microscopic perspective on the dynamical origin of a central ingredient of Lund hadronization.

</details>


### [106] [All three-loop four-point massless integrals with one off-shell leg](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20820)
*Thomas Gehrmann,Petr Jakubčík,Jungwon Lim,Andreas von Manteuffel,Cesare Carlo Mella,Lorenzo Tancredi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有一个离壳外腿的无质量三圈四点主积分的完整集合，通过被积函数分析识别出具有简单极点和单位前导奇点的被积函数，构造了一个规范积分基，并提供了所有相关积分的解析解和高精度级数展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些积分是大型强子对撞机上希格斯玻色子或矢量玻色子伴随喷注产生过程的三圈虚修正所必需的基本构件，用于实现下下一代领头阶QCD预测。

Method: 通过被积函数分析识别具有简单极点和单位前导奇点的被积函数，构建规范积分基；处理包含371个主积分和19维顶层扇区的积分族；提供到最小迭代积分集的映射及高精度级数展开。

Result: 获得了全部相关积分的解析解，表示为按其解析性质分级的最小迭代积分集，并附有高精度级数展开。

Conclusion: 成功构建了具有一个离壳外腿的无质量三圈四点主积分的完整规范基，为高精度QCD计算提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We present the complete set of massless three-loop four-point master integrals with one off-shell external leg. These are essential building blocks for the three-loop virtual corrections to Higgs-boson or vector-boson production in association with a jet at the Large Hadron Collider, required for next-to-next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD predictions. We construct a complete basis of canonical integrals by identifying integrands with simple poles and unit leading singularities, solely by integrand analysis. This includes an integral family with 371 master integrals and a 19-dimensional top sector. The solutions for all relevant integrals are provided as a map to a minimal set of iterated integrals graded according to their analytic properties, together with high-precision series expansions.

</details>


### [107] [Machine-learning-based neutrino-event generation constrained by experimental measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20870)
*Jose Luis Bonilla,Krzysztof M. Graczyk,Rwik Dharmapal Banerjee,Beata E. Kowal,Hemant Prasad,Jan T. Sobczyk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于μ子型中微子-原子核带电流散射的生成式代理模型，该模型无需事件级实验样本即可通过微分截面数据进行校准，并能泛化到不同中微子束流和未参与拟合的可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上难以获取完整事件级中微子相互作用样本，亟需一种能直接由微分截面数据校准、同时保持事件级生成能力与跨实验适用性的建模方法。

Method: 基于理论相互作用模型构建生成式代理模型，利用MicroBooNE实验测得的微分截面数据对其进行细调校准，并验证其对未参与拟合观测量及不同中微子通量场景的泛化能力。

Result: 校准后的代理模型不仅在拟合目标上表现优异，还显著改善了未参与拟合的可观测量描述；且可直接迁移至不同中微子束流实验，仍保持精确的出射μ子运动学事件级预测。

Conclusion: 实验微分截面数据可有效约束生成式事件模型，使其兼顾精度、泛化性与跨实验可迁移性，为中微子振荡分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a generative surrogate for charged-current \(ν_μ\)-nucleus scattering that can be calibrated to measured differential cross sections without event-level experimental samples. Starting from a theoretical interaction model, we fine-tune the surrogate to a MicroBooNE measurement and show that this also improves the description of observables not used in the fit. The calibrated model can also be applied to measurements from different neutrino fluxes without retuning, while retaining event-level predictions of outgoing-muon kinematics. Our results show that experimental cross sections can directly constrain generative event models while preserving their applicability across different observables and beam configurations.

</details>


### [108] [CT25: the new generation of CTEQ-TEA PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20940)
*Alim Ablat,Daniel Adamiak,Aurore Courtoy,Sayipjamal Dulat,Yao Fu,Marco Guzzi,T. J. Hobbs,Joey Huston,Maximiliano Ponce Chavez,Kirtimaan Mohan,Pavel Nadolsky,Tanishq Sharma,Dan Stump,Keping Xie,C. -P. Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CT25系列新一代CTEQ-TEA部分子分布函数（PDFs），旨在提升QCD精度计算，涵盖多种应用场景，并提供多种互补误差PDF集合以应对不同需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足高能物理实验（如HL-LHC和EIC）对大动量分数区域PDF不确定性的精确需求，以及多样化应用场景下对PDF精度与不确定度量化的新要求。

Method: 构建CTEQ-TEA Parton Cloud在线数据库，开展探索性全局拟合；提出多种互补误差PDF集合（如CT25、CT25m、CT25pd等），并引入新方法量化理论、参数化及核目标相关的不确定性。

Result: 发布CT25系列PDF集合，包括针对通用与专用计算优化的不同版本，并提供适用于大x区域PDF不确定性研究的互补框架。

Conclusion: CT25代表了PDF全球分析的重要进展，提升了QCD理论预测的可靠性与适用性，为未来高精度实验提供关键输入。

Abstract: This contribution reviews CT25, a new generation of CTEQ--TEA parton distribution functions (PDFs) for precision QCD. With its first member sets released in 2025, the CT25 family addresses common uses of parton distributions in diverse contexts. We describe the CTEQ--TEA Parton Cloud, an online database of exploratory global fits to precision QCD measurements, from which we select PDF solutions for general- and special-purpose calculations. These solutions are published as complementary error PDF ensembles---the baseline CT25, CT25m, CT25pd, and ensembles with conservative priors---optimized for distinct uses. They implement new approaches to quantifying uncertainties and different treatments of theoretical, parametrization, and nuclear-target uncertainties. In particular, they provide complementary frameworks for exploring PDF uncertainties at large partonic momentum fractions relevant to HL-LHC and EIC studies. Further details will appear in the forthcoming CT25 publication.

</details>


### [109] [Bounds on hadronic contributions to the anomalous magnetic moment of the $τ$ lepton from QCD Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20945)
*Siyuan Li,J. Ho,R. T. Kleiv,D. Leighton,E. J. Obbink,T. G. Steele*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则方法，结合Laplace和有限能量求和规则及Hölder不等式约束，对τ子反常磁矩的领头阶强子真空极化（HVP）贡献进行了严格限制，得到a_τ^HVP,LO = (3.501±0.048)×10⁻⁶，并与实验及格点QCD结果一致；同时给出a_μ^HVP,LO = (7.217±0.233)×10⁻⁸的联合QCD确定值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确测定τ子反常磁矩a_τ的强子真空极化主导贡献，弥补实验测量困难，需发展基于第一性原理QCD的理论约束方法；此前该方法已用于μ子，本文将其推广至τ子并提升精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯（LSRs）与有限能量（FESRs）QCD求和规则，结合Hölder等基本不等式构建理论边界；计算包含五阶圈图的轻夸克贡献、一阶夸克质量修正、四维凝聚态NLO与六维凝聚态LO项；引入粲偶素与底偶素共振贡献。

Result: 获得a_τ^QCD = (3.385±0.048)×10⁻⁶（纯QCD部分），加入粲/底偶素后得a_τ^HVP,LO = (3.501±0.048)×10⁻⁶；进一步融合格点结果，导出a_μ^HVP,LO = (7.217±0.233)×10⁻⁸。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了适用于任意轻子g−2的稳健QCD求和规则分析框架，为a_τ提供首个基于QCD第一性原理的高精度理论约束，推动低能QCD可观测量的精确计算方法发展。

Abstract: A QCD sum-rule study is performed to place bounds on the leading-order (LO) hadronic vacuum polarization (HVP) contribution to the anomalous magnetic moment of the $τ$ lepton ($a_τ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO},\, a_τ= (g-2)_τ/2$). The QCD contributions to $a_τ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO}$ are modeled as a combination of Laplace QCD sum rules (LSRs) and finite-energy QCD sum rules (FESRs) of various weights, which are bounded by Hölder and related fundamental inequalities. The light-quark ($u,\,d,\,s$) contributions are evaluated up to five-loop order in perturbative contributions in the chiral limit, LO in light quark mass corrections, next-to-leading order (NLO) in dimension-four QCD condensates and to LO in dimension-six QCD condensates. Extending previous work on $a_μ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO}$, we present a robust methodology for using QCD sum rules to constrain $a_τ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO}$, and more generally any lepton $g-2$. A criterion is developed to extract from the bounds analysis a final result of $a_τ^\mathrm{QCD} = \left( 3.385\pm 0.048 \right) \times 10^{-6}$, which after inclusion of charmonium and bottomonium contributions gives a final result $a_τ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO} = \left( 3.501\pm0.048\right) \times 10^{-6}$, comparable to data-driven and lattice predictions. Lattice QCD results along with our value for $a_τ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO}$ are used to obtain a combined QCD determination from lattice and sum-rule methods of $a_μ^\mathrm{HVP,\,LO} = (7.217\pm0.233)\times10^{-8}$. Our analysis provides fundamental QCD sum-rule inputs into the theoretical determinations of $a_τ$, and establishes new methodologies for QCD sum-rule determinations of low-energy observables.

</details>


### [110] [The Sill distribution for all partial waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20975)
*Krzysztof Kyzioł,Francesco Giacosa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Sill分布以适用于任意角动量分波（partial waves），在保持精确归一化的同时，正确描述了物理阈值附近的共振谱形，尤其对Δ(1232)++粒子的p波线形给出了高精度拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准相对论Breit-Wigner分布（rBW）在共振峰靠近低能阈值时失效；原始Sill分布在保持归一化的同时仅适用于s波衰变，亟需推广至更高阶分波。

Method: 通过对传播子质量项进行简单修正，在Sill分布框架中引入正确的ℓ波阈值行为，并保持整体归一化不变。

Result: 成功构建了适用于任意分波的广义Sill分布；p波Sill分布能精确拟合Δ(1232)++从阈值至1.35 GeV的线形，并同时复现其实轴质量和宽度及复极点位置，与PDG汇编结果一致。

Conclusion: 广义Sill分布为高能物理中近阈值共振的谱函数建模提供了更普适、准确且解析友好的工具，尤其适用于非s波主导的强子共振分析。

Abstract: In high-energy physics, short-lived resonances appearing in scattering processes are described by energy-dependent spectral functions. The relativistic Breit--Wigner distribution (rBW), obtained as a relativistic extension of the standard Breit--Wigner form, is commonly employed owing to its simplicity. However, when the lower-energy threshold lies sufficiently close to the resonance peak, sizable deviations from the rBW line shape are expected. The Sill distribution provides a simple way to incorporate the physical threshold while preserving exact normalization, but in its original formulation it is inherently restricted to $s$-wave decays. Here, we extend the Sill distribution to arbitrary partial waves. The correct $\ell$-wave threshold behaviour is implemented while exact normalization is retained through a simple modification of the mass term of the propagator. As a benchmark, the $p$-wave Sill provides a very good description of the $Δ(1232)^{++}$ line shape up to 1.35 GeV and simultaneously reproduces its on-shell mass and width and its complex pole position, in agreement with independent determinations compiled by the PDG.

</details>


### [111] [Testing the inelastic dark matter interpretation of the LZ event with heavy-element experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21041)
*Peter W. Graham,Juan P. Luengas,Harikrishnan Ramani,Samuel S. Y. Wong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LZ实验观测到的248 keV事件的非弹性暗物质解释，并分析了不同实验（如古探测器、中微子探测器）如何检验该模型，特别强调暗物质速度分布高能尾部（受大麦哲伦云影响）对模型可行性及参数测量的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验观测到的248 keV信号，并评估其是否可能源于非弹性暗物质；同时探究当前约束（如太阳中微子）下热Higgsino等候选者是否仍可行，关键在于暗物质速度分布的高能尾部细节尚不明确。

Method: 基于不同受大麦哲伦云（LMC）影响构建的暗物质速度分布模型，系统分析其对非弹性暗物质信号解释的影响；结合LZ数据，评估古探测器（重元素）、大型中微子实验（加重金属靶）等互补实验在检验模型、破除参数简并（质量、质量劈裂）及溯源LMC作用方面的潜力。

Result: 发现调节速度分布高能尾部（如降低通量或延伸至更高速度）可改善Higgsino拟合并规避太阳中微子限制；古探测器可覆盖大部分相关参数空间并确定质量与劈裂；中微子探测器通过辐射衰变率测量可为Higgsino解释提供强证据。

Conclusion: 即使氙实验确认该信号，仍需多实验协同验证其非弹性暗物质本质；提升速度分布模拟分辨率至关重要；古探测器与增强型中微子实验是检验该解释的关键互补手段。

Abstract: We consider the inelastic dark matter interpretation of the recent 248 keV event observed by LZ and identify different experiments that could test it. Details of the underlying model and the ability of complementary experiments to test it depend significantly on the high-velocity tail of the dark matter distribution. We consider several example velocity distributions motivated by the impact of the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC) in order to map out the effects of this dependence. For example, we illustrate the type of high-velocity tail that would make the thermal Higgsino viable despite stringent constraints from solar neutrinos: Reducing the flux of the tail relative to simulation results improves the Higgsino fit, while extending the tail to higher velocities at fluxes below current simulation resolution evades the solar neutrino bound. This motivates more detailed simulations at higher resolution to understand the tail better. Even if xenon experiments confirm the signal event, complementary experiments are still required to test whether the underlying model is inelastic dark matter and to break degeneracies in the measurement of dark matter parameters such as mass and mass splitting. Heavy-element paleodetectors could cover most of the parameter space relevant to the LZ event. Their use of different elements could determine the dark matter mass and splitting, while their unique sensitivity to the history of the dark matter distribution could test the signal's LMC origin. Luminous detection in large neutrino detectors with heavy-element supplements could directly test the inelastic nature of the signal and measure the mass splitting, while its sensitivity to the radiative decay rate could provide strong evidence for the Higgsino interpretation.

</details>


### [112] [Two-loop HTL-resummed thermodynamics for pure $\mathcal N=2$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21116)
*Qianqian Du,Bingxiang Luo,Ubaid Tantary*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了四维N=2超对称杨-米尔斯理论在热场论框架下的两圈硬热圈（HTL）重求和热力学势，采用维数约化正则化方法，结果具有规范不变性，并通过真空能量、胶子质量与夸克质量反项完成重整化；热质量参数由包含跑动耦合的自洽间隙方程数值确定，并据此计算了压强、熵密度、能量密度及迹反常等热力学量，分析了其下阶修正效应与重整化标度依赖性，并考察了夸克质量设定（m_q=0与否）对结果的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超对称杨-米尔斯理论在高温极限下的非微扰热力学性质，特别是高阶量子修正对热力学量的影响及对重整化方案的敏感性。

Method: 采用硬热圈（HTL）重求和框架结合两圈微扰计算，使用维数约化正则化，引入真空能量、胶子质量m_d和夸克质量m_q反项进行重整化；热质量参数由含跑动耦合的自洽间隙方程数值求解。

Result: 得到了规范不变的两圈HTL重求和热力学势；计算了压强、熵密度、能量密度和迹反常，发现NLO修正在小耦合下为正且显著，在大耦合下减弱重整化标度依赖；m_q=0设定会显著改变m_d与标量质量m_s，并反转前三个热力学量的NLO修正符号，定性改变迹反常行为。

Conclusion: NLO量子修正对热力学量有实质性影响，且结果强烈依赖于夸克质量处理方式，凸显在超对称热场论中谨慎选择质量参数方案的重要性。

Abstract: We compute the two-loop hard-thermal-loop (HTL) resummed thermodynamic potential of pure four-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory using regularization by dimensional reduction. Our result is gauge invariant and was renormalized using vacuum energy, gluon mass $m_d$ and quark mass $m_q$ counterterms. The thermal mass parameters are determined self-consistently and numerically from gap equations incorporating the running coupling. Based on these solutions, we numerically calculate the scaled pressure, entropy density, energy density, and trace anomaly and compare them with the results in the static perturbative limit through $\mathcal O(λ^2)$. For the first three quantities, next-to-leading-order (NLO) corrections are positive and have discernible effects at small coupling, and reduce the renormalization-scale dependence at relatively large couplings. We assess $m_q$-prescription dependence by comparing these results with those obtained by setting $m_q=0$. We find that it has a substantial impact on $m_d$ and the scalar mass $m_s$ at large couplings, and it reverses the signs of the NLO corrections to the first three quantities and qualitatively alters the trace anomaly.

</details>


### [113] [PTagent: An LLM-assisted tool for finite-temperature model construction in cosmological phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21118)
*Lei Wu,Yang Xiao,Jin Min Yang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出PTagent框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）代理从SARAH模型文件、论文和DRalgo源中自动构建宇宙相变研究所需的有限温度数值模型，分离模型解释与数值实现，提升可检验性与可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学相变计算需要定义明确、理论一致的有限温度数值模型，但其人工构建过程繁琐且易出错，亟需自动化、可复现的建模方法。

Method: 设计基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体框架PTagent，结合专用后端模块：LLM负责解析和组织模型信息（如SARAH文件、论文、DRalgo），识别未决物理选择；后端执行模型依赖的计算，并生成CosmoTransitions或PhaseTracer格式的有限温度势函数。

Result: 实现了模型解释与数值实现的解耦，支持可审查、可复现的有限温度模型构建流程，并成功生成兼容主流相变求解器的数值势。

Conclusion: PTagent为宇宙学相变研究提供了首个融合LLM智能解析与专业物理计算的自动化建模框架，显著提升建模效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Cosmological phase-transition calculations require well-defined finite-temperature numerical models, whose construction involves assembling model information under consistent theoretical prescriptions. In this work we present \texttt{PTagent}, a large language model (LLM) agent-based framework for constructing such models from SARAH model files, research papers, and DRalgo sources. The LLM agent interprets and organizes model information and identifies unresolved physical choices, while dedicated backends perform the model-dependent calculations. After review and approval of the resulting physical model specification, the backends generate the corresponding CosmoTransitions-style or PhaseTracer-style numerical potentials. By separating model interpretation from numerical implementation, PTagent provides an inspectable and reproducible approach to finite-temperature model construction for cosmological phase-transition studies.

</details>


### [114] [Two-variable longitudinal distribution of the $Λ_b$ in a three-quark light-front model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21148)
*Shuai Xu,Xiao-Nan Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过协变的三夸克光前波函数确定了Λ_b重子的归一化双变量纵向分布，不引入初等二夸克，保留了重-轻及内部轻夸克动量尺度，并给出相关平均值及其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供一个具有有限质量的三夸克纵向结构基准，并区分重-轻动量分配与轻夸克对内关联，需构建不依赖于初等二夸克假设的Λ_b纵向分布模型。

Method: 采用协变三夸克光前波函数，选取标量ud通道（Cγ_5γ^+结构），对两个独立横动量积分，导出归一化双变量纵向分布Ψ_2^LF(x_u,x_d)。

Result: 得到模型尺度下的价态分布Ψ_2^LF(x_u,x_d)，具有x_u↔x_d对称性，集中在轻夸克份额均较小区域；计算得⟨x_u⟩=⟨x_d⟩=0.130，⟨x_b⟩=0.739，⟨ζ²⟩=0.153，⟨ζ⁴⟩=0.055，及其参数变化范围。

Conclusion: 该模型成功分离重-轻动量分配与轻夸克对内动量分享，为Λ_b重子纵向结构提供了紧凑、物理清晰的有限质量基准。

Abstract: We determine a normalized two-variable longitudinal distribution of the $Λ_b=(ud)b$ baryon from a covariant three-quark light-front wave function. The scalar $ud$ channel is selected with the good-component structure $Cγ_5γ^+$, and the two independent transverse momenta are integrated without introducing an elementary diquark. The resulting model-scale valence distribution, $Ψ_2^{\rm LF}(x_u,x_d)$, retains both the heavy--light and internal-light momentum scales of the parent wave function. It is symmetric under $x_u\leftrightarrow x_d$, is concentrated where both light-quark fractions are small, and separates the total light-pair momentum from its internal sharing. For the central parameter set we find $\langle x_u\rangle=\langle x_d\rangle=0.130$, $\langle x_b\rangle=0.739$, $\langleζ^2\rangle=0.153$, and $\langleζ^4\rangle=0.055$, where $ζ=(x_u-x_d)/(x_u+x_d)$. Parameter variations give $\langle x_u\rangle=0.130^{+0.007}_{-0.007}$, $\langle x_b\rangle=0.739^{+0.014}_{-0.014}$, $\langleζ^2\rangle=0.153^{+0.014}_{-0.016}$, and $\langleζ^4\rangle=0.055^{+0.009}_{-0.009}$. The heavy--light momentum partition is controlled mainly by $β_{b,ud}$ and $m_b$, whereas the internal $u$--$d$ sharing is most sensitive to $β_{ud}$. The result provides a compact finite-mass benchmark for three-quark longitudinal structure, with distinct observables for heavy--light momentum sharing and correlations inside the light pair.

</details>


### [115] [Nonleptonic decays of the double-charmed baryon $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$ with dynamically determined baryon wave functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21307)
*Yu-Shuai Li,Hong-Tao An*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了双粲重子Ξcc+到单粲重子与轻赝标介子的两体非轻子衰变，结合非相对论夸克模型与数值求解三体薛定谔方程所得重子波函数，同时考虑可因子化与不可因子化（特别是W-交换）振幅；发现W-交换贡献在多个道中与可因子化振幅相当甚至主导，并预测了若干分支比高达百分之几的衰变道，为LHCb实验提供了重要候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LHCb合作组近期发现双粲重子Ξcc+的启发，系统研究其两体非轻子衰变特性。

Method: 在非相对论夸克模型框架下，结合由数值求解三体薛定谔方程得到的重子波函数，计算可因子化与不可因子化（尤其是基于1/2±低激发粲重子中间态极点模型的W-交换）衰变振幅。

Result: W-交换振幅在多个衰变道中与可因子化振幅相当甚至占主导；预测了多个CF衰变道的大分支比，如B(Ξcc+→Ξc′0π+) = 8.90%、B(Ξcc+→Ωc0K+) = 7.09%等。

Conclusion: 该理论预测为LHCb等实验探测Ξcc+衰变提供了关键理论支持和优先测量道建议。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of the double-charmed baryon $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$ by the LHCb Collaboration, we systematically investigate the two-body nonleptonic decays of the $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$ into a single-charmed baryon and a light pseudoscalar meson. In our calculation, both factorizable and nonfactorizable decay amplitudes are evaluated within the framework of nonrelativistic quark model combined with the baryon wave functions dynamically determined by numerically solving the three-body Schrödinger equation with a nonrelativistic potential model. In particular, the nonfactorizable $W$-exchange amplitudes are estimated under pole model by considering $J^{P}=1/2^{\pm}$ low-lying intermediate charmed-baryon poles, and our results show that these contributions are comparable to or even dominate the factorizable amplitudes in several decay channels. Moreover, assuming a lifetime of $τ_{Ξ_{cc}^{+}}=47~\text{fs}$, we predict relatively large branching fractions for several CF decays, including $\mathcal{B}(Ξ_{cc}^{+}\toΞ_{c}^{\prime0}π^{+})=8.90\%$, $\mathcal{B}(Ξ_{cc}^{+}\toΩ_{c}^{0}K^{+})=7.09\%$, $\mathcal{B}(Ξ_{cc}^{+}\toΣ_{c}^{+}\bar{K}^{0})=6.92\%$, $\mathcal{B}(Ξ_{cc}^{+}\toΞ_{c}^{+}π^{0})=5.28\%$, and $\mathcal{B}(Ξ_{cc}^{+}\toΣ_{c}^{++}K^{-})=5.17\%$. These channels are promising candidates for future experimental searches at the LHCb.

</details>


### [116] [Determining the Threshold Excess in Top-Quark Pair Production through a Global PDF Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21479)
*Richard D. Ball,Jaco ter Hoeve*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过ATLAS和CMS在8和13 TeV下的tt̄微分截面测量数据，结合NNLO QCD理论预测（含NLO电弱修正与高阶不确定性估计），对顶夸克对产生中的阈值超出Δ进行了经验性测定，并全面考虑了PDF、顶夸克质量mt和强耦合常数αs(mZ)之间的相关不确定性。结果表明：8 TeV下无显著阈值超出；13 TeV下联合所有测量得到约2σ以上的超出；且Δ与mt存在强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定顶夸克对产生过程中的阈值超出Δ，以检验标准模型预测并探索可能的新物理信号，同时需严谨处理参数间相关不确定性。

Method: 利用ATLAS和CMS在8和13 TeV的tt̄微分截面数据，结合NNLO QCD + NLO EW理论预言，采用包含PDF、mt和αs(mZ)相关不确定性的全局拟合方法提取Δ。

Result: 8 TeV未见显著阈值超出；13 TeV联合分析显示超出NNLO QCD背景约2.1σ；发现Δ与mt存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 当前数据在13 TeV暗示可能存在阈值超出，但尚不足以确认新物理；Δ与mt的强关联性表明未来精度测量必须联合约束二者以避免系统偏差。

Abstract: We present an empirical determination of the threshold excess $Δ$ in top-quark pair production, taking full account of correlated uncertainties with PDFs, the top-quark mass $m_t$ and the strong coupling $α_s(m_Z)$. We extract $Δ$ from a variety of differential $t\bar{t}$ cross-section measurements by ATLAS and CMS at 8 and 13 TeV, using theoretical predictions of the $t\bar{t}$ continuum up to NNLO QCD accuracy, including an estimate of missing higher order uncertainties and NLO EW corrections. At 8 TeV we see no evidence of a significant threshold excess, but at 13 TeV obtain results above the NNLO QCD $t\bar{t}$ background at just over two $σ$ when we combine all the available measurements. We also find evidence of a strong correlation between $Δ$ and $m_t$, which must be taken into account if the uncertainty on any threshold excess is to be estimated reliably.

</details>


### [117] [Revisiting $B_c^{(*)}$ production in $Z$-boson decays at NLO in QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21485)
*Qi-Wei Ke,Lei Guo,Jun Zeng,Xu-Chang Zheng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD色单态框架下，计算了Z玻色子衰变中Bc(*)介子（包括1S和2S赝标量与矢量态）产生的下领阶（NLO）QCD修正，给出了产生宽度、能量分数及不变质量分布，并分析了理论不确定性与重整化尺度选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对径向激发Bc介子在Z玻色子衰变中产生的唯象研究，提供高统计Z工厂实验所需的产生率与运动学输入。

Method: 在色单态NRQCD框架下，采用下领阶QCD微扰计算，涵盖底夸克与粲夸克产生拓扑，并应用最大共形原理（PMC）确定重整化尺度。

Result: Bc和Bc*的K因子分别为1.52和1.33；NLO修正增强小至中等能量分数区域，并改变不变质量谱；系统考察了重整化尺度、重夸克质量及原点波函数带来的不确定性。

Conclusion: NLO修正显著提升理论预言精度，结果可为未来Z工厂上Bc及其径向激发态的研究提供关键理论支持。

Abstract: We study $B_c^{(*)}$ production in $Z$-boson decays at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD within the color-singlet nonrelativistic QCD framework. The analysis covers both the $1S$ and $2S$ pseudoscalar and vector states, extending the phenomenological study to radially excited $B_c$ mesons. We calculate direct production widths and energy-fraction and invariant-mass distributions, including both charm- and bottom-quark production topologies. For the central inputs and $μ_R=2m_b$, the $K$ factors are $1.52$ for $B_c$ and $1.33$ for $B_c^*$. We examine uncertainties from the renormalization scale, heavy-quark masses, and wave functions at the origin, and apply the principle of maximum conformality to fix the renormalization scale from the $β_0$-dependent NLO contribution. The NLO corrections enhance the small- and intermediate-energy-fraction regions and modify the invariant-mass spectra. We discuss these features in relation to heavy-quark fragmentation. The results provide production-rate and kinematic inputs for studies of ground-state and radially excited $B_c$ mesons at high-statistics $Z$ factories.

</details>


### [118] [Hidden-charm and hidden-beauty hybrid mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21490)
*Raghavendra Kaushal,Bhaghyesh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用非相对论势模型计算了多种混合粲偶素和底偶素态的质量谱，通过拟合格点QCD得到的混合势与夸克偶素势之差来确定胶子激发势，并发现若干实验观测到的粒子与理论预测的混合态质量及量子数相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释实验中观测到的高能区新强子态（如Ψ(4230)、Υ(10753)等）是否为混合态，从而检验QCD中胶子自由度的物理表现。

Method: 采用非相对论势模型，将混合势建模为夸克偶素势与胶子激发势的线性组合；胶子激发势由格点QCD给出的势差拟合得到；求解薛定谔方程获得各混合态质量谱。

Result: 计算得到Π_u、Σ_u^-、Π_g、Δ_g、Σ_g^-、Δ_u、Σ_u^+等混合粲偶素和底偶素的质量谱；Ψ(4230)、Ψ(4500)、X(4500)、Ψ(4660)及Υ(10753)、Υ(11050)的实验质量与J^{PC}与模型预测的混合态一致。

Conclusion: 这些实验粒子很可能是混合粲偶素或底偶素态，支持胶子在强子内部起实质性结构作用的观点。

Abstract: The mass spectra of $Π_u$, $Σ_u^-$, $Π_g$, $Δ_g$, $Σ_g^-$, $Δ_u$, and $Σ_u^+$ hybrid charmonium and hybrid bottomonium states are computed using a non-relativistic potential model. The hybrid potential is modelled as a linear combination of quarkonium potential and gluonic excited potential. The gluonic excited potential is computed by fitting the potential difference between hybrid potential and quarkonium potential obtained from the lattice quantum chromodynamics. Experimental masses and $J^{PC}$ values of $Ψ(4230)$, $Ψ(4500)$, $X(4500)$, and $Ψ(4660)$ are compatible with hybrid charmonium states, and $Υ(10753)$ and $Υ(11050)$ are compatible with hybrid bottomonium states obtained from our model, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Invertible Froggatt-Nielsen Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21688)
*Hajime Otsuka,Ryo Yokokura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了反常味对称性的非可逆完成及其在狄拉克中微子质量中的应用，提出了一种通过非可逆缺陷实现Froggatt-Nielsen机制的新框架，并利用瞬子效应解释中微子质量的指数压制与等级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准Froggatt-Nielsen机制中U(1)味对称性需精细破缺以生成中微子小质量的问题，探索由反常和拓扑结构驱动的更自然、非可逆的对称性实现方式。

Method: 构建带混合反常的有限非阿贝尔/模味对称性缺陷，结合红外单极退耦极限与紫外SU(2)_H隐藏规范理论中的约束瞬子效应；在FN对称性被瞬子显式破缺后，对重费米子积分得到中微子Yukawa矩阵。

Result: 导出中微子Yukawa耦合形式(Y_ν)_{ij} ∝ ζ_H ε^{ℓ_i + n_j}，其中ζ_H = exp[−8π²/g_H²(v_H)]提供指数压制，ε的幂次由FN电荷决定；成功再现中微子质量比m₂/m₃ ∼ ε（倾向正规序），并同时解释夸克与带电轻子的等级结构。

Conclusion: 非可逆味对称性完成是实现狄拉克中微子质量的有效新范式，将对称性破缺、拓扑效应与等级起源统一于一个自洽的量子场论框架中。

Abstract: We investigate non-invertible completions of anomalous flavor symmetries and their application to Dirac neutrino masses. We formulate symmetry defects dressed by topological quantum field theories for flavor symmetries including finite non-Abelian flavor transformations with rational anomaly characters and modular flavor symmetries with the rational part of modular anomaly cocycles, for gauge sectors with the required exact higher-form symmetry. As a concrete example, we construct a Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) model in which an order-four subgroup $\mathbb Z_4^{\rm FN}\subset U(1)_{\rm FN}$ is completed into a non-invertible defect through its mixed anomaly with an intermediate hidden $U(1)_H$ gauge sector in the infrared monopole-decoupling limit. The non-invertible FN symmetry can be explicitly broken at the energy scale where monopoles become dynamical. This breaking from the infrared viewpoint can be realized in an ultraviolet hidden $SU(2)_H$ theory as instanton effects, where a constrained instanton with topological charge one generates a fermion mixing term that violates FN charge by one unit. Its coefficient contains the nonperturbative factor $ζ_H\sim\exp[-8π^2/g_H^2(v_H)]$. When independent FN-breaking terms are subleading, integrating out the heavy fermions gives $(Y_ν)_{ij}\proptoζ_Hε^{\ell_i+n_j}$ in a rotated basis for the right-handed neutrinos, to leading order in the instanton-induced fermion mixing. The nonperturbative factor provides the exponential suppression of the neutrino Yukawa couplings, while the FN charges determine the powers of $ε$. For the chosen FN charges and generic order-one coefficients, $m_2/m_3\simε$, favoring normal ordering. An independent $\mathbb Z_8^D$ gauge symmetry forbids Majorana masses. The same FN charge assignment reproduces the leading quark and charged-lepton Yukawa hierarchies.

</details>


### [120] [Nuclear PDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21697)
*K. Greve,T. Ježo,M. Klasen,A. Kusina,F. I. Olness,J. Wissmann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了当前模型无关核部分子分布函数（nPDFs）全局分析的现状，强调在LHC重离子碰撞时代需纳入多样、大规模实验数据与最新理论计算，并合理评估不确定性，尤其关注质量数A依赖性的物理意义与拟合质量评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LHC重离子碰撞实验的发展，亟需更精准、模型无关的核部分子分布函数（nPDFs），以提取核介质中QCD效应的新信息；同时需避免参数化偏差等人为约束，真实反映已知与未知知识边界。

Method: 系统比较多个研究组的nPDF全局拟合结果，分析χ²/DOF、各实验χ²分布、不确定性形状、数据敏感性等拟合质量指标，并重点考察质量数A依赖性的建模与物理诠释。

Result: 指出当前nPDF拟合中A依赖性长期被弱化为纯函数工具，而LHC时代要求其成为具有明确物理含义的关键自由度；强调核不确定性常是理论预测主导误差源，应通过多样化数据与无偏参数化予以合理刻画。

Conclusion: 高质量nPDF全局分析必须兼顾数据广度、理论精度与不确定性诚实性；A依赖性需从功能角色提升为物理可观测量，相关拟合质量评估体系也应相应完善。

Abstract: We summarize the current status of global analyses of model-independent nuclear parton distri- bution functions (nPDFs). A carefully conducted global analysis of nPDFs in the era of heavy-ion collisions at the LHC is key to gaining access to new information through the data measured by the collider experiments. This implies incorporating as large and diverse a dataset as possible, as well as the latest theoretical calculations, in order to capture a faithful representation of nuclear structure that reflects the current state of knowledge. On the other hand, the uncertainties in par- ticular must reflect what is not known. For many observables, nuclear uncertainties represent the most significant source of error in current theoretical models; nevertheless, they should not, where possible, be artificially constrained (for example, through parameterization bias). The quality of a global (n)PDF fit is often measured by the $χ^2$ per degree of freedom, which is approximately one for a fit that describes the data precisely without overfitting. Many additional aspects allow to characterize a good fit, e.g. the $χ^2$ distribution per experiment, the shape of the uncertainties, and the sensitivity to different types of data. We inspect various fit quality indicators for fits obtained by different groups. Morever we pay close attention to the $A$-dependence. Traditionally, the A-dependence had a primarily functional role in global nPDF analyses and comparatively less attention has been paid to it when characterizing a fit. However, we have now entered the era of nuclei collisions at the LHC and need to investigate the $A$-dependence properly.

</details>


### [121] [Multi-Higgs production from a theory of varying Higgs quartic and top Yukawa couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21751)
*Ulf Danielsson,Rikard Enberg,Gunnar Ingelman,Soumyadip Kundu,Tanumoy Mandal,Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了希格斯四次自耦合和顶夸克Yukawa耦合作为动力学产生的可变耦合的对撞机现象学，通过两个标量场φ₁和φ₂实现；根据标量质量与两倍顶夸克质量的关系，其衰变主导为hh或tt̄，产生显著的共振hh和背景压制的hhh信号；利用ATLAS数据设定了TeV尺度的新物理有效能标Λ₁、Λ₂的限制，并指出HL-LHC可通过多通道联合测量进一步检验电弱扇区的动力学起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究电弱扇区（特别是希格斯四次耦合和顶夸克Yukawa耦合）是否具有动力学起源，而非基本参数。

Method: 构建含两个新标量场φ₁、φ₂的理论模型，使其动态生成可变的Higgs四次耦合与top Yukawa耦合；分析其在LHC上的衰变模式（hh或tt̄主导）、共振产生截面及关联产生hhh过程；通过重铸ATLAS共振搜寻结果设定Λ₁、Λ₂约束；评估HL-LHC在多通道（hh、tt̄、多Higgs）下的探测能力。

Result: 发现显著的共振hh产生截面和背景压制的hhh关联产生信号；ATLAS现有数据已对有效新物理能标Λ₁、Λ₂给出TeV量级限制；HL-LHC有望通过hh、tt̄与多Higgs通道联合测量进一步覆盖该参数空间。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了可检验的电弱扇区动力学起源方案，其多通道信号特征使LHC/HL-LHC成为检验该思想的关键平台。

Abstract: We study the collider phenomenology of a theory in which the Higgs quartic self-coupling and the top Yukawa coupling are \textit{varying couplings}, which arise dynamically from two scalar fields, $φ_1$ and $φ_2$. Depending on the scalar masses relative to twice the top-quark mass, the heavy scalars decay mainly to Higgs boson pairs ($hh$) or to top-quark pairs ($t\bar t$), giving two distinct signatures. We find sizeable cross sections for resonant $hh$ production, and also for the background-suppressed process with three Higgs bosons ($hhh$) from associated production, $pp \to hφ_i \to hhh$. By recasting ATLAS resonance searches, we set bounds on the effective new-physics scales $Λ_1$ and $Λ_2$ at the TeV scale. We also show that the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will probe this parameter space further, through combined measurements of the $hh$, $t\bar t$ and multi-Higgs channels. This gives a strong, multi-channel test of whether the electroweak sector has a dynamical origin.

</details>


### [122] [From Non-Thermal to Thermal: Cosmological Imprints of Self-Interacting Secluded Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21817)
*Koushik Dutta,Deep Ghosh,Ahamadullah Khan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过自相互作用实现热化的暗物质（DM）模型，重点关注由暴胀子衰变产生的实标量DM场的热化过程，并分析其对宇宙学约束和DM质量下限的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质与标准模型隔离，但可通过自相互作用实现内部热化；探究其热化机制及对宇宙学观测（如大爆炸核合成、自由流长度）的影响。

Method: 数值求解包含2→2弹性自散射的DM动量分布Boltzmann方程，考虑由暴胀子相干振荡衰变产生的初始DM分布，无粒子数改变过程。

Result: 发现DM温度可高于或低于标准模型温度（取决于暴胀子质量与再热温度相对大小），对应负或正化学势；热化显著抑制共动自由流长度，使DM质量下限比非热情形减弱约40%；给出特定参数下的DM质量下限：再热温度5×10⁶ GeV时为2.2×10⁻¹⁰ GeV，5 MeV时为0.47 GeV。

Conclusion: 自相互作用驱动的热化可显著改变暗物质的热历史与结构形成性质，放宽质量下限，为轻质量暗物质提供可行理论框架，且不违背现有宇宙学约束。

Abstract: Dark matter (DM), secluded from the Standard Model (SM), can attain internal thermalization through self-interactions. We investigate the thermalization of a real scalar DM field with a quartic self-coupling, initially produced from the perturbative decay of the coherently oscillating inflaton field during reheating. The thermalization process has been studied numerically by solving Boltzmann equation of the DM momentum distribution function in presence of $2\rightarrow2$ DM elastic self-scatterings. In the absence of number-changing processes, the thermalized dark sector is characterized by a temperature and a nonzero chemical potential, determined by the inflaton mass and the reheat temperature. We find that if the inflaton mass is greater (smaller) than the reheat temperature, the DM temperature can be greater (smaller) than the SM temperature during Big Bang Nucleosynthesis, without violating any cosmological constraints. This is realized with a negative (positive) chemical potential for the DM temperature greater (smaller) than the SM one. Depending on DM production history, we can achieve the lower limit on the DM mass much below than in warm DM scenarios. The thermalization via self-scatterings tends to suppress the DM co-moving free-streaming length, compared to the non-thermal scenario without any collisions. Consequently, the lower limit on the DM mass weakens approximately by $40\%$ for a suitable choice of quartic self-coupling than in the non-thermal scenario. We obtain the lower limits on the thermal DM mass to be $2.2\times10^{-10}$ GeV and $0.47$ GeV for reheat temperatures, $5\times 10^6$ GeV and $5$ MeV respectively, while the inflaton mass is set at $10^3$ GeV.

</details>


### [123] [Decay modeling in 4top production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21813)
*Manal Alsairafi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供了在13.6 TeV质心能量下，LHC上pp→tt̄tt̄+X过程在四轻子衰变道的NLO QCD理论预言，并对比了固定阶NLO计算与MC@NLO、POWHEG等部分子喷注匹配方法的结果，特别关注自旋关联和衰变中矩阵元修正的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四顶夸克产生是检验标准模型及新物理的重要探针，其高精度理论预言对实验测量至关重要；而自旋关联与衰变处理方式显著影响理论预测精度。

Method: 采用固定阶NLO QCD计算，并与MC@NLO和POWHEG两种部分子喷注匹配方法进行对比；MC@NLO严格保持全部自旋关联，POWHEG结合Pythia模拟衰变并近似处理自旋关联；进一步分析了在衰变中引入矩阵元修正的影响。

Result: 给出了13.6 TeV下四顶夸克产生在四轻子末态的积分截面与微分分布的NLO QCD预言；明确了不同匹配方案（尤其是衰变中自旋关联与矩阵元修正）对结果的影响程度。

Conclusion: 固定阶与匹配方法在关键可观测量上存在可分辨差异，强调在精确测量中需谨慎选择衰变建模方式；矩阵元修正对部分微分分布有明显影响，支持在高精度分析中纳入此类修正。

Abstract: We present NLO QCD predictions for the $pp\to t\bar{t}t\bar{t}+X$ process in the 4 lepton decay channel at the integrated and differential level for the LHC centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~TeV. Specifically, we compare the fixed order NLO QCD calculations to the parton-shower matched results obtained with \textsc{MC@NLO} and \textsc{Powheg} matching. In the former, NLO QCD corrections are consistently included in the production and decay of the four top quarks, preserving all spin correlations. In the latter, higher order effects in top-quark decays with approximate spin correlations are simulated in the parton showers, using Pythia. Additionally the impact of including matrix element corrections in the top quark decays in the parton-shower matched predictions is analysed.

</details>


### [124] [Modified hydrogen recombination rate due to non-Hubble redshift effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21992)
*I. V Krasnov,N. S. Nedelko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了宇宙再复合时期的计算方法，提出Lyman-alpha光子与运动中中性氢原子的相互作用可能引入对标准ΛCDM模型的重要修正，并通过CMB数据分析发现该修正倾向于支持非哈勃红移效应，同时给出更高的H₀、更小的Ωₘ、S₈和nₛ值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来不同观测手段得出的宇宙学参数存在显著分歧，亟需修正理论模型以解决该‘危机’。

Method: 重新考察标准再复合计算，引入Lyman-alpha光子与运动中性氢原子相互作用的动力学机制，并将其纳入CMB数据拟合分析。

Result: CMB数据分析显示，修正模型偏好非哈勃红移效应，且给出H₀略高、Ωₘ和S₈略低、nₛ更小的结果。

Conclusion: Lyman-alpha辐射输运中的运动学修正可能缓解当前宇宙学参数张力，为ΛCDM模型提供可行的物理拓展方向。

Abstract: The accumulation of observational data in recent years has led to a peculiar situation in which measurements of the cosmological parameters made by various collaborations and using different methods yield significantly different values. A solution to this problem may be found by modifying the models used to obtain these values. In this work, we revisit the standard approach to the recombination calculation and investigate a mechanism that can introduce corrections to the rate at which Lyman-alpha photons escape the resonance line. We find that interactions of Lyman-alpha line photons with moving neutral hydrogen atoms can become a source of such a correction. An analysis of CMB data for the modified model favors the existence of a non-Hubble redshift effect and prefers slightly higher values of $H_0$ and smaller values of $Ω_m$ and $S_8$ compared to the standard $Λ$CDM model, as well as a smaller value of the scalar spectrum index $n_s$.

</details>


### [125] [Low-Scale See-Saw Mechanism and the Majorana Nature of Heavy Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21886)
*Takuya Okawa,S. T. Petcov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在e⁺-e⁻和μ⁺-μ⁻对撞机实验中，利用前向-后向角分布对称性来判定低能标I型跷跷板机制中重马约拉纳中微子本性的可行性，并指出FCC-ee第一阶段运行有望发现质量低于Z⁰玻色子的重中性轻子并验证其马约拉纳性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定低尺度I型跷跷板机制中重马约拉纳中微子的马约拉纳本性，以验证中微子质量起源及轻子数破坏机制。

Method: 基于两类对称性保护的双右手中微子跷跷板模型及一类三右手中微子实现，利用e⁺-e⁻和μ⁺-μ⁻对撞中重中性轻子产生过程的前向-后向角分布对称性作为马约拉纳本性的判据。

Result: FCC-ee第一阶段运行具备显著潜力：不仅能发现质量低于Z⁰玻色子质量的重中性轻子，还能通过角分布对称性探测量子数破坏并提供其马约拉纳本性的实验证据。

Conclusion: 前向-后向角分布对称性是探测重中性轻子马约拉纳本性的有效实验判据，FCC-ee等未来正负电子对撞机在检验低能标跷跷板机制方面具有关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the possibility to determine the Majorana nature of the heavy Majorana neutrinos present in the low-scale type I seesaw mechanism of neutrino mass generation in experiments at $e^+ \text{-} e^-$ and $μ^+ \text{-} μ^-$ colliders. This is done on the examples of two specific symmetry-protected seesaw scenarios with two right-handed (RH) singlet neutrinos and one seesaw realization with three RH neutrinos. We use the forward-backward symmetry in the angular distribution of the heavy neutral leptons produced in the $e^+ \text{-} e^-$ and $μ^+ \text{-} μ^-$ collisions as an indicator of their Majorana nature. Our results indicate that the experiments at FCCee during its first phase of operation have a remarkable potential not only to discover the heavy neutral leptons associated with the type I seesaw mechanism if they have masses smaller than the $Z^0$-boson mass, but also to probe and provide evidence for their Majorana nature.

</details>


### [126] [From Axion Detection to Dark Matter Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22105)
*Jae Hyeok Chang,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用全球轴子探测器网络，将轴子发现转化为对暗物质子结构和瞬变现象的天文学研究，通过干涉相位、空间相干性和光谱分析来测量轴子子结构的流方向和速度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将轴子探测从单纯发现提升为暗物质天文学研究，探索轴子子结构（如潮汐流、微团簇、轴子星等）的形成与演化。

Method: 结合干涉测量相位、空间相干性与光谱分析，利用四站点网络联合测量纵向和横向速度弥散，并结合动力学与种群模型进行解释。

Result: 可实现对轴子潮汐流速度分布的联合测量，进而检验不同轴子产生机制；方法可拓展至微团簇、轴子星及爆发信号，扩大实验室探测的天体物理覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 全球轴子探测器网络不仅可用于轴子发现，更可发展为研究暗物质子结构及其天体物理演化的新型天文观测平台。

Abstract: A global haloscope network could turn an axion discovery into astronomy of dark matter substructure and transients. We combine interferometric phase, spatial coherence, and spectroscopy to study the formation and evolution of axion substructure through measurements of flow directions and velocity distributions. For tidal streams, a four-site forecast shows how longitudinal and transverse velocity dispersions can be measured jointly. Interpreted with dynamical and population models, these measurements could test axion production scenarios. Extensions to miniclusters, axion stars, and emitted bursts would broaden the astrophysical reach of laboratory detection.

</details>


### [127] [Lossy Lorentz boosts at ultra-relativistic energies: Numerical study and source survey of high-energy physics software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21897)
*Christian Bierlich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文调查了高能物理软件中洛伦兹变换实现的数值精度问题，指出信息丢失（如仅用速度参数或笛卡尔坐标表示）是主要误差来源，并提出使用更丰富的输入和光锥坐标来改善精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理软件中广泛使用的洛伦兹变换在大boost下会导致四维矢量不变质量等物理量失准，亟需系统评估与改进。

Method: 对主流对撞机和空气簇射软件中的洛伦兹变换实现进行调研，并通过三种数值测试比较代表性算法；分析误差来源，区分表示法信息丢失与计算公式本身的问题。

Result: 发现主要误差源于boost参数化（仅用速度）和结果存储（仅用笛卡尔分量）导致的信息丢失；采用更丰富的boost输入（如完整洛伦兹矩阵）和保留光锥坐标可显著提升精度；双精度下当前对撞机硬过程安全，但低质量系统、极高boost辅助参考系、单精度粒子记录及空气簇射模拟等场景存在风险。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹变换的数值稳健性依赖于表示方式而非单纯算术公式；推荐在关键应用中采用信息更完整的boost参数化与坐标表示，尤其在极端尺度比或超高精度需求场景中。

Abstract: Most high-energy-physics software packages implement Lorentz boosts for themselves. At sufficiently large boosts, these routines can return finite, plausible-looking four-vectors whose invariant masses and rest-frame properties are no longer accurate. We survey implementations in widely used collider and air-shower software and compare representative algorithms in three numerical tests.
  We find that the main problem is usually information discarded by the representation, rather than the arithmetic of the boost formula. Information can be lost when a boost is specified only by its velocity, and again when the result is stored only through Cartesian components. Richer boost input prevents the first loss, while retaining light-cone coordinates prevents the second. Ordinary hard processes at present colliders are safe in double precision; the relevant regimes are low-mass systems, highly boosted auxiliary frames, single-precision particle records and air-shower simulations. Extreme scale hierarchies in parton-shower accuracy tests and ultra-heavy particle decays provide still more demanding cases.

</details>


### [128] [Probing the Axion--Photon--Dark Photon Interaction with LHC Mono-Photons and Fermi-LAT Gamma-Ray Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21989)
*Chuan-Ren Chen,Takaaki Nomura,Van Que Tran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子-光子-暗光子相互作用，在LHC和伽马射线谱线搜寻中给出新的限制；利用CMS单光子数据，得到比LEP更优的95%置信度排除限，并结合Fermi-LAT伽马线数据，全面排除该基准下热遗迹轴子作为暗物质的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轴子作为暗物质候选者时，其与标准模型粒子（特别是光子和暗光子）的耦合效应，填补LHC与天基观测在参数空间上的互补约束空白。

Method: 通过重分析CMS单光子末态实验数据（recasting），推导轴子-暗光子-光子耦合常数gaγ'γ的95%置信度排除限；同时结合Fermi-LAT伽马射线谱线观测，分析轴子s波湮灭到双光子过程的限制。

Result: 获得目前最强的LHC对gaγ'γ的限制（mγ' ≲ 100 GeV时达~4.8×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹），较LEP提升3倍以上；HL-LHC预期再提升~2.7倍；当ma ≳ 160 GeV且mγ'=300 GeV时，Fermi-LAT约束强于LHC；二者联合完全排除该模型中热遗迹轴子作为暗物质的可能。

Conclusion: 轴子-光子-暗光子相互作用在高能对撞机与天基伽马观测中具有强互补性；当前实验已足以排除所考虑质量范围内作为热遗迹暗物质的轴子模型。

Abstract: We investigate the axion--photon--dark photon interaction at the LHC and in gamma-ray line searches. By recasting the CMS mono-photon data, we derive a $95\%$ CL exclusion reaching $g_{aγ'γ}\simeq4.8\times10^{-4}\ {\rm GeV}^{-1}$ for dark photon masses $m_{γ'}\lesssim100$ GeV, with sensitivity extending to $m_{γ'}\simeq2.3$ TeV, where the effective theory approaches its validity limit. This improves upon the existing LEP mono-photon constraint by a factor of $3$ or more, depending on the dark photon mass. The projected HL-LHC sensitivity improves by a further factor of $\sim2.7$ and extends the reach to $m_{γ'}\simeq3$ TeV. The same interaction also induces $s$-wave annihilation of axions into $γγ$. If the axion constitutes dark matter, Fermi-LAT gamma-ray line data become more constraining than the LHC for $m_a\gtrsim160$ GeV at $m_{γ'}=300$ GeV, where the on-shell collider signal is kinematically suppressed. Combining collider and gamma-ray line constraints excludes a thermal relic axion across the axion mass range considered for this benchmark.

</details>


### [129] [DUNE Beam-Energy Dependence of Sensitivity to the Violation of the Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22045)
*Monjowara Khatun,Debajyoti Dutta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究DUNE实验在不同束流能量配置（低能LE与中能ME）下对等效原理可能破缺（VEP）的灵敏度，发现LE配置对VEP参数ΦΔγ₂₁和ΦΔγ₃₁给出更强的3σ限制（约8.5×10⁻²⁴），且消失道比出现道具有更平滑、更稳健的排除能力；同时指出正负中微子模式可独立探查VEP，强调束流能量优化与通道分析的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子振荡实验（如DUNE）对等效原理破缺（VEP）的检验能力，并比较不同束流能量配置（LE vs ME）对VEP参数约束效果的差异。

Method: 基于DUNE实验模拟，对比分析低能（LE）和中能（ME）束流配置下，中微子出现与消失道对VEP参数ΦΔγ₂₁和ΦΔγ₃₁的约束能力，考虑θ₂₃=43°的真实取值及正负中微子运行模式。

Result: LE配置在3σ置信水平下对ΦΔγ₂₁和ΦΔγ₃₁的限制分别为±8.47×10⁻²⁴和±8.53×10⁻²⁴；消失道比出现道具有更优且更少相位简并的VEP敏感度；正负中微子模式均可独立探测VEP，其相对灵敏度依赖于束流能量配置。

Conclusion: DUNE的低能束流配置在约束VEP参数方面优于中能配置，且通道选择与运行模式（ν/ν̄）对VEP探测至关重要，凸显束流能量优化与精细化通道分析的必要性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the dependence of DUNE's sensitivity to possible violations of the equivalence principle (VEP) on the beam-energy configuration, comparing the Low-Energy (LE) and Medium-Energy (ME) beams. We find that the LE configuration generally provides stronger constraints on the VEP parameters than the ME configuration. For $θ_{23}=43^\circ$, the LE configuration constrains $ΦΔγ_{21}$ and $ΦΔγ_{31}$ to $8.47\times10^{-24}$ and $8.53\times10^{-24}$, respectively, at the $3σ$ CL. The appearance channels shows stronger phase-dependency degeneracies while the disappearance channels exhibits smoother exclusion sensitivity. This work further confirms the advantage of the LE configuration over the ME, in constraining the correlated VEP parameter space. For the assumed true value of $θ_{23}=43^\circ$, The LE configuration restricts the VEP parameter space to $\mathitΦΔγ_{21}\in[-8.47,\,8.47]\times10^{-24}$ and $\mathitΦΔγ_{31}\in[-8.53,\,8.53]\times10^{-24}$ at $3σ$.We also find that neutrino-mode and antineutrino-mode running can independently probe VEP effects at DUNE, with the relative sensitivity depending on the beam-energy configuration. These results highlight the importance of beam-energy optimization and channel-wise analysis in exploring possible violations of the equivalence principle at DUNE.

</details>


### [130] [Spurious Singularities in Dipole Subtraction Cross Sections and their Damping with Convergence Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22102)
*Michael H. Seymour,Andrzej Siódmok,James Whitehead*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在偶极子减除算法中引入收敛因子的方法，以抑制减除截面中由底层Born矩阵元引起的虚假奇点，从而改善统计收敛性并消除对人为调节参数（如生成截断）的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极子减除算法中减除截面存在真实截面所没有的虚假奇点，这在偶极子蒙特卡洛演化和匹配算法等应用中会导致统计收敛差或对人为调节参数（如技术截断或生成截断）产生依赖。

Method: 在减除截面中引入额外的收敛因子以压制虚假奇点，并通过相空间积分推导出修正后的偶极子插入算符。

Result: 数值验证表明，所提出的收敛因子能有效消除因虚假奇点导致的对生成截断的对数依赖，显著改善统计收敛性。

Conclusion: 引入收敛因子是处理偶极子减除中虚假奇点的有效方法，提升了偶极子蒙特卡洛演化与匹配算法的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: In the dipole subtraction algorithm, the real-virtual cancellation in NLO calculations of arbitrary observables is implemented by defining a subtraction cross section that matches all of the singularities of the real emission cross section. The converse, however, is not true: the subtraction cross section contains additional singularities, coming from the underlying Born matrix element, that the real cross section does not. We call these spurious singularities. If used as originally proposed, this is not a problem, because the value of the observable damps these singularities, but in applications such as dipole showers and matching algorithms, it can lead to poor statistical convergence, or dependence on unphysical regulators such as technical or generation cuts. In this paper, we show that additional factors, which we call convergence factors, can be applied to the subtraction cross section to damp these spurious singularities and improve the convergence. We perform the required phase-space integrals to yield modified dipole insertion operators, and verify numerically that they indeed remove the logarithmic dependence on generation cuts that arises in their absence.
  This paper is dedicated to the memory of Stefano Catani, with whom the work was originally started.

</details>
