### [131] [JWST Observations of RR Lyrae in Leo P](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20692)
*Catherine M. Slaughter,Alessandro Savino,Meredith Durbin,Andrew E. Dolphin,Kristen B. W. McQuinn,Max J. B. Newman,Evan D. Skillman,Daniel R. Weisz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用HST/ACS和JWST/NIRCam对矮星系Leo P中RR Lyrae变星进行联合时序测光分析，校准了适用于JWST的周期-韦森海特-金属丰度（PWZ）关系，并给出与Gaia一致的距离测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索JWST在金属丰度极低环境下研究RR Lyrae变星的能力，并借助已知的HST关系建立更精确的JWST PWZ校准。

Method: 对Leo P中HST和JWST观测到的RR Lyrae变星进行时序光变分析，测定其周期、振幅和平均星等；分别拟合HST和JWST数据的PWZ关系，并结合WLM的JWST数据做联合拟合。

Result: 识别出20颗HST和15颗JWST RR Lyrae；获得Leo P的RR Lyrae距离模数μ=26.04±0.07（1.61±0.06 Mpc）；联合拟合使PWZ斜率和截距的平均分数不确定度分别降低31%和11%。

Conclusion: JWST可用于高精度金属丰度相关的RR Lyrae距离测量，联合多星系JWST数据可显著提升PWZ关系的校准精度。

Abstract: We analyze co-spatial HST/ACS and JWST/NIRCam time-series photometric observations of RR Lyrae in the low-metallicity dwarf galaxy Leo P. This study provides a new opportunity to explore the properties of and potential for metal-poor RR Lyrae studies with JWST using well-understood relationships from past HST work. We identify 20 RR Lyrae in the HST imaging and 15 RR Lyrae in the JWST imaging. We determine the periods, amplitudes, and mean magnitudes for each of the RR Lyrae. We fit the Period-Wesenheit-Metallicity (PWZ) function to the HST RR Lyrae population, and obtain a Gaia-consistent RR Lyrae-based distance to Leo P of $μ= 26.04\pm0.07$ ($1.61\pm0.06$ Mpc), consistent with existing distance measurements. We determine a JWST-based PWZ using Leo P data along with previous JWST RR Lyrae observations in WLM. The simultaneous fit decreases the mean fractional uncertainty by 31% on the slope and by 11% on the intercept, increasing the constraining power as compared to the existing calibration.

</details>


### [132] [The Impact of High-Mass Clusters on the Binary Black Hole Merger Rate over Cosmic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20698)
*Aidan Mai,Kyle Kremer,Fulya Kıroğlu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用CMC模型网格，首次将N=10^7粒子的CMC模型纳入致密星团中双黑洞并合率的宇宙学计算，发现本地星团双黑洞并合率主要由低质量、高金属丰度、近期形成的星团主导；层级并合（二代及以上）主要发生在高质量星团中，但其整体贡献受星团初始质量函数指数截断抑制，因此层级并合比例对Schechter质量MS高度敏感，可借此用引力波观测独立约束星团初始质量函数的高质量端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密星团中双黑洞并合的宇宙学起源及其对引力波事件的贡献，特别是层级并合的比例如何反映星团初始质量函数的特征。

Method: 采用CMC（Cluster Monte Carlo）模型网格覆盖观测到的星团质量、金属丰度和维里半径全参数空间，并引入N=10^7粒子的高精度模型（含新太阳金属丰度模型）；结合解析星团形成模型，将星团形成率与模型得到的延迟时间分布进行卷积计算合并率。

Result: 本地星团双黑洞并合率以低质量（M1≲20 M⊙）、高金属丰度、近期形成的星团为主；高质量星团主导层级并合，但其总贡献受限于星团初始质量函数的指数截断；Schechter质量MS=10^7 M⊙时层级并合占比27%，优于经典值MS=10^{6.26} M⊙对应的18%；模型自然再现了室女座星系团球状星团质量分布和当前数密度。

Conclusion: 层级双黑洞并合率对星团初始质量函数的高质端极为敏感，引力波观测中层级并合比例可作为独立探针约束MS；本工作提供的并合权重数据已公开，可用于与LVK观测分布及其他形成模型直接比对。

Abstract: We present a cosmological calculation of the binary black hole merger rate from dense star clusters, using a $\texttt{Cluster Monte Carlo}$ ($\texttt{CMC}$) model grid spanning the full observed parameter space of cluster mass, metallicity, and virial radius. For the first time, we incorporate $N=10^{7}$-particle $\texttt{CMC}$ models into this calculation, including a new solar-metallicity model, extending the $\texttt{CMC Cluster Catalog}$ to the most massive clusters observed in the local Universe. Using an analytic cluster formation model which convolves the cluster formation rate with delay-time distributions from our model suite, we find that the local cluster BBH merger rate is dominated by low-mass ($M_1 \lesssim 20\,M_\odot$) mergers originating from recently formed, high-metallicity clusters. While high-mass clusters are the primary sites of hierarchical (second-generation and higher) mergers, their contribution to the overall merger rate is suppressed by the exponential cutoff of the cluster initial mass function. The hierarchical merger rate is therefore highly sensitive to the exact value of the Schechter mass, $M_S$, suggesting that gravitational wave constraints on the hierarchical merger fraction can independently constrain the high-mass end of the cluster initial mass function. Our cluster formation model reproduces the observed present-day GC number density without fine tuning, and we find $M_S = 10^7\,M_\odot$ provides the best match to the observed Virgo GC mass distribution. For this value, $27\%$ of local dynamical mergers are hierarchical, compared to $18\%$ for the canonical value of $M_S = 10^{6.26}\,M_\odot$. Our merger weights, which we make publicly available, can be used for direct comparison with LVK distributions and other formation models.

</details>


### [133] [CoRGI in MaNGA - II. NGC 5717: cold-gas accretion and disk growth in a BGG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20704)
*Damir Gasymov,Ivan Yu. Katkov,Eva K. Grebel,Anna Pasquali,Evgenii V. Rubtsov,Katarina Kraljic,Igor V. Chilingarian,Kirill A. Grishin,Anna S. Saburova*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大质量中心星系NGC 5717中罕见的恒星反向旋转盘，利用MaNGA等多源数据揭示其年轻、贫金属、冷动力学的次级盘起源于外部冷气体吸积（可能来自宇宙细丝），支持在星系群组装早期阶段发生的原位恒星形成，是大质量中心星系仍能通过冷气体吸积生长盘的稀有例证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大质量中心星系（如最亮星系群成员）能否通过外部冷气体吸积形成恒星反向旋转结构，挑战传统认为此类环境会加热或剥离冷气体、抑制该通道的观点。

Method: 结合SDSS-IV MaNGA积分场光谱、CFHT成像、辅助HI数据及SDSS/DESI对星系群环境的光谱观测，分析NGC 5717的动力学结构、恒星年龄与金属丰度、气体分布，并构建化学演化模型以约束气体来源与恒星形成历史。

Result: 确认NGC 5717拥有一个年轻（~1.3 Gyr）、贫金属（[Fe/H] ~ −0.7 dex）、延展（h ~ 2.4 kpc）、大质量（log M⋆/M⊙ ~ 9.9）的冷动力学反向旋转恒星盘；电离气体与其共转，但中性氢（HI）含量极低（log MHI/M⊙ ≲ 9）；化学演化模型支持来自宇宙细丝的气体吸积及高效爆发式恒星形成伴强金属外流。

Conclusion: NGC 5717代表一种罕见情形：大质量中心星系在星系群组装早期，借助外部冷气体（可能源自宇宙细丝）吸积，实现了次级盘的原位生长，表明冷气体吸积机制在致密环境中仍可能短期有效。

Abstract: Large-scale stellar counter-rotation, in which two coplanar stellar components rotate in opposite directions, is usually interpreted as a signature of external gas accretion. Such systems are most often found in relatively sparse environments, where cold gas can be acquired and remain dynamically undisturbed long enough to settle into a disk. It is less clear whether this channel can operate in massive central galaxies, whose group or cluster environments can heat, strip, or perturb cold gas. Here we present NGC 5717, one of the most massive galaxies known to host a counter-rotating stellar disk and the first identified case in a brightest group galaxy. Using integral-field spectroscopy from SDSS-IV Mapping Nearby Galaxies at Apache Point Observatory (MaNGA), together with CFHT imaging, ancillary HI data, and SDSS/DESI spectroscopy of the surrounding group, we show that the galaxy contains a dynamically cold counter-rotating disk embedded in an old dynamically hot elliptical component. The disk is young ($\sim 1.3$ Gyr), metal-poor ([Fe/H] $\sim -0.7$ dex), extended ($h \sim 2.4$ kpc), and massive ($\log M_\star/M_\odot \sim 9.9$); the associated ionized gas co-rotates with it, while the available HI data indicate no significant remaining reservoir of neutral atomic gas ($\log M_{\mathrm{HI}}/M_\odot \lesssim 9$). These properties support an in situ origin of the secondary component from externally accreted gas. The chemical evolution model favors gas accretion from a cosmic filament, which is consistent with the surrounding cosmic-web geometry, and indicates highly efficient burst-like star formation with substantial metal-enriched outflows. NGC 5717 may therefore represent a rare example of disk growth in a massive central galaxy, enabled by external cold-gas accretion during an earlier phase of the group assembly history.

</details>


### [134] [How Little Red Dots Turn Blue: Evolution of Kelvin-Helmholtz Contracting Black Hole Envelopes and Observational Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20709)
*Zihao Wang,Kohei Inayoshi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型，解释詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）发现的‘小红点’（LRDs）与‘小蓝点’（LBDs）之间的物理联系：二者是同一黑洞—恒星联合系统在不同演化阶段的表现，由气体包层收缩及吸积盘暴露所驱动，并预测了其在紫外—光学谱斜率平面上的可检验演化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRDs与LBDs的物理本质及相互关系尚不明确，亟需统一模型解释其观测差异与潜在演化关联。

Method: 构建一个嵌入厚气体包层并伴随核区恒星形成的快速生长黑洞模型，通过模拟包层开尔文-亥姆霍兹收缩及吸积盘暴露过程，拟合LRDs与LBDs的SED，并推导其在β_UV–β_opt平面上的演化轨迹。

Result: 模型成功再现LRDs与LBDs的观测SED；预测出一条稳健的红向偏移演化轨迹，起始于LRD区域、经(β_UV, β_opt)≈(2,−1)后转向LBD区域；过渡时标为5–50 Myr，取决于黑洞反馈效率与包层气体保留能力。

Conclusion: LRDs与LBDs很可能是同一类早期超大质量黑洞成长系统的不同演化相位；该模型提供的演化轨迹及其丰度比可作为约束早期黑洞反馈与气体留存物理的关键观测诊断工具。

Abstract: James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations have revealed compact, red-colored, broad-line emitters known as little red dots (LRDs), while a bluer counterpart population has been referred to as little blue dots (LBDs). Their physical connection remains unclear: they may represent different evolutionary stages of the same phenomenon, distinct populations, or different phases of the broader evolution of early galaxies and their black holes (BHs). We propose a model in which a rapidly growing BH is embedded in a geometrically thick gaseous envelope accompanied by concurrent nuclear star formation. The model reproduces the observed spectral energy distributions (SEDs) of LRDs and LBDs, and naturally connects the two phases through Kelvin-Helmholtz contraction of the envelope and exposure of the nuclear accretion disk, as the gas supply declines due to external stellar activity or feedback. We predict the corresponding evolutionary tracks in the UV-optical slope ($β_{\rm UV}$-$β_{\rm opt}$) plane which bridge the apparent gap between LRDs and LBDs. These evolutionary tracks start from the LRD regime and consistently exhibit a red excursion that extends toward $(β_{\rm UV},β_{\rm opt})\sim(2,-1)$ before turning toward the LBD regime. This feature is largely insensitive to variations in the model parameters that govern the envelope structure, providing a robust signature of the transitional population. The transition lasts $\sim5$-$50\,\mathrm{Myr}$, depending primarily on the BH feedback efficiency and the gas-retention efficiency of the envelope. The abundance ratio of transitional systems to LRDs therefore provides a constraint on BH feedback and gas retention during the rapid growth of early supermassive BHs.

</details>


### [135] [The Pristine survey XXIX. Data Release 2: Photometric metallicities for 15 millions stars down to $G\sim 20.5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20714)
*Zhen Yuan,Anke Ardern-Arentsen,Nicolas F. Martin,Else Starkenburg,Morgan Fouesneau,Piercarlo Bonifacio,Elisabetta Caffau,Vanessa Hill,Georges Kordopatis,Camila Navarrete,Julio Navarro,Samuel Rusterucci,Federico Sestito,Salvatore Taibi,Kim A. Venn,Akshara Viswanathan,HanYuan Zhang,Giuseppina Battaglia*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Pristine巡天的第二版数据发布（DR2），扩展了观测覆盖范围和深度，利用Gaia合成光度数据校准并训练神经网络模型，从而获得约1500万颗恒星的光度金属丰度，显著提升了对银河系贫金属晕星的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对银河系贫金属晕星的探测能力，尤其是更暗、更远的金属丰度极低的恒星，弥补DR1在深度和覆盖范围上的限制。

Method: 基于2015年3月至2025年1月的所有Pristine窄带CaHK测光数据，使用Gaia DR3合成CaHK光度的亮星子集训练PhotCalib神经网络进行绝对定标，并结合已有(CaHK, G, GBP, GRP)到[Fe/H]的经验关系推导光度金属丰度。

Result: DR2覆盖约8000平方度，提供1500万颗FGK型恒星的光度金属丰度，与DESI独立光谱金属丰度结果一致；识别出超20万颗极贫金属星（VMP）和6500颗极端贫金属星（EMP）候选体。

Conclusion: DR2显著拓展了Pristine巡天的深度与面积，为研究银河系早期化学演化和晕结构提供了迄今最大、最深的光度金属丰度星表之一。

Abstract: Tailored narrow-band photometric observations are an efficient way to measure the metallicity of Milky Way stars. The Pristine survey has been mapping the northern sky since 2015 using narrow-band $CaHK$ photometry obtained with the MegaCam imager on the Canada-France-Hawaii Telescope (CFHT). Data Release 1 (DR1) provided a high-precision absolute calibration of the survey observed before October 2022 using \Gaia DR3 XP synthetic photometry, yielding photometric metallicities over $\sim$6,500 deg$^2$, but was limited in depth to the availability of \Gaia spectro-photometry ($G \lesssim 17.65$). We present the Pristine Data Release 2 (DR2) that includes all Pristine observations until January 2025, corresponding to an additional 1,500\,deg$^2$ of coverage, and including all faint sources down to $G\sim20.5$. This release aims to exploit the full depth of the Pristine observations to uncover the faint metal-poor halo. We process all images observed from the start of the survey in March 2015 through to January 2025 following the methodology presented in DR1. The core of the calibration utilizes the subset of bright stars with \Gaia synthetic $CaHK_\mathrm{syn}$ magnitudes to train the \texttt{PhotCalib} neural network. We then derive photometric metallicities using the established Pristine ($CaHK, G, G_{\rm BP}, G_{\rm RP}$) to [Fe/H] model. The resulting catalogue covers approximately $\sim$ 8,000 deg$^{2}$ and provides photometric metallicities for 15 millions FGK stars, which show good agreement with recently published, independent Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) metallicities. With its increased coverage and access to a significantly fainter regime compared to DR1, DR2 significantly increases the volume of the survey. We report more than 200,000 and 6,500 very and extremely metal-poor star candidates, respectively.

</details>


### [136] [HI rotation curves and mass-size relation for 568 galaxies from the Apertif Wide-area Extragalactic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20718)
*Konstantin Haubner,Federico Lelli,Enrico Di Teodoro,Kelley M. Hess,Francis Duey,Stacy McGaugh,James Schombert,Elizabeth A. K. Adams,W. J. G. de Blok,Danielle M. Lucero,Helga Dénes,Julián Garrido,Joeri van Leeuwen,Manuel Parra-Royón,Anastasia A. Ponomareva,Susana Sánchez Exposito*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AWES巡天的首次数据发布，提供了568个可靠的HI动力学模型，构成了目前最大、最均匀的HI旋转曲线样本，并基于此研究了HI质量-尺寸关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展的HI旋转曲线是研究星系演化及检验不同暗物质和修正引力模型的有力工具，但受限于射电干涉仪的巡天速度，其样本量历史以来一直较小。

Method: 基于Apertif宽视场河外巡天（AWES）中探测到的1924个HI星系，构建了568个可靠的动力学模型，获取了旋转曲线、速度弥散剖面、表面密度剖面及HI盘几何结构；并利用HI表面密度剖面研究HI质量-尺寸关系。

Result: 获得了迄今最大、最均匀的HI旋转曲线样本，覆盖HI质量三个数量级、外旋速度20–300 km/s、尺度2–60 kpc；发现HI质量-尺寸关系极为紧密，拟合斜率为1.96±0.03，表明HI盘具有近似恒定的平均面密度。

Conclusion: AWES提供的大样本HI动力学数据为星系演化与暗物质/引力理论研究提供了强有力的新资源；HI质量-尺寸关系的紧密性支持HI盘面密度近似恒定的物理图景。

Abstract: The extended HI rotation curves of galaxies are one of the most powerful tools for studying galaxy evolution and for testing different dark matter and modified gravity models. However, their sample sizes have historically been limited to a few hundred galaxies, mostly due to the survey speed of radio interferometers. Here, we present a first data release of HI kinematic models from the Apertif Wide-area Extragalactic Survey (AWES). Starting from 1924 galaxies detected in HI, we constructed 568 reliable kinematic models, which provide rotation curves, velocity dispersion profiles, surface density profiles, and HI disk geometries. This dataset constitutes the largest homogeneous HI rotation curve sample to date. The HI disks of the modeled galaxies span three orders of magnitude in HI mass, have outer rotation velocities between ~20 and ~300 km/s, and extend from 2 to more than 60 kpc. The number of data points of the rotation curves ranges from 3 to 23, with a peak value at 4 and a broad tail to larger values. The rotation curves display a variety of inner shapes, but in general, they tend to become flat at large radii. Based on the HI surface density profiles derived during the kinematic modeling, we studied the HI mass-size relation with a large homogeneous sample of 213 galaxies. The relation is very tight, with an observed scatter along the mass direction of ~0.101 dex and an inferred intrinsic scatter along the same direction of only 0.067 +/- 0.007 dex. The best fitting slope is 1.96 +/- 0.03 when fitting for mass as a function of size, indicating that HI disks have a constant average surface density.

</details>


### [137] [Wandering intermediate-mass black holes and off-nuclear tidal disruption events from early star clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20719)
*Claire E. Williams,Smadar Naoz,Yihao Zhou,Tatsuya Akiba-Lewis,Mark Vogelsberger,Blakesley Burkhart,Naoki Yoshida,Avi Chen,Kyle Kremer,William Lake,Federico Marinacci,Shyam H. Menon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过高红移致密星团中恒星碰撞形成的游荡中等质量黑洞（IMBHs）所引发的非核潮汐撕裂事件（TDEs）及其宇宙学演化特征，建立了半解析模型并预测其发生率与空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游荡超大质量黑洞的存在证据来自非核TDEs，但其起源尚不清楚；本文旨在探究由高红shift致密星团中恒星并合形成的游荡IMBHs是否可解释这类事件，并追溯其早期形成历史。

Method: 构建半解析模型，采样红移7–20间低质量子晕关联的星团，结合IllustrisTNG TNG50模拟的并合树追踪15个椭圆星系（恒星质量10^9–10^11 M⊙）的组装过程；对比两种高红移星团形成模型（最大模型与保守模型），模拟IMBH形成后的损失锥耗尽导致的TDE爆发。

Result: 非核TDE在IMBH形成后10–100 Myr内成批发生，典型距离星系中心10–10^3 kpc；事件率随星系质量增加，在最大模型中，最重星系前身体在z≈7–13达峰值率5×10^{-4}–4×10^{-2} yr^{-1}每星系。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用高红移非核TDE观测约束早期形成IMBH的星团种群提供了理论框架和可检验预测。

Abstract: Off-nuclear tidal disruption events (TDEs) reveal evidence for massive black holes outside galactic centers, but the origins of this wandering population remain uncertain. We investigate wandering intermediate-mass black holes (IMBHs) formed through runaway stellar collisions in dense, high-redshift star clusters and predict their TDE signatures across cosmic time. Our semi-analytic model samples clusters associated with low-mass subhalos at $7<z<20$ and uses merger trees from the IllustrisTNG TNG50 simulation to follow the assembly of 15 elliptical galaxies with stellar masses of $10^9-10^{11}~M_\odot$. We compare two models for high-redshift cluster formation: a maximal model taken from a high-resolution simulation and a conservative model calibrated from local cluster relations. Off-nuclear TDEs produced through this mechanism occur in bursts within 10-100 Myr of IMBH formation as the loss-cone surrounding the black hole is depleted. We estimate the rate of off-nuclear TDEs expected across time per galaxy, and characterize these events as typically occurring between $10-10^3$ kpc from their host galaxy centers. Event rates increase with galaxy mass; in the maximal model, the progenitors of the most massive galaxies reach peak off-nuclear TDE rates of approximately $5\times10^{-4}-4\times10^{-2}~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$ per galaxy at $7\lesssim z\lesssim13$. This work provides a framework for constraining the early population of star clusters that form IMBHs through runaway collapse via their contribution to the rate of off-nuclear TDEs in the high redshift Universe.

</details>


### [138] [An Attenuation Bump at z=9.3: Probing Dust Diversity at Cosmic Dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20722)
*Giulia Rodighiero,Santiago Álvarez-Córdoba,Michele Catone,Borja Pérez-Díaz,Andrea Ferrara,Giorgia Girardi,Lorenzo Napolitano,Paola Santini,Hollis B. Akins,Caitlin M. Casey,Maximilien Franco,Pietro Benotto,Rachana Bhatawdekar,Alessandro Bianchetti,Laura Bisigello,Daniela Calzetti,Paolo Cassata,Marco Castellano,Christopher J. Conselice,Poulomi Dam,Pratika Dayal,Andrea Enia,Gaia Edes Esposito,Andreas L. Faisst,Giovanni Gandolfi,Steven Gillman,Andrea Grazian,Carlotta Gruppioni,Michaela Hirschmann,Edoardo Iani,Kohei Inayoshi,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Georgios Magdis,Alessandro Marconi,Crystal L. Martin,Bo Milvang-Jensen,Annagrazia Puglisi,Alvio Renzini,R. Michael Rich,Pierluigi Rinaldi,Brant E. Robertson,Stephen Serjeant,Irene Shivaei,Sune Toft,Amelia Vietri,Benedetta Vulcani,Fabian Walter,Stephen M. Wilkins,Jorge A. Zavala*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了在红移z=9.3335的星系Black Pearl中探测到迄今最高红移的紫外（UV）衰减峰（2175 Å bump），该特征揭示了宇宙大爆炸后约5亿年时星际尘埃成分的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究早期宇宙（高红移）星系中尘埃性质，特别是UV衰减峰的存在及其与本地银河系特征的异同，以理解早期尘埃形成与演化机制。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRSpec数据，在ZENITH和EMBER巡天中对Black Pearl星系进行光谱分析；采用Drude轮廓拟合UV衰减峰，并结合灵活衰减律的联合测光-光谱建模，独立检验UV峰存在性及星系物理参数；辅以光学发射线诊断分析电离源性质。

Result: 明确探测到中心波长2132±17 Å、FWHM为413±54 Å、峰值衰减0.21±0.02 mag的UV衰减峰；该峰相对于银河系标准2175 Å峰蓝移43±17 Å（~2.5σ），且比已知z≈7的UV峰更显著蓝移；建模给出恒星质量log M* ≈ 8.35 M⊙、SFR ≈ 2.36 M⊙/yr、Av ≈ 0.34 mag；发射线表明存在硬电离谱和中等AGN贡献，但不足以主导UV连续谱。

Conclusion: z=9.3335处UV峰的蓝移提供了早期宇宙尘埃载体（如PAHs或小石墨颗粒）成分与本地不同的最清晰证据，表明高红移时期UV衰减峰特性具有显著多样性。

Abstract: We report the identification of a UV attenuation bump in the Black Pearl, a galaxy at a spectroscopic redshift of 9.3335 observed with JWST/NIRSpec as part of the ZENITH and EMBER surveys. This is the highest-redshift UV bump reported to date, probing dust properties only approximately 500 Myr after the Big Bang. The feature is directly detected as a broad depression in the rest-frame UV continuum and is well described by a local Drude profile, with a central wavelength of 2132+/-17 Angstroms, an FWHM of 413+/-54 Angstroms, and a peak attenuation of 0.21+/-0.02 mag. Joint spectrophotometric modeling with a flexible attenuation law independently recovers a pronounced UV bump without imposing its presence a priori, regardless of the adopted star-formation history. The modeling indicates a relatively low-mass, actively star-forming galaxy, with a logarithmic stellar mass of 8.35 (+0.09/-0.08) in solar units, a star-formation rate of 2.36 (+0.54/-0.42) solar masses per year, and a visual attenuation of 0.34 (+0.12/-0.11) mag. Rest-frame optical emission-line diagnostics indicate a hard ionizing spectrum and allow for a moderate AGN contribution, but provide no evidence for a powerful AGN capable of dominating the observed UV continuum. The bump is blueshifted by 43+/-17 Angstroms (approximately 2.5 sigma) relative to the canonical Milky Way 2175 Angstrom feature and by 127 +/- 17 Angstroms relative to the few UV bumps currently known at redshift approximately 7, while its width is consistent with the broad distribution measured along Milky Way sightlines. The displaced central wavelength therefore provides the clearest evidence for a nonstandard UV-bump profile at redshift 9.3335, pointing to substantial diversity in the properties of UV-bump carriers at early cosmic times, with both PAHs and small graphite grains remaining plausible candidates.

</details>


### [139] [A model for the ongoing subcluster interaction in Centaurus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20726)
*Felipe S. Lohmann,Magda Arnaboldi,Michael Hilker,Andreas Burkert,Marilena Spavone,Ortwin Gerhard,Marina Rejkuba,Steffen Mieske,Tom Richtler,Marco Mirabile,Michele Cantiello,Enrichetta Iodice,Sara Federle*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用VLT/FORS深场成像数据，研究了半人马座星系团内球状星团（GCs）的空间分布与颜色特性，揭示其子团Cen 30与Cen 45之间近期发生近对头碰撞，并通过GC桥、密度分布及星系光度函数演化等多方面证据支持该动力学场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半人马座星系团正经历Cen 30与Cen 45两个子团的相互作用，其内晕球状星团（GCs）可记录这一组装历史；本文旨在刻画GC总体性质并约束该星系团近期并合过程。

Method: 基于VLT/FORS的V和I波段深场成像，结合形态与颜色筛选GC候选体；采用Voronoi镶嵌法分析GC空间分布与密度轮廓；利用GC比频率与颜色分布反演星系光度函数演化。

Result: 发现GC空间分布存在显著子结构：红GC集中在最亮星系周围，蓝GC分布更弥散；确认并量化了连接NGC 4696与NGC 4709的GC‘桥’，其GC数量达两星系投影系统预期值的两倍；推得星系光度函数暗端斜率α = −1.87±0.06；提出Cen 45近期沿视线方向穿过Cen 30的近对头碰撞模型。

Conclusion: GC的空间与颜色分布为半人马座星系团的子团并合提供了关键动力学证据；所提出的近对头碰撞场景统一解释了大视向速度差、高温X射线气体、GC桥及NGC 4709膨胀的GC系统等观测特征。

Abstract: Intracluster globular clusters (GCs) preserve the hierarchical and chemical history of galaxy cluster assembly. The nearby Centaurus cluster is believed to be undergoing an interaction between two subclusters, Cen 30 and Cen 45, which should leave an imprint on its intracluster GCs. We aim to characterise the GC population in Centaurus and constrain its recent assembly history, ultimately constructing an interaction scenario consistent with the observational evidence. Using deep VLT/FORS imaging in the $V$ and $I$ bands, we select GC candidates around the cluster centre based on morphology and colour criteria. We use a Voronoi tessellation approach to study the GC spatial distribution and density profiles, demonstrating its advantages over traditional annuli-based methods. We then constrain the evolution of the galaxy luminosity function using the GC specific frequency and colour distribution. The GC spatial distribution shows significant substructure. As in other clusters, red GCs are concentrated on the brightest galaxies, whereas blue GCs trace a more extended structure. We confirm and quantify the previously reported 'bridge' of GCs connecting NGC 4696 and NGC 4709, showing that it hosts twice as many GCs as expected from the projected GC systems of the two galaxies alone. The inferred evolution of the galaxy luminosity function agrees with previous results for Hydra I, yielding a faint-end slope of $α=-1.87_{-0.06}^{+0.06}$ for the galaxy population that assembled the Centaurus core. We propose a nearly head-on collision scenario in which Cen 45 recently passed through Cen 30, with the two subclusters moving approximately along the line of sight. This scenario explains the large line-of-sight velocity difference between the subclusters, the hot X-ray-emitting gas between them, the GC bridge, and the puffed-up GC system of NGC 4709.

</details>


### [140] [The ALMA-QUARKS Survey. IV. Core Mass Function and Evolutionary Trends in Core Formation Efficiency and Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20738)
*Wenyu Jiao,Tie Liu,Dongting Yang,Fengwei Xu,Guido Garay,Patricio Sanhueza,Kee-Tae Kim,Yankun Zhang,Siju Zhang,Xunchuan Liu,Lei Zhu,Amelia M. Stutz,Mika Juvela,Neal J. Evans,Shanghuo Li,Sami Dib,Paul F. Goldsmith,Aiyuan Yang,Jihye Hwang,Di Li,Gyuho Lee,Xindi Tang,Chang Won Lee,Anindya Saha,Naval Kishor Bhadari,Yuhan Yang,Swagat R. Das,Dmitry A. Ladeyschikov,Shivani Gupta,Sheng-li Qin,Hong-li Liu,Xiaofeng Mai,Prasanta Gorai,Tapas Baug,James O. Chibueze,Pablo García,Leonardo Bronfman,Andrej M. Sobolev,Jian-Wen Zhou,Yunfan Jiao,Jianjun Zhou,Li Chen,Chuanshou Li,Zhenying Zhang,Jixiang Weng,L. Viktor Tóth*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: ALMA-QUARKS巡天对139个大质量原星团进行了高分辨率观测，识别出3374个致密核并分类；发现致密核质量函数在高质端呈平缓幂律，但按距离分组后接近Salpeter形式；核心形成效率与母云团光度/质量比正相关；分子示踪剂探测率随演化阶段系统变化，且氘化物种丰度受环境显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大质量恒星形成的物理机制，特别是致密核的统计性质、质量分布、形成效率及其化学演化规律。

Method: 利用ALMA在1.3 mm波段对ATOMS选源的139个大质量原星团进行~0.3″分辨率观测；使用getsf算法识别3374个致密核，并结合谱线诊断（H30α、CH₃CN、CH₃OH）和CO外流判断演化类型；统计分析核心质量函数（CMF）、核心形成效率（CFE）及分子示踪剂探测率。

Result: 得到包含物理参数的致密核星表；全局CMF高质端幂律指数γ=0.98±0.03（偏平），但按距离分组后接近Salpeter（γ≈1.35）且低质量截断随距离增大；CFE与母云团L/M正相关；不同演化阶段分子示踪剂探测率存在系统差异；相比ASHES巡天，相同演化阶段氘化物探测率差异显著，表明环境对化学演化有重要影响。

Conclusion: 大质量原星团中致密核的统计性质受观测选择效应（如距离）显著影响，其形成效率受母云团能量状态调控，而化学演化不仅依赖演化阶段，更受局部环境制约。

Abstract: The ALMA-QUARKS (Querying Underlying Mechanisms of Massive Star Formation with ALMA-Resolved Gas Kinematics and Structures) survey targets 139 massive protoclusters selected from the ATOMS survey. The targets were observed at 1.3~mm wavelength with an angular resolution of $\sim0.3^{\prime\prime}$, corresponding to $\sim10^3$~au at the sample's median distance of $\sim3.8$~kpc. Using \texttt{getsf}, we identify 3374 dense cores and classify them as starless, protostellar, hot core, or H{\sc ii} region candidates based on spectral-line diagnostics (e.g., H30$α$, CH$_3$CN, and CH$_3$OH) and CO outflow associations. The resulting catalog provides core physical properties and enables statistical investigations of dense core populations in massive protocluster environments. The derived global core mass function (CMF) after excluding H{\sc ii} region candidates, which is biased by distance effects, has a relatively flat power-law slope at the high-mass end ($\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}\log M \propto M^{-γ}$, with $γ=0.98\pm0.03$). In contrast, the measured high-mass slopes in individual distance bins are approximately Salpeter-like, while their fitted lower mass limits increase with distance. We further examine the core formation efficiency (CFE) across the full sample and find that it positively correlates with the luminosity-to-mass ratio of the parental clumps, consistent with an evolutionary increase in the fraction of mass concentrated into dense cores. We also investigate the detection rates of different molecular tracers and find systematic chemical variations with evolutionary stage. Compared with the ASHES survey, we find that the detection rates of deuterated species vary significantly even for cores at the same evolutionary stages, suggesting an important environmental influence on the chemical evolution of cores.

</details>


### [141] [The optical colors of hidden spiral-like features in nine Virgo early--type dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20819)
*Mauricio Gracia,Rory Smith,Anna Pasquali,Laura Ferrarese,Patrick Côté,Hyowon Kim,Paula Calderón-Castillo,Kirsi Pohl*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析室女座星系团中9个矮椭圆星系（dwarf ETGs）的光学颜色，研究其内部嵌入的螺旋状结构（如旋臂）的光度特性，发现这些结构仅贡献总光度的1.5%-5.5%，且颜色多样性显著（部分更红、部分更蓝、部分无差异），表明其形成路径不止一种。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮椭圆星系中发现嵌入的螺旋状结构，但其起源尚不明确；需通过光度和颜色特征约束其形成机制。

Method: 利用NGVS深度g/i波段图像，采用残差法分离螺旋状成分与弥散星系主体，定义旋臂区与间隙区，并测量各自光占比和g-i颜色。

Result: 螺旋状结构光占比仅约1.5–5.5%（i波段）；颜色呈现三类：4个星系中旋臂比间隙更红，4个无显著色差，1个旋臂明显更蓝。

Conclusion: 矮椭圆星系中的螺旋状结构具有显著的光度与颜色多样性，暗示存在多种演化通道，而非单一形成机制；该方法已具备拓展至更大样本的能力。

Abstract: Dwarf early-type galaxies (ETGs) are abundant in nearby clusters and provide a key laboratory for studying environmentally driven galaxy transformation. Although dwarf ETGs were once considered structurally simple, deep imaging has revealed embedded spiral-like features (e.g., bars and spiral arms) in some of them, contributing only a small fraction of the total light. Their origin at dwarf scales remains uncertain. As a pilot study, we investigate spiral-like features in nine Virgo dwarf ETGs with clear embedded arms, using optical colors to test whether these features differ from the surrounding diffuse component, which would constrain their formation pathways. Using deep g- and i-band NGVS images, we apply a residual-based method to separate excess light associated with the spiral-like component from the diffuse host. The residual images define arm and interarm regions, enabling direct measurements of light fractions and g-i colors for both components. The spiral-like features contribute only a few percent of the total light (about 1.5-5.5% in i and 1.7-3.7% in g). We find a diverse range of colors across the sample: four galaxies host spiral-like features redder than their interarm regions, four show no robust arm-interarm color difference, and one exhibits a distinctly bluer spiral-like component. The latter is consistent with a recently quenched transition object, while the redder and color-uniform features are consistent with older or more metal-rich stellar populations. Spiral-like features in Virgo dwarf ETGs thus exhibit a surprising diversity in their photometric properties, implying multiple evolutionary channels rather than a single one. This pilot analysis also demonstrates readiness for the much larger NGVS sample of dwarfs with potential spiral-like features.

</details>


### [142] [Self Calibrated Galaxy Groups from the DESI DR1 Bright Galaxy Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20919)
*I. Williams,J. Tinker,K. Wang,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,A. Carnero Rosell,F. J. Castander,T. Claybaugh,S. Cole,A. de la Macorra,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztanaga,S. Gontcho A Gontcho,G. Gutierrez,C. Hahn,M. Ishak,S. Juneau,R. Kehoe,A. Kremin,C. Lamman,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,A. Leauthaud,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,J. Moustakas,S. Nadathur,E. Paillas,W. J. Percival,F. Prada,I. Perez-Rafols,C. Ravoux,A. J. Ross,G. Rossi,R. Ruggeri,L. Samushia,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,D. Schlegel,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarle,R. Vaisakh,B. A. Weaver*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文基于DESI Bright Galaxy Survey数据，使用自校准星系群寻找算法构建了一个包含220多万个星系的星系群样本，并给出了中心/卫星星系分类与暗物质晕质量估计；分析了卫星比例、光度-晕质量关系、恒星质量-晕质量关系及非参数HOD，发现其可被标准HOD模型拟合；还提出一种混合近邻（HNN）方法缓解光纤观测不完备性带来的系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统星系群寻找算法依赖模拟校准参数，存在模型依赖性；同时，DESI等巡天面临光纤观测不完备问题，需发展稳健的数据驱动方法。

Method: 采用Self Calibrated Group Finder算法，利用观测样本自身的成团性信息校准自由参数；对DESI DR1 BGS数据构建星系群目录；引入Hybrid Nearest Neighbor（HNN）新方法，结合测光红移与多邻源信息填补未观测目标红移。

Result: 获得覆盖~2680 deg²、含2,229,741个星系的群表；给出f_sat、L–M_halo、M_star–M_halo及非参数HOD；HOD拟合得α≈0.75（恒星形成星系）和≈1.1（宁静星系）；发现中央星系熄火比例主要由晕质量决定；HNN方法在SV3数据上红移正确率约50%，且HOD统计无偏。

Conclusion: 自校准方法可有效减少对模拟的依赖，提升群识别可靠性；HNN为处理光纤不完备提供了实用新思路；结果支持‘晕质量主导星系演化’的主流图景，并为后续宇宙学与星系形成研究提供高质量输入。

Abstract: We present a galaxy group catalog built from Data Release 1 of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Bright Galaxy Survey, using the Self Calibrated Group Finder. The algorithm uses clustering data from the observed sample to calibrate the Group Finder's free parameters, rather than simulations. The catalog contains 2,229,741 galaxies from ~$2680$ deg$^2$ of sky with 3-pass coverage and $r$-band fluxes $\lt 19.5$. The catalog classifies all galaxies in the flux-limited sample as centrals or satellites and estimates host dark matter halo masses. We show the satellite fraction $f_{\rm sat}$, luminosity halo mass relation, stellar halo mass relation and halo occupation distributions (HODs) from the catalog. We find these non-parametric HODs can be fit by standard HOD models, with satellite power-law index $α$ values around 0.75 for star-forming galaxies and around 1.1 for quiescent objects. In agreement with recent results, we find that the quenched fraction of central galaxies depends mostly on halo mass and is nearly independent of central galaxy stellar mass for galaxies more massive than $10^{10.3}$ $M_\odot h^{-1}$ and halos more massive than $10^{12.2}$ $M_\odot h^{-1}$. In an appendix, we introduce a new Hybrid Nearest Neighbor (HNN) technique to mitigate systematics from fiber incompleteness. HNN uses photometric redshifts and multiple on-sky neighbors to assign redshifts to unobserved targets. Calibrated on DESI Survey Validation 3 data, our technique yields correct redshifts $\sim 50\%$ of the time and produces unbiased halo occupation statistics.

</details>


### [143] [JWST reveals a highly ionized nebula powered by an ultraluminous X-ray source with a massive black hole in the metal-poor galaxy Holmberg II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20948)
*F. A. Fogantini,P. Zeidler,L. M. Oskinova,T. Bao,W. -R. Hamann,P. Marchant,D. M. -A. Meyer,D. Pauli,S. Reyero Serantes,A. A. C. Sander,T. Shenar,R. Soria*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST对低金属丰度星系Holmberg II中的ULX源Ho II X-1周围星云进行积分视场光谱观测，首次探测到[Ne V]和[Ne VI]发射线，证实该ULX（一个吸积黑洞）是周围百pc尺度高度电离星云的直接电离源，并揭示电离辐射呈各向异性、与射电喷流方向一致，排除激波主导电离机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST近期观测在一些低金属丰度星系中探测到高电离势离子（如[Ne V]、[O IV]）的精细结构线，引发对其电离源本质的疑问；ULX被认为是潜在贡献者，但缺乏单个ULX与高度电离星云之间的直接关联证据。

Method: 利用JWST积分视场单元（IFU）光谱技术，对已知存在He II λ4686和[O IV] 25.89 μm发射的ULX Ho II X-1周围星云进行高空间分辨率（~15–25 pc）观测，并分析谱线空间分布、电离结构、运动学及与射电结构的关联。

Result: 首次在Ho II X-1周围探测到[Ne V]和[Ne VI]；最高电离谱线集中在偏离ULX、朝向射电结的致密区域，呈现各向异性电离；谱线窄且符合仪器分辨率，表明气体运动宁静，激波非主导电离机制。

Conclusion: 本工作为单个ULX（即低金属丰度星系中的吸积黑洞）驱动高度电离星云提供了直接观测证据，并表明其电离辐射受喷流活动调制，具有显著各向异性。

Abstract: Recent James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations have revealed fine-structure lines of ions with ionization potentials ${\gtrsim}50$\,eV, such as [Ne\,{\sc v}] and [O\,{\sc iv}], in nebular spectra of some low-metallicity galaxies, raising questions about the nature of the ionizing sources. Ultraluminous X-ray sources (ULXs) have been considered as potential contributors, but direct evidence linking individual ULXs to highly ionized nebulae is limited. Here we present JWST IFU spectroscopy of the nebula surrounding the template ULX Ho\,II\,X--1 in the low-metallicity galaxy Holmberg\,II, already known to emit He\,{\sc ii}\,$\lambda4686$ and [O\,{\sc iv}]\,25.89\,$μ$m lines. We resolve the infrared nebula on spatial scales of ${\sim}15-25$\,pc and report the first detections of [Ne\,{\sc v}] and [Ne\,{\sc vi}] in this source. We find anisotropic ionizing conditions: emission from the highest-ionization species arises from compact regions offset from the ULX toward the previously detected radio knots, whereas lower-ionization species trace the wider nebula. The preferential alignment of the highest-ionization emission with the radio axis suggests anisotropic escape of the ionizing continuum and a physical connection with jet-ejection activity. Spectral lines across the nebula are narrow and consistent with the instrumental resolution, indicating kinematically quiescent gas and disfavoring shocks as the dominant ionization mechanism. Together, these results provide direct evidence linking an individual ULX to a highly ionized nebula in a low-metallicity galaxy. These JWST observations of Ho~II~X--1 show that an accreting black hole in a low-metallicity galaxy is responsible for the extreme ionization of the surrounding nebula on ${\sim}100$\,pc scales.

</details>


### [144] [ACACIAS II: element abundances for 39 stars in the dwarf galaxy Sculptor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21028)
*Anell Cornejo-Cárdenas,Sven Buder,Melissa K. Ness,J. Trevor Mendel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ACACIAS项目的第二阶段，利用The Cannon数据驱动方法分析了Sculptor矮椭球星系中39颗恒星的MUSE低分辨率、低信噪比光谱，获得了[Mg/Fe]、[Mn/Fe]、[C/Fe]、[N/Fe]和[Al/Fe]等元素丰度，精度为0.09–0.37 dex，并探讨了不同光谱建模策略对丰度恢复效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过邻近星系中单颗恒星的化学丰度研究其化学增丰历史，并将银河系及其邻近星系置于更广阔的宇宙学背景下进行比较；构建横跨不同质量矮星系的均匀恒星丰度数据集，以与银河系性质对比。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法The Cannon，结合APOGEE高精度标签与模拟MUSE分辨率的LAMOST光谱，分析Sculptor星系中39颗恒星的低分辨率（R=1800）、低信噪比（~25）MUSE光谱；分别尝试限制波段模型与全谱模型（含大气吸收掩模），以优化不同元素丰度与恒星参数的测定。

Result: 成功测定[Mg/Fe]、[Mn/Fe]、[C/Fe]、[N/Fe]和[Al/Fe]五种元素丰度（基于APOGEE标度），精度达σ = 0.09–0.37 dex；发现[Mg/Fe]、[Mn/Fe]、[C/Fe]在特定谱窗中恢复最佳，而恒星参数与[Al/Fe]则依赖全谱信息加telluric掩模。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了低分辨率、低信噪比恒星光谱在构建均匀外星系考古数据集方面的可行性，为理解本星系群内化学增丰多样性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Element abundances in nearby galaxies provide insights into their chemical enrichment histories and place the Milky Way and its neighbours in a broader cosmological context. These systems can be studied through individual stars, whose resolved spectra enable the construction of a chemical abundance census of the Local Group. We present the second stage of the ACACIAS (extra-galactic ArChAeology with Cannon Inferred Abundances from resolved integral field unit Spectra) program, which aims to build the first homogeneous data set of stellar abundances across dwarf galaxies spanning a range of masses for comparison with Milky Way properties. We analyse low-resolution (R=1800), low-SNR (signal-to-noise ratio, ~ 25) MUSE spectra for 39 stars in the Sculptor dwarf spheroidal galaxy. Using the data-driven method The Cannon, we combine high-fidelity labels from the Apache Point Observatory Galactic Evolution Experiment (APOGEE) survey with MUSE-like Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST) spectra to analyse the MUSE data. This enables the determination of [Mg/Fe], [Mn/Fe], [C/Fe], [N/Fe], and [Al/Fe] on the APOGEE abundance scale, with precisions of σ = 0.09-0.37 dex. Comparing model configurations, we show that [Mg/Fe], [Mn/Fe], and [C/Fe] are best recovered from targeted spectral windows, whereas stellar parameters and [Al/Fe] benefit from full-spectrum information with telluric masking. This reflects the complementary strengths of restricted-window models, which recover abundances from direct spectral features, and full-spectrum models, which exploit correlations with other labels when diagnostic features are weak or absent. Our approach demonstrates the potential of low-resolution, low-SNR stellar spectra to build homogeneous datasets for extragalactic archaeology to better understand the chemical enrichment diversity of the Local Group.

</details>


### [145] [ALMA observations towards M83 reveal N2H+ tracing dense gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21142)
*Nanase Harada,Yuri Nishimura,Toshiki Saito,Mario Tafalla,Jin Koda,Doris Arzoumanian,Sophia K. Stuber,Akihiko Hirota,Yoshimasa Watanabe*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用ALMA观测邻近星系M83中N2H+和HCN的J=1-0谱线，发现N2H+在高柱密度区域对致密气体比HCN更敏感，其强度与HCN呈超线性关系；该趋势可通过含幂律尾部的对数正态柱密度分布（N-PDF）模型解释，且N2H+对幂律尾更敏感；但在星系核区因温度较高导致N2H+易被破坏，其作为致密气体示踪剂的可靠性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证银河系中N2H+比HCN或HCO+更优地示踪恒星形成致密分子气体这一趋势是否在邻近星系M83中同样成立。

Method: 利用ALMA观测M83西南棒端区域的N2H+(1-0)和HCN(1-0)谱线及1-mm/3-mm连续谱，并结合归档的CO(1-0)数据；采用辐射转移模型，基于参数化的柱密度概率分布函数（N-PDF）模拟谱线强度随柱密度的变化。

Result: N2H+与HCN强度整体呈近似线性相关，但在高柱密度区呈超线性关系；N2H+对N-PDF中的幂律尾部比HCN更敏感；星系核区因温度升高导致N2H+易被破坏，其作为致密气体示踪剂的可靠性降低。

Conclusion: N2H+在高柱密度、非中心区域是比HCN更灵敏的致密气体示踪剂，但其适用性受环境温度影响，在星系核等高温区域受限；N-PDF的结构（尤其是幂律尾）是理解不同示踪剂相对强度的关键。

Abstract: Galactic observations have shown that N2H+ intensities preferentially trace star-forming dense molecular gas compared with HCN or HCO+, which are conventionally used as dense-gas tracers in extragalactic studies. In this study, we aim to verify this trend in the nearby star-forming galaxy M83. Using ALMA, we observed the J=1-0 transitions of N2H+ and HCN, along with the 1-mm and 3-mm continuum, and analyzed the archival CO(1-0) data. The data achieved a spatial resolution of 48 pc in the southwestern bar-end region of M83, covering an area of 2.1 kpc x 1.8 kpc. N2H+ and HCN intensities are approximately linearly correlated, indicating that HCN luminosity can still reasonably predict the amount of dense gas as well as N2H+ luminosity does. However, in regions with high column densities, N2H+ becomes a super-linear function of HCN, suggesting that the N2H+ emission is more sensitive to dense gas than HCN. We employed a radiative transfer model that predicts line intensities as a function of column density, adopting parametric column density probability distribution functions (N-PDFs) to describe the underlying column density distribution. The model demonstrates that the observed linear or superlinear trend can be explained by a log-normal distribution only or a log-normal and a power-law tail distribution in the N-PDF. Furthermore, the N2H+ intensity is significantly more sensitive to the power-law tail than the HCN intensity. In addition, our analysis incorporating archival data shows that N2H+ is a less reliable tracer of dense gas in galactic center regions, where warmer temperatures may promote N2H+ destruction.

</details>


### [146] [An Energetic Consistency Test for Feedback-Driven Gas Loss in Milky Way Dwarf Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21186)
*Sena A. Matsui,Tsutomu T. Takeuchi,Suchetha Cooray,Kiyoaki Christopher Omori*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过能量预算测试分析了九个银河系矮卫星星系的气体损失机制，发现超新星反馈能量虽在多数情况下足以剥离气体，但恒星形成常在其后持续较长时间，表明能量充足性不等于实际淬灭发生时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮椭球星系（dSphs）缺乏冷气体，但其气体损失的主导机制（恒星反馈、再电离或环境效应）尚不明确。

Method: 基于已发表的星族演化史（SFH）、速度弥散、有效半径和V波段光度，对九个银河系矮卫星进行量级能量预算检验；定义能量等效时间τ_E，即核心坍缩超新星累积能量首次等于气体束缚能的时间，并与90%恒星质量形成时刻t_90,lb比较。

Result: 在标准参数下，所有九个星系均满足能量阈值（K+B16运动学-尺寸模型），但恒星形成常在阈值穿越后长期持续；τ_E与t_90,lb的接近性仅具描述性，非独立支持反馈致淬灭的证据；是否达阈值强烈依赖于晕质量、半径、残余气体分数及超新星-气体耦合效率。

Conclusion: 超新星反馈能量在多数矮星系中足以驱动气体剥离，但能量充足性并不决定恒星形成终止的实际时机，淬灭过程还受其他动力学与物理机制调控。

Abstract: Dwarf spheroidal galaxies (dSphs) contain little detectable cold gas, but the relative roles of stellar feedback, reionization, and environmental effects in their gas loss remain uncertain. We apply an order-of-magnitude energy-budget test to nine Milky Way dwarf satellites using published color--magnitude-diagram star formation histories (SFHs), galaxy-level velocity dispersions, effective radii, and integrated $V$-band luminosities. For each galaxy and halo prescription, we define the energy-equivalence time $τ_{\mathrm{E}}$, when a crossing exists, as the first elapsed time at which the cumulative effective energy from core-collapse supernovae equals the adopted gas binding energy. This quantity is an energetic diagnostic rather than a direct measurement of wind launch. Under the fiducial parameters, all nine galaxies reach the nominal threshold under the kinematic--size prescription K+B16, yet star formation often continues long after threshold crossing. We compare the corresponding lookback time $t_{\mathrm{E,lb}}$ with $t_{90,\mathrm{lb}}$, the lookback time at which 90\% of the total initial stellar mass had formed. Because both times are derived from the same cumulative SFH, their temporal proximity is interpreted descriptively rather than as independent evidence of feedback-driven quenching. Whether the threshold is reached depends on the adopted halo mass, radius, retained gas fraction, and supernova--gas coupling efficiency. These results distinguish the availability of sufficient cumulative feedback energy from the processes that determine the timing of quenching.

</details>


### [147] [A machine-learning-driven dataset of 144,111 polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon infrared spectra for interpreting aromatic infrared bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21205)
*Xinghong Mai,Zu-Jia Lu,Zhao Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习双谐波（ML-DH）框架的高通量红外光谱数据库，用于覆盖星际介质中多环芳烃（PAHs）更广泛的化学空间，显著提升对芳香红外谱带（AIBs）的解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAH参考光谱库覆盖的化学空间不完整，难以匹配星际介质中预期存在的庞大PAH结构多样性，限制了AIBs的准确解读。

Method: 采用结合神经网络力场与电子偶极模型的机器学习双谐波（ML-DH）框架，实现接近量子化学精度但计算成本大幅降低，并经DFT计算和基准光谱库验证。

Result: 构建了包含144,111条红外光谱的数据集，涵盖48,037种闭壳层、偶碳苯型PAH在中性、阳离子和阴离子态下的结构，并附每条谱的不确定性估计；所有数据、优化几何构型及代码均已开源。

Conclusion: 该数据库可有效补充现有光谱库，助力JWST及其他未来红外天文任务对AIBs的建模与解释。

Abstract: The interpretation of interstellar aromatic infrared bands (AIBs) relies on reference spectral libraries, but the chemical space covered by existing databases remains incomplete relative to the vast structural diversity of polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) expected in the interstellar medium. We present a large-scale, high-throughput PAH infrared (IR) spectral database that complements existing reference libraries by providing significantly more continuous coverage of the PAH chemical space for small to medium-sized PAHs. We used a machine-learning double-harmonic (ML-DH) framework that integrates neural network force fields with electronic dipole models. This framework achieves quantum-chemical accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. It has been validated against independent density functional theory calculations and benchmark spectral databases. The resulting dataset comprises 144,111 IR spectra for 48,037 closed-shell, even-carbon benzenoid PAH structures across neutral, cationic, and anionic charge states, complete with per-spectrum uncertainty estimates. The spectral library, optimized geometries, and model code are publicly available. They can be used to complement existing libraries for interpreting and modeling JWST AIB observations and those from future IR missions.

</details>


### [148] [Thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich Effect in the circumgalactic medium - III: does richness matter?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21628)
*Sanskriti Das,Yi-Kuan Chiang,Smita Mathur*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ACT+Planck观测数据，首次测量了700万个质量在10^12–10^14 M⊙、红移z≤1的DESI Legacy Survey星系/星系群周围介质（CGM/IGrM）的热苏尼亚耶夫-泽尔多维奇效应（tSZ），并探究热压强如何依赖于星系团丰富度；发现压力剖面形态随质量和丰富度变化，反映不同物理机制主导（热反馈、冷却等）；CGM中热能-质量关系显著偏离自相似性，表明低质量和丰富度系统是z≤1宇宙热能的主要贡献者，呼吁在标度关系校准中引入除质量外的其他星系本征与环境参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系和星系群周围热气体（CGM/IGrM）的热压强分布及其物理起源，特别是其如何随星系团丰富度和质量变化，以检验反馈机制（热模/动能模）与重力平衡的传统图景。

Method: 基于ACT与Planck联合观测数据，对7百万个DESI Legacy Survey星系/星系群（M200c=10^12–10^14 M⊙, z≤1）进行tSZ信号堆叠分析；拟合GNFW型热压强剖面，并系统比较不同质量-丰富度子样本的β指数与偏心参数r_o；推导热能-质量关系并检验自相似性。

Result: 在10^12.2 M⊙以上质量下以5.8σ显著探测到一晕项tSZ信号；部分质量-丰富度区间需更平坦或偏心的压强剖面（β=1.3–4.0, r_o>0.1R200c）；CGM热能-质量关系比自相似预期低7.3σ，而IGrM符合自相似；存在超维里温度底（≈2–7 T200c），暗示显著加热，但无需普遍动能反馈。

Conclusion: CGM/IGrM热压强剖面多样性揭示多种物理机制共存；低质量、低丰富度系统主导z≤1宇宙热能预算；仅用质量标定热能关系已不充分，须纳入星系本征与环境属性。

Abstract: We measure the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich Effect in the circumgalactic/intragroup medium (CGM/IGrM) of 7 million $\rm M_{200c} = 10^{12-14}$M$_\odot$ $z \leqslant 1$ DESI Legacy Survey galaxies/groups using ACT+Planck observations, and investigate for the first time how the thermal pressure depends on richness. We detect the one-halo term down to 10$^{12.2}$M$_\odot$ at $5.8σ$. While several mass-richness bins are well described by the standard GNFW thermal-pressure profile ($β=5.49,r_o=0$), others favor a flatter and/or miscentered profile ($β=1.3-4.0,r_o>0.1 \rm R_{200c}$), with the mass dependence of this pattern varying with richness. The wide variety of thermal pressure profiles across mass and richness indicates a range of physical regimes, from dominant thermal feedback to efficient cooling with subdominant thermal feedback, and even substantial cooling. In some mass bins, the super-virial ($\approx2-7 \rm T_{200c}$) temperature floor (the lowest temperature allowed by the data for baryon-sufficient halos) points to significant heating. However, dominant kinetic-mode feedback is not essential anywhere, challenging a universal (ejective)feedback-vs-gravity picture. The slope of the thermal energy-mass relation in IGrM is consistent with self-similarity, but in the CGM it falls below self-similarity by $7.3σ$. This makes halos of relatively low mass and richness the dominant contributors to the universe's thermal energy budget at $z\leqslant1$. We therefore argue that calibrations of thermal energy scaling relations must move beyond the convention of using mass as the sole demarcator and instead incorporate additional intrinsic and environmental properties of galaxies.

</details>


### [149] [Disk tilts in Milky Way-like galaxies and their link to mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21226)
*Runsheng Cai,Ling Zhu,Jiang Chang,Lan Zhang,Xiangxiang Xue,Xi Kang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用TNG50模拟研究了93个银河系质量星系的盘面再取向事件及其与并合事件的关系，发现约40%的平滑演化型盘面倾斜事件可导致倾角超过30度，主要由质量相近或更小、沿逆行或近垂直轨道并合的卫星触发，且盘面倾向于与卫星轨道角动量对齐，同时仍能维持薄盘结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解银河系质量星系中恒星盘面再取向的发生机制及其与并合事件的关联，特别是盘面如何在经历显著倾角变化时仍保持稳定和薄盘结构。

Method: 基于TNG50宇宙学流体动力学模拟，选取93个银河系质量星系样本，识别117次具有明确定义起止时间的盘面倾角事件，并按演化方式分为平滑型与剧烈扰动再生型；对每次倾角事件匹配时间上最显著的并合事件，重点分析平滑演化型事件的倾角速率、持续时间、并合参数及盘-卫星角动量关系。

Result: 平滑型盘面倾角事件通常速率低于10 deg/Gyr、持续2–9 Gyr，多与小质量卫星（质量≤星系恒星质量）的切向、逆行或近垂直轨道并合相关；约40%事件倾角超30度；盘面趋向于与卫星轨道角动量对齐，逆行并合可导致近乎正交的中间构型；因倾角变化缓慢，盘面可保持稳定并持续增长，最终仍形成薄盘。

Conclusion: 盘面再取向不必然破坏盘的稳定性；在特定并合条件下（如小质量、逆行、切向轨道），可实现大角度平滑倾斜，且恒星盘仍能维持薄而稳定的结构，这对理解星系形态演化与并合历史具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigated disk reorientation and its relationship to mergers in a sample of 93 Milky Way-mass galaxies drawn from the TNG50 simulations. From this sample, we identified 117 individual disk-tilt events with clearly defined start and end times, and we divided them into two classes based on whether the disk evolves smoothly or undergoes rapid disruption and regrowth during the tilt duration. For each tilt event, we linked the most massive merger that occurs in close temporal association with it. We focus on those tilt events occurring during phases of smooth disk evolution, which typically show tilt rates below 10 degrees per Gyr, persist for 2-9 Gyr, and are generally connected to minor mergers. Roughly 40% of these events reach tilt angles exceeding 30 degrees, most often triggered by satellites whose masses are comparable to or less massive than the galaxy's total stellar mass, merging on tangential orbits with orbital angular momentum that is retrograde or nearly perpendicular to the disk spin. The satellite consistently drives the disk tilt toward alignment with the satellite's orbital spin; satellites that came in on retrograde orbits relative to the disk spin can thus induce large tilt angles and can produce an almost orthogonal configuration between the stellar disk and the satellite's orbital plane as an intermediate phase. Owing to the comparatively slow tilt rates, the stellar disk can keep stable and continue to grow throughout the reorientation process. Consequently, galaxies that experience strong disk tilts can still maintain stellar disks that are as thin as those in systems without significant reorientation.

</details>


### [150] [Are High-EW LAEs good analogs for the earliest galaxies? A study on the ionizing conditions and gas properties of LAEs at $2\lesssim z\lesssim7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21232)
*Lena Pless,Themiya Nanayakkara,Karl Glazebrook,Michael Maseda,Jarle Brinchmann,Tanya Urrutia*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec光谱和NIRCam成像数据，分析了88个红移2–7的莱曼α发射体（LAEs），发现高等效宽度（EW）的LAEs虽倾向于更低金属丰度，但EW与金属丰度间无强单调关系；它们整体偏离质量-金属丰度关系，表明化学演化程度更低，可作为再电离主导星系的有用（但非完美）类似体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lyman-α发射体（LAEs）被认为是主导宇宙再电离的低质量、低金属丰度星系的类似体，尤其高Lyα等效宽度（EW）的LAEs被认为尚未经历显著化学增丰；本文旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 基于MUSE观测选出88个z≈2–7、HST深场不可见的LAEs，并开展JWST/NIRSpec光谱和NIRCam成像后续观测；利用BPASS+Cloudy光电离模型拟合光学发射线推断气体相金属丰度，通过NIRCam测光SED拟合获得恒星质量。

Result: 仅50个LAEs在NIRCam中被可靠探测（AB≈30.3 mag）；16个具有可靠恒星质量和金属丰度的样本落在低金属丰度星系诊断图区域；R3强线金属丰度指标随EW增大而减小，但全谱拟合金属丰度与EW无强单调关系；高EW LAEs普遍低于质量-金属丰度关系，表明其化学演化更原始。

Conclusion: 高EW LAEs是研究再电离主导星系的有用但不完美的类似体；结合EW与恒星质量可更有效识别化学最原始的星系。

Abstract: Lyman-$α$ emitters (LAEs) are frequently proposed as analogs of the low-mass, metal-poor galaxies thought to dominate cosmic reionization. In particular, galaxies with large Ly$α$ equivalent widths (EWs) are expected to have not experienced significant chemical enrichment. We test this hypothesis using a sample of 88 LAEs at $2 \lesssim z\lesssim 7$, selected from MUSE observations that are undetected in deep HST imaging and subsequently followed up with JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy. Despite the unprecedented sensitivity of JWST, only 50 of the LAEs exhibit secure counterparts in available deep NIRCam imaging reaching typical 3$σ$ depths of $m_\text{AB}\sim30.3$ mag, highlighting the intrinsically faint nature of the population. Gas-phase metallicities are inferred by fitting optical emission lines with a grid of BPASS+cloudy photoionization models, while stellar masses are derived from NIRCam photometry through SED fitting. The final sample with secure stellar masses and gas-phase metallicities encompasses only 16 LAEs, occupying regions of standard emission-line diagnostic diagrams consistent with low-metallicity star-forming systems. Although the strong-line metallicity diagnostic R3 appears to decrease with increasing EW, we find no compelling evidence for a strong monotonic relation between EW and gas-phase metallicity when metallicities are inferred through full emission-line fitting. However, galaxies with stronger Ly$α$ emission tend to lie below the average mass-metallicity relation at fixed stellar masses, suggesting that they are preferentially less chemically evolved than typical galaxies of the same mass. We conclude that high-EW LAEs provide useful, though imperfect, analogs of the galaxies thought to drive cosmic reionization, and combining EW with stellar mass may provide a more effective route for identifying the most chemically primitive galaxies.

</details>


### [151] [Mixed modified-gravity--modified-inertia MOND theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21699)
*Mordehai Milgrom*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了修正牛顿动力学（MOND）的非相对论性理论族，这些理论同时修改牛顿引力与惯性作用量，并引入参数η（0≤η≤1）表征深MOND极限下各项的作用量标度维数；η=0对应纯引力修正，η=1对应纯惯性修正；仅当两者协同作用时才再现MOND核心现象（如旋转曲线渐近平坦、质量-速度关系等），且运动方程与引力场方程各自出现不同的插值函数，揭示了能产生MOND现象的理论多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索更基本的MOND理论中引力与惯性可能同时偏离牛顿形式的可能性，厘清仅修改引力或仅修改惯性之外的理论结构及其独特预言。

Method: 构建一类非相对论性MOND理论族，通过引入标度维数参数η统一描述引力与动能作用量的联合修正，在一般性框架下分析其运动方程与场方程的结构及导出的天文现象。

Result: 发现单一修改（仅引力或仅惯性）无法产生MOND现象，必须二者协同；成功复现旋转曲线渐近平坦、质量-渐近速度关系、深MOND下的M-σ关系等核心预测；揭示运动方程与引力场方程各自具有不同插值函数，且均不具标准深MOND极限。

Conclusion: 存在多种可实现MOND现象的理论路径，单纯区分‘修改引力’或‘修改惯性’过于简化；联合修改框架凸显了MOND现象学背后理论结构的丰富性与复杂性。

Abstract: It is plausible that in a more fundamental theory of MOND both gravity and inertia will differ from their Newtonian versions. Such theories, if they embody the basic tenets of MOND, will share its primary predictions with existing theories, which modify either gravity or inertia. But they are expected to differ in their structure and in their secondary predictions. Here, I discuss families of nonrelativistic MOND theories that modify both the kinetic and the gravitational actions of Newtonian dynamics. Each family is spanned by one parameter $0\leη\le 1$, which is the scaling dimension of the deep-MOND limit of all terms in the action. Pure modified gravity ($η=0$) and pure modified inertia ($η=1$) are the end cases in each family. I speak of families, since the modified action terms themselves are not strictly specified, but are treated in rather general terms. These theories are artificial in that they are not based on a single unifying idea that dictates the modification of the two components. But they are still useful in pinpointing some idiosyncracies of such theories. For example, neither of the two modified terms, by itself, leads to MOND phenomenology; only their combined effect does. I show how the two combine to make some of the primary MOND predictions, such as asymptotic flatness of rotation curves, the mass-asymptotic-speed relation, and the deep-MOND, virial, mass-velocity-dispersion ($M-σ$) relation. Another idiosyncracy of such theories is the emergence of different interpolating functions in the kinetic equations of motion and in the gravitational field equations, each relevant under different circumstances, and neither of which have the standard deep-MOND limit. Such examples alert us to the wide range of theories that can lead to MOND phenomenology.

</details>


### [152] [A ${}^{13}$CO Detection in a Massive Molecular Gas Flow in Abell 1795's Central Galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21532)
*J. A. Gray,H. R. Russell,F. Combes,O. Almaini,P. E. J. Nulsen,A. C. Fabian,B. R. McNamara,T. Rose,P. Salomé*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA对Abell 1795星系团中心星系的分子气体进行^{13}CO(2-1)观测，发现其延伸分子细丝的质量比先前估计低一个数量级，支持AGN气泡直接拖曳或热气体就地冷凝形成分子气体的两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冷却核心星系团Abell 1795中延伸分子细丝的起源，特别是其与AGN喷流所吹起的射电气泡之间的物理关联。

Method: 使用ALMA望远镜获取^{13}CO(2-1)谱线观测数据，结合^{12}CO(2-1)对比分析速度结构，并基于不同激发温度和覆盖分数模型估算分子气体质量及CO-H2转换因子α_CO。

Result: 首次在^{13}CO(2-1)中探测到北侧分子细丝，测得其质量为(0.1–2)×10⁹ M⊙，显著低于以往基于^{12}CO的估计；推导出α_CO = 0.4–5 M⊙/(K km s⁻¹ pc²)，约为银河系标准值的0.1–1倍；确认气泡机械能与X射线冷却率均足以支撑细丝形成。

Conclusion: 分子细丝可能由AGN射电气泡直接拖曳形成，也可能源于被抬升的低熵热气体就地冷凝；两种机制均需多代气泡活动才能解释更高质量的细丝。

Abstract: We present new ALMA ${}^{13}$CO(2-1) observations of the central galaxy in the cool-core cluster Abell 1795, targeting the origin of extended molecular filaments. The filaments extend for 5--7 kpc and are closely drawn up around large radio bubbles inflated by jets from the central active galactic nucleus (AGN). Smooth velocity gradients along their lengths are consistent with entrained flows lifted by the expanding bubbles. Here we detect the northern molecular filament in the low-opacity molecular tracer ${}^{13}$CO(2-1), which can provide a more reliable measure of the molecular gas mass and conditions than ${}^{12}$CO. The velocity structure in ${}^{13}$CO(2-1) is consistent with that of the ${}^{12}$CO(2-1) emission. Using a range of plausible excitation temperatures and covering fractions, we thereby determine a filament molecular gas mass of (0.1--2) $\times \ 10^9 \ \text{M}_\odot$, or down to an order of magnitude below previous estimates. This implies a CO-to-H$_2$ conversion factor of $α_\text{CO} =$ 0.4--5 $\ \text{M}_\odot $ (K km s$^{-1}$ pc$^2)^{-1}$ in the filament, or $\sim$ 0.1--1 times the standard assumption of the Milky Way value. Direct entrainment by the N radio bubble is now plausible, as it can provide sufficient mechanical energy and buoyant support for the molecular gas flow. Similarly, measured X-ray cooling rates are also now consistent with in-situ condensation of uplifted, low-entropy hot gas. Both formation mechanisms require multiple generations of bubbles at higher filament masses.

</details>


### [153] [A clump population framework for star formation across galactic scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21623)
*J. W. Zhou,Sami Dib,Tie Liu,Ralf S. Klessen,Davide Elia,Kee-Tae Kim,Mika Juvela,Pavel Kroupa,Patricio Sanhueza,Médéric Boquien,Keping Qiu,Michele Ginolfi,Dario Colombo,Timothy Davis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同星系环境中分子云尺度上的恒星形成规律，发现分子气体质量（M_H2）与恒星形成率（SFR）之间存在跨越十个数量级的一致关系，且对环境依赖很弱；该关系可由嵌入分子云中的致密团块恒星形成模型解释，从而统一了银河系与河外星系的恒星形成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究恒星形成是否在不同空间尺度和星系环境中遵循普适规律。

Method: 利用树图算法识别30个矮星系和盘星系中的分子云，结合CO积分强度和中红外辐射分别估算分子气体质量和恒星形成率；进一步拓展至银河系亚秒差距尺度及311个近邻星系，并构建致密团块嵌入分子云的理论模型。

Result: 发现M_H2–SFR关系在所有星系中一致，跨越十个数量级，且对星系环境不敏感；理论模型成功复现该关系。

Conclusion: 恒星形成在团块尺度上对环境依赖很弱，这解释了星系尺度上M_H2–SFR关系的普适性，建立了银河系与河外星系恒星形成的观测统一框架。

Abstract: Whether star formation across different spatial scales and galactic environments follows universal laws remains poorly understood. We investigate cloud-scale star formation in 30 dwarf and disk galaxies. Molecular clouds are identified using a dendrogram algorithm, and their molecular gas masses ($M_{\rm H_2}$) and star formation rates (SFRs) are derived from integrated CO and mid-infrared emission, respectively. We find a consistent $M_{\rm H_2}$--SFR relation across all galaxies, with little sensitivity to galactic environment. Extending the analysis to Milky Way cloud substructures down to sub-parsec scales and 311 diverse nearby galaxies, we find that this relation persists across ten orders of magnitude in molecular gas mass and SFR. A theoretical model in which star formation occurs within dense clumps embedded in molecular clouds reproduces this observed relation. The weak environmental dependence of star formation at the clump scale provides a possible explanation for the nearly universal $M_{\rm H_2}$--SFR relation across galaxies. This framework establishes an observationally grounded connection between star formation in the Milky Way and external galaxies.

</details>


### [154] [The Radius-Luminosity Relationship for Active Galactic Nuclei with Hierarchical Bayesian Reverberation Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22096)
*Hugh McDougall,Tamara M. Davis,Benjamin J. S. Pope,Paul Martini,Zhefu Yu,Chris Lidman,Geraint F. Lewis,Christian Adamcewicz,Rahma Alfarsy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的端到端贝叶斯框架，用于大规模活动星系核（AGN）的混响映射分析，可同时拟合光变延迟和群体模型（如半径-光度关系），显著提升延迟与黑洞质量测量的精度和数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混响映射受低质量光变曲线、观测季节性混叠及统计方法不一致等问题制约，限制了大规模巡天的数据利用效率。

Method: 构建一个数值稳定、完全边缘化数据不确定性与质量的端到端贝叶斯框架，支持大规模AGN时间序列的延迟拟合与群体模型联合推断。

Result: 在OzDES样本上，该方法将高置信延迟测量数从24个提升至约297个，对R-L关系等群体性质的约束能力提升一倍以上，延迟与质量回收数量提高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架是现有堆叠与质量筛选方法的稳健推广，为未来大规模AGN时序分析提供了可扩展、抗假阳性的统计基础。

Abstract: Reverberation mapping of active galactic nuclei uses the time taken for variability to travel through the system as a measure of system size, with this size being used to inform estimates of the mass of the central black hole, a technique that has graduated to an industrial-scale effort in recent decades. Such large target-count, multi-year surveys have been consistently undercut by poor light curve quality and the aliasing problems of seasonal observations. These impacts compound with statistical approaches that are not consistent between works and sometimes combine inconsistent statistical frameworks. Here, we present a new end-to-end Bayesian framework for fitting lags at scale in large surveys, simultaneous with the fitting of population models such as the radius-luminosity relation, and formulated in a numerically tractable way. This procedure is a statistically and numerically robust generalisation of the stacking and quality cut procedures of past works, fully marginalising over data uncertainty and data quality. Our approach remains numerically stable when scaling to larger surveys, and can act as a basis for any Bayesian population modelling of large sets of AGN time series observations. Applied to the OzDES sample, we demonstrate that this new approach yields more than double the constraining power on population-level properties like the R-L relationship, and an order-of-magnitude increase in the number of recovered lags and masses. With this method, we go from 24 high quality lag recoveries with traditional methods to a new effective sample size of ~297, all while being robust against random chance false positive measurements.

</details>


### [155] [From Scaling Relations to Physical Laws: Constraining the Physics of Star Formation Across Local Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21891)
*Jakob den Brok,Cecilia Bacchini,Leindert A. Boogaard,Mathilde Bouvier,Viviana Casasola,Laura Colzi,Timothy A. Davis,Cosima Eibensteiner,Daniel Espada,Simon Glover,Logan H. Jones,Ryan Keenan,Ralf S. Klessen,Ivanna Langan,Dieu D. Nguyen,Miguel Pereira-Santaella,Miguel Querejeta,Ilaria Ruffa,Francesco Salvestrini,Jiayi Sun,Sabine Thater,Robin Tress,Antonio Usero,Thomas G. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出ALMA2040——一个面向2040年代的新一代（亚）毫米波干涉仪，旨在通过高灵敏度、宽带多谱线/同位素观测，在巨分子云（GMC）尺度上解析邻近星系中的分子气体物理状态（温度、密度、激发态、化学成分），从而厘清引力、湍流和恒星反馈在恒星形成调控中的各自作用，推动从经验性气体-恒星形成关系迈向可预测的物理理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对冷密气体如何转化为恒星这一核心问题仍缺乏物理理解；尽管已建立经验性气体-恒星形成关系，但其背后主导机制（引力、湍流、反馈）难以区分，因现有或近期设施无法兼顾统计样本量、云尺度分辨率与多谱线诊断能力。

Method: 设计并论证新一代旗舰级干涉仪ALMA2040，具备更高谱线灵敏度与带宽，支持邻近星系中分子气体的多跃迁、多同位素观测，并在巨分子云（GMC）尺度上实现物理参数（温度、密度、激发、化学）的独立测量。

Result: ALMA2040将实现对数百万个独立气体区域的物理状态测量，覆盖多样化的星系环境，使分子气体从经验示踪剂转变为定量探针，支撑构建可预测的恒星形成物理理论。

Conclusion: ALMA2040是解决星系演化中‘冷密气体→恒星’转化这一根本问题的关键设施，将在2040年代联合光学与射电望远镜，催生关于气体恒星形成转换率的预测性物理理论。

Abstract: The conversion of cold dense gas, traced by molecular line emission, into stars remains one of the fundamental unsolved problems in galaxy evolution. Observations over the past decades have established empirical relations between gas and star formation, yet their physical origin remains uncertain. Gravity, turbulence, and stellar feedback as main drivers provide fundamentally different pictures of how star formation is regulated, while reproducing remarkably similar galaxy-scale scaling relations. To disentangle the individual importance of each process, not only its mass and distribution, but also its temperature, density, excitation, and chemistry must be probed. However, current or planned (sub)millimetre facilities cannot combine statistical breadth, cloud-scale resolution, and multi-line diagnostics across large galaxy samples. A new, flagship interferometer with increased line sensitivity and bandwidth enables obtaining multi-transition, multi-isotopologue observations of molecular gas at GMC-resolved scales across nearby galaxies, enabling millions of independent measurements of its physical state across a wide range of extragalactic environments. This will transform molecular gas from an empirical tracer into a quantitative probe of the physical processes governing the relation between the dense gas and star formation. With the 2040s landscape of optical and radio facilities, ALMA2040 will facilitate a predictive theory of the conversion of gas into stars.

</details>


### [156] [Disentangling Multi-epoch Single-lined Binary Star Spectra to Reveal Interstellar Medium Features via MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21997)
*Shao-Song Huang,Jian-Jun Chen,Xiao-Xiao Ma,Ji-Wei Shi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖模板的MCMC-RVSD算法，利用双星轨道运动引起的恒星谱线多普勒位移与静止的星际吸收线（DIBs）之间的差异，实现两者的分离，并在模拟和LAMOST实测数据上验证了其高精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模板匹配的方法受限于模板质量，难以准确剥离冷星谱中严重混杂的恒星谱线，从而影响DIBs参数的精确测量。

Method: 提出RV-based贝叶斯MCMC框架MCMC-RVSD算法，利用双星光谱中恒星线随视向速度（RV）移动而DIBs保持静止的特性，同时拟合恒星与星际成分；通过模拟光谱和LAMOST中分辨率实测双星数据进行验证，并引入MCMC自相关时间τ_DIB评估拟合质量。

Result: 算法能精确恢复DIB λ6614参数，在R=3000–7500光谱上表现稳健；RV跨度>20 km s⁻¹是成功分离的关键条件；对SNR>50、RV误差<5 km s⁻¹的观测噪声具有鲁棒性；理想条件下参数不确定性显著小于传统模板法；可扩展至多恒星线污染、非高斯DIB及其它DIB。

Conclusion: MCMC-RVSD是一种可靠、通用的时间域光谱巡天中精确测量星际特征的新工具，突破了模板依赖限制，提升了DIBs研究的精度与适用范围。

Abstract: Diffuse Interstellar Bands (DIBs) are interstellar absorption features with unidentified carriers in the optical and near-infrared spectra. When observed in cool star spectra, they are heavily contaminated by stellar lines. Traditional methods rely on template matching to remove the stellar component, but their accuracy is fundamentally limited by template quality. We present a template-independent algorithm that separates stellar and interstellar features in binary star spectra by exploiting orbital motion: stellar lines shift with changing radial velocity (RV), whereas DIBs remain stationary in the rest frame. The MCMC-RVSD algorithm, implemented as an RV-based Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo framework, fits both components simultaneously. We validated our approach by applying it to mock spectra simulating LAMOST medium-resolution observations. The method precisely recovers the parameters of DIB $λ$6614. It performs reliably for spectra with $R=3000$-$7500$. We applied the method to 434 real binary sources from the LAMOST medium-resolution survey and introduced the MCMC autocorrelation time ($τ_{\mathrm{DIB}}$) to assess the fit quality. Our analysis shows that an RV span $>20$ km s$^{-1}$ is the primary driver of successful line separation. Denser orbital sampling provides further improvement, and the algorithm remains robust against typical observational noise at SNR $>50$ and RV errors $<5$ km s$^{-1}$. Under ideal conditions, our approach yields significantly smaller parameter uncertainties than traditional template matching. Additionally, the MCMC-RVSD algorithm can also handle multi-stellar line contamination, non-Gaussian DIBs and other DIBs by adjusting model and prior settings. This algorithm provides a reliable, general-purpose tool for precise measurement of interstellar features in time-domain spectroscopic surveys.

</details>


### [157] [A Comprehensive AGN Inventory for the Dwarf-Dominated RESOLVE and ECO Surveys: Mass-Dependent AGN Types and Occupation Fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22115)
*Mugdha S. Polimera,Sheila J. Kannappan,Chris T. Richardson,David V. Stark,Kathleen D. Eckert,Thomas H. Jarrett,Derrick S. Carr,Zackary L. Hutchens,Jillian M. Bellovary,Mark A. Norris*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过多种方法（BPT图、VO图、WISE中红外颜色选择）在RESOLVE和ECO巡天中系统识别活动星系核（AGN），发现AGN占据率随星系重子质量显著上升，并揭示了AGN类型随星系性质（如恒星形成率、气体含量、环境）连续变化的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AGN在不同质量星系中的分布规律及其与星系演化物理性质（如气体含量、恒星形成、环境）的关系，解决现有AGN识别方法（尤其是WISE）的系统误差问题。

Method: 结合BPT图（标准及替代分界线）、VO图（[O I]或[S II]）、WISE中红外颜色选择；按重子质量/恒星质量划分矮星系、过渡星系和巨星系；整合X射线/宽线AGN档案数据；发布统一处理的SDSS谱线测量、重处理WISE数据及UV+中红外恒星形成率。

Result: AGN占据率从矮星系的2–5%陡增至巨星系的20–48%，跨越重子质量~10^9.7 M⊙约5倍；WISE标准星表存在高假阳/假阴率；AGN类型随星系性质呈连续分布：SF-AGN（高SF端）→ Bonus AGN → Composites → Conventional AGN（低SF端）。

Conclusion: AGN并非孤立现象，其出现频率与本质类型由宿主星系整体物理状态决定，构成一个与星系演化紧密耦合的连续体；需多指标联合、谨慎使用WISE等单指标方法，并强调数据一致性与系统误差量化。

Abstract: We catalog Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) in the volume- and mass-limited RESOLVE and ECO surveys using (i) the BPT plot with standard and alternate demarcations, (ii) the BPT plot and VO plots (using [O~I] or [S~II]) to find ``SF-AGN'' with mixed star-forming and AGN classifications, and (iii) mid-IR WISE color selection. Dividing RESOLVE+ECO into dwarf, transitional, and giant galaxies by baryonic mass, we find AGN occupation fractions of 2--5\%, 16--27\%, and 20--48\%, respectively, indicating a steep $\sim$5$\times$ increase across baryonic mass $\sim$10$^{9.7}$ M$_\odot$. These percentages are computed relative to all galaxies in each mass division, including those not searchable for AGN, with numerical ranges representing large systematics between crossmatched emission-line measurements from different SDSS catalogs. Stellar-mass divisions yield similar results. Adding (incomplete) archival X-ray/broadline AGN detections, the dwarf AGN occupation fraction reaches 3--6\%. WISE AGN are detected in $<$1\% of galaxies at all masses with optimized WISE photometry. We show that standard WISE catalog photometry yields high rates of both false positives and false negatives. AGN demographics shift with mass due to correlations with gas content, star formation, and group environment, forming a continuum: SF-AGN at the high-SF end, then ``Bonus AGN'' (defined by alternate literature BPT demarcations), then Composites, then Conventional AGN at the low-SF end. Typical (metal-poor, star-forming) dwarfs most often host SF-AGN, whereas typical transitional galaxies most often host Bonus AGN and Composites. We release homogenized SDSS line measurements, reprocessed WISE data, and UV+mid-IR star formation rates for RESOLVE and ECO.

</details>


### [158] [The Mission Market: Flying NASA's Vetted, Unflown Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22159)
*Blakesley Burkhart*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: Mission Market 是一种前瞻性的市场承诺机制，旨在通过吸引AI公司、慈善机构和科学家资助，为低成本但高科学价值的NASA任务提供资金，从而解决因预算限制而无法实施的优秀科学任务问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年NASA验证的优秀科学任务数量超过其预算所能支持的数量，许多具有高科学价值的任务因资金不足而长期搁置。

Method: 提出 Mission Market 作为一种先进的市场承诺机制，主要通过AI公司投资前沿科学数据以支持基础模型开发，辅以慈善捐赠和科学家个人资助来筹集资金。

Result: 该机制有望使部分低成本、高科学价值的NASA任务得以实施，缓解联邦拨款竞争压力，并促进跨领域合作。

Conclusion: Mission Market 提供了一种创新的资金解决方案，可有效支持NASA在天体物理、日地物理和行星科学等领域中被低估但极具潜力的科学任务。

Abstract: Every year NASA validates more excellent science missions than it can afford to fly. Some of the most promising missions in astrophysics, heliophysics, and planetary science are judged selectable, yet remain grounded indefinitely. Mission Market is an advance market commitment that can fly lower cost missions without competing for federal appropriations. Funding comes primarily from the enormous capital now flowing into artificial intelligence (AI) companies that value generating frontier data for scientific foundation models and is also supplemented by traditional means such as philanthropy and patron-scientists.

</details>


### [159] [Stellar Mass Census of $S^4G$ Local Bars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22188)
*Yasmin Cavalcante-Coelho,Karín Menéndez-Delmestre,Taehyun Kim,Arianna Cortesi,Natan de Isídio,Thiago S. Gonçalves,Kartik Sheth,Osbaldo Sánchez-García,Marco Grossi,Thiago Bueno-Dalpiaz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用S^4G巡天数据，对344个棒旋星系在3.6和4.5微米波段进行多波段二维结构分解，量化了棒的恒星质量，并揭示了棒性质与星系整体质量及内部结构（尤其是核球）之间的系统性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化棒的恒星质量有助于理解棒的增长如何依赖于宿主星系的质量和内部结构，从而深入认识棒驱动的星系缓变演化过程。

Method: 采用GALFITM软件对S^4G样本中344个棒旋星系在Spitzer 3.6和4.5微米波段图像进行多波段二维光度分解，分离盘、核球、棒和核点源等成分，并估算各成分的恒星质量。

Result: 发现大质量且具有显著核球的星系拥有更高质量的棒和更高的棒/总恒星质量比；核球通常呈低Sérsic指数（n<2），暗示其为核星盘；在固定星系质量下，有核球星系的相对棒长显著长于无核球星系，呈现双峰分布。

Conclusion: 棒的演化可能在不同质量星系中遵循不同路径，受星系内部缓变过程（如盘形成时序）及环境效应共同影响。

Abstract: Bars are stellar structures commonly found in disk galaxies and important agents of secular evolution. Quantifying the stellar mass content of bars provides a physically motivated approach to assess how bar growth depends on galaxy mass and internal structure. We perform a GALFITM multi-band mid-infrared two-dimensional decomposition of 344 barred galaxies from the Spitzer Survey of Stellar Structure in Galaxies ($S^4G$) in the 3.6 and 4.5 $μ$m bands. We estimate the stellar mass associated with each structural component (i.e., disk, bulge, bar, and nuclear point source) and investigate how bar properties correlate with global galaxy characteristics. Our final models consist of 2-4 components per galaxy, with the simplest configuration (disk + bar) typically associated with low-mass systems. We find that more massive galaxies with prominent bulges host more massive bars and higher median bar-to-total stellar mass ratios. These bulges generally present Sérsic indices below 2, consistent with a photometric signature of nuclear stellar disks. Additionally, when we consider the relative bar lengths at a fixed galaxy mass, we find a bimodal distribution where galaxies with bulges host relatively longer bars compared to their bulgeless counterparts. These findings could be the result of different evolutionary paths, possibly linked to different disk formation epochs, allowing bars more time to grow in size and mass. Our study indicates that bar evolution may follow distinct pathways across different galaxy mass regimes, and is likely influenced by secular processes and environmental effects.

</details>


### [160] [Dark Matter in the Galaxy: Insights from the Dynamics of Stellar Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22202)
*Mohammad Ayaz,Priya Hasan*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用银河系恒星流的形态与运动学特征来约束暗物质晕结构、形状及子结构的研究进展，涵盖观测特征、建模方法、现存挑战及未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质是宇宙中主要物质成分，但其基本性质和分布仍不清楚；恒星流作为银河系引力势和暗物质晕的有力示踪器，可提供关键约束。

Method: 综述分析法，系统梳理恒星流（如Sagittarius、GD-1等）的观测特征（间隙、支流、扰动）及解析、统计与贝叶斯建模方法，并讨论参数简并性、观测不完备性、前身天体不确定性与时变摄动（如大麦哲伦云）等挑战。

Result: 恒星流为约束银河系暗物质晕结构、形状与子结构提供了有效途径；当前方法已取得一定成果，但仍受限于多种系统性误差；未来巡天数据与动力学建模提升有望区分冷、温、自相互作用暗物质模型。

Conclusion: 恒星流是探测银河系暗物质分布的重要探针；需结合多方法建模与高质量观测以突破现有瓶颈，并推动暗物质本质研究。

Abstract: Dark matter constitutes most of the matter in the Universe, yet its fundamental nature and distribution remain poorly understood. Stellar streams, formed through the tidal disruption of globular clusters and dwarf galaxies, provide powerful tracers of the Milky Way's gravitational potential and dark matter halo. In this review, we examine how stellar stream morphology and kinematics can constrain the halo's structure, shape, and substructure. We discuss key observational signatures, including stream gaps, spurs, and perturbations, and investigate five prominent streams: Sagittarius, GD-1, Palomar 5, Orphan-Chenab, and Cetus Polar. We summarize analytical, statistical, and Bayesian methods used to model stellar streams and infer dark matter properties, highlighting challenges arising from parameter degeneracies, incomplete observations, progenitor uncertainties, and time-dependent perturbations, particularly those associated with the Large Magellanic Cloud. Finally, we discuss how upcoming astrometric and photometric surveys, together with improved dynamical modelling, may strengthen constraints on the Milky Way's dark matter distribution and help distinguish between cold, warm, and self-interacting dark matter scenarios.

</details>
